- **The Kulapati's Doom Revealed** — the dog discloses that it was itself the former, conscientious kulapati of Kālañjara yet fell to a dog-birth, so making an angry man abbot over sacred wealth is a punishment disguised as a boon _(actors: the dog, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramas-justice-the-dog-and-the-mendicant.md) — (Uttara Kāṇḍa, prakṣipta) [late text]
- **The lost hunt of King Babhruvāhana** — the king of Mahodaya wounds a deer, loses his army in the deep forest, and rests at a lotus lake beneath a fig tree _(actors: Babhruvāhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/babhruvahana-and-the-preta.md) — (Garuḍa Purāṇa, Preta-khaṇḍa 4; Sāroddhāra 7.17–27)
- **The massacre of frogs and Āyu's curse** — the enraged king orders frogs slaughtered realm-wide, until the frog-king Āyu reveals his deceitful daughter and curses her sons to scorn brāhmaṇas before restoring her _(actors: Parikṣit, Āyu, Suśobhanā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/manduka-frog-princess-sushobhana.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva CXCII)
- **The māyā withdrawn and Śiva-bhakti prescribed** — Viṣṇu dissolves the illusion, reveals it as Śiva's māyā curing Nārada's pride, and directs the repentant sage to Śiva-worship, launching the Brahmā–Nārada frame of the Rudra-saṃhitā _(actors: Viṣṇu, Nārada, Brahmā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/narada-moha-svayamvara.md) — (Śiva Purāṇa, Rudra-saṃhitā, Sṛṣṭi-khaṇḍa 4)
- **The monkey-face humiliation at Śrīmatī's svayaṃvara** — in the phantom city of King Śīlanidhi, Nārada begs Viṣṇu for "Hari's form," receives a monkey's face by the hari-pun, and is spurned by princess Śrīmatī, who garlands Viṣṇu himself _(actors: Nārada, Viṣṇu, Śrīmatī, Śīlanidhi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/narada-moha-svayamvara.md) — (Śiva Purāṇa, Rudra-saṃhitā, Sṛṣṭi-khaṇḍa 3)
- **The mutilation at Pañcavaṭī** — Śūrpaṇakhā woos Rāma, is deflected in jest, lunges at Sītā, and has her nose and ears cut off by Lakṣmaṇa — the spark of the whole war _(actors: Śūrpaṇakhā, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shurpanakha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya Kāṇḍa 17–18)
- **The mutual curse into birds** — Vasiṣṭha curses Viśvāmitra into a baka (crane) and Viśvāmitra retorts by cursing Vasiṣṭha into an Āḍi bird, hurling both brahmarṣis out of their human forms _(actors: Vasiṣṭha, Viśvāmitra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/adi-baka-bird-war.md) — (Mārkaṇḍeya Purāṇa IX; Devī-Bhāgavata VI.12)
- **The naming of Pañcāpsaras tīrtha** — the pool is named 'Five-Apsaras lake' after the nymphs won and hidden there, the Araṇya Kāṇḍa's one embedded sacred-lake etiology _(actors: Dharmabhṛta narrating to Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mandakarni-and-panchapsaras-lake.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya Kāṇḍa 11)
- **The Nīla mountain unveiled and five ascend to Vaikuṇṭha** — the blazing mountain and its golden temple where Brahmā worships Viṣṇu appear, and the king with his wife, minister, brāhmaṇa and the weaver receive four-armed bodies and ride a vimāna to Vaikuṇṭha _(actors: Viṣṇu, Ratnagrīva, Brahmā, the five devotees)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/ratnagriva-purushottama-kshetra.md) — (Padma Purāṇa, Pātāla-khaṇḍa 22)
- **The omen of Rohiṇī's cart** — Ayodhyā's astrologers warn King Daśaratha that Śani is about to enter the Rohiṇī-śakaṭa-bheda, a transit portending a twelve-year drought and famine on earth _(actors: Daśaratha, Vasiṣṭha, Śani)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/dasharatha-shani-stotra-legend.md) — (Skanda Purāṇa, Nāgara-khaṇḍa)
- **The preta teaches the king its own liturgy of release** — the deformed ghost prescribes the Nārāyaṇa rite, vṛṣotsarga (bull-release) and the golden ghee-pot gift, then pays the king with a great jewel and vanishes _(actors: the preta Sudeva, Babhruvāhana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/babhruvahana-and-the-preta.md) — (Garuḍa Purāṇa, Preta-khaṇḍa 4–5; Sāroddhāra 7.46–65)
- **The queen's boon ends the feud** — Dala's queen worships the ṛṣi and asks as her boon only that he forgive her husband, whereupon Dala returns the Vāmī mares and the curse-driven quarrel is closed _(actors: Dala, his queen, Vāmadeva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/manduka-frog-princess-sushobhana.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva CXCIII)
- **The rākṣasa rout** — with their commander dead, the surviving demons break and flee back into Laṅkā as the war moves on to Indrajit's Māyā-Sītā sorcery _(actors: rākṣasa host, vānaras)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/makaraksha-son-of-khara.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 79–80)
- **The robber waylays the brāhmaṇa Śaṅkha** — a hunter-robber strips the sage of his goods, then, pitying his scorched feet in the Vaiśākha heat, returns his sandals and converts _(actors: Śaṅkha, the hunter-robber (Vālmīki-to-be), Dantila, Kohala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/valmiki-ratnakara-conversion.md) — (Skanda Purāṇa, Vaiṣṇava-khaṇḍa, Vaiśākhamāsa-māhātmya 17)
- **The Śambūka episode** — to revive a brahmin's dead son, Rāma beheads the śūdra ascetic Śambūka for yuga-forbidden tapas, the sharpest ethics flashpoint of the epic [late text][disputed] _(actors: Rāma, Śambūka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/rama.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara Kāṇḍa)
- **The Sentence of Abbacy** — the dog asks that its assailant be made kulapati of the Kālañjara maṭha, and Rāma, taking it for a reward, consecrates Sarvārtha-siddha to the office _(actors: the dog, Rāma, Sarvārtha-siddha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramas-justice-the-dog-and-the-mendicant.md) — (Uttara Kāṇḍa, prakṣipta) [late text]
- **The seven-night battle on the Sindhu** — Bharata's army fights the illusion-wielding Gandharva host for seven days and nights without decision _(actors: Bharata, the Gandharvas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bharata-gandharva-conquest-takshashila.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa 101; the host's king Śailūṣa and its three-crore count are the Agni Purāṇa's, ch. 11)
- **The shattered trident** — dismounted, Makarākṣa hurls a blazing śūla (pike) at Rāma, who cuts it to pieces in mid-air with his arrows _(actors: Makarākṣa, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/makaraksha-son-of-khara.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 79)
- **The storming of Mahodaya and Huṇḍa slain** — grown and armed, Nahuṣa breaks Huṇḍa's fortress-host and kills the demon, fulfilling Pārvatī's prophecy and Aśokasundarī's curse in one stroke _(actors: Nahuṣa, Huṇḍa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ashokasundari-nahusha-hunda.md) — (Padma Purāṇa, Bhūmi-khaṇḍa 117–119)
- **The stranger-king's śrāddha releases the preta** — Babhruvāhana performs the rites at Mahodaya and Sudeva, freed from the preta-state, attains heaven — proof-case that even a stranger's śrāddha saves the dead _(actors: Babhruvāhana, Sudeva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/babhruvahana-and-the-preta.md) — (Garuḍa Purāṇa, Preta-khaṇḍa 5; Sāroddhāra 7.66–69)
- **The suitors' siege of Mithilā** — Kings who fail the vīrya-śulka bow-test besiege Mithilā for a year until Janaka, granted an army through tapas, routs them _(actors: Sīradhvaja Janaka, the suitor-kings)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/videha-mithila-line-janaka.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla-kāṇḍa 66)
- **The thousand-headed Rāvaṇa of Vilaṅkā is defeated** — after Laṅkā's war Rāma faces a mightier thousand-headed Rāvaṇa, with Sītā's śakti given the decisive role in cognate tellings [regional] [disputed] _(actors: Rāma, Sītā, the thousand-headed Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/odia-literature-sarala-and-panchasakha.md) — (Sāraḷā Dāsa, Vilaṅkā Rāmāyaṇa)
- **The Trimūrti revealed and the liberation** — the four guests prove to be Brahmā, Viṣṇu, and Śiva in disguise; Rantideva, desiring no boon and fixed in Vāsudeva, sheds all grief and, with his followers, attains the yoga of Nārāyaṇa _(actors: Rantideva, Brahmā, Viṣṇu, Śiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rantideva.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 9.21.13–18)
- **The Vālī-vadha** — Rāma shoots Vālī from concealment during the duel with Sugrīva and answers the dying king's indictment with his rājadharma defense, the epic's classic ethics debate _(actors: Rāma, Vālī, Sugrīva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/rama.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā Kāṇḍa)
- **The Vāmana war (second saṅgrāma)** — Viṣṇu as the Dwarf begs three paces from Bali, strides the worlds as Trivikrama and binds the asura-emperor _(actors: Viṣṇu as Vāmana/Trivikrama, Bali)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/twelve-devasura-battles.md) — (Matsya Purāṇa 47; Bhāgavata Purāṇa 8.15–23)
- **The vengeance-challenge to Rāma** — Makarākṣa confronts Rāma on the field and taunts him for killing Khara in the Daṇḍaka forest, demanding single combat _(actors: Makarākṣa, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/makaraksha-son-of-khara.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 78–79)
- **The wager over Hariścandra's truthfulness** — Vasiṣṭha praises Hariścandra as the most truthful king; Viśvāmitra disputes it and undertakes to break him, with the king as the stake _(actors: Vasiṣṭha, Viśvāmitra, Hariścandra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/hariscandra.md) — (Mārkaṇḍeya Purāṇa 7; Devī Bhāgavata Purāṇa 7)
- **The water-covenant of Parikṣit** — the Ikṣvāku king Parikṣit weds a singing maiden he meets by a forest lake on her one condition — that he never show her water _(actors: Parikṣit, Suśobhanā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/manduka-frog-princess-sushobhana.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana Parva CXCII / CE 3.190)
- **The worlds shaken by wing-blows** — the birds' battering topples mountains, tilts the earth, and surges the oceans, killing multitudes and threatening the ruin of the worlds _(actors: Āḍi, Baka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/adi-baka-bird-war.md) — (Mārkaṇḍeya Purāṇa IX)
- **Trijaṭā among the Aśoka-vana guard** — Trijaṭā is set among the rākṣasīs guarding the captive Sītā in Rāvaṇa's Aśoka grove in Laṅkā _(actors: Trijaṭā, Sītā, Rāvaṇa, guard rākṣasīs)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/trijata.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa)
- **Triśaṅku hung between earth and heaven, localized at Vaḍnagar** — the Ikṣvāku king's bodily ascent and mid-sky suspension by Viśvāmitra is re-homed to the Hāṭakeśvara tīrtha-cycle _(actors: Triśaṅku, Viśvāmitra)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/vadnagar-hatakeshvara-kshetra.md) — (Skanda Purāṇa, Nāgara-khaṇḍa 7)
- **Trishanku's three sins and curse-naming** — Prince Satyavrata angers his father, abducts a bride, and kills Vasiṣṭha's cow, earning the curse-name Triśaṅku from the sage _(actors: Satyavrata/Triśaṅku, Vasiṣṭha, Trayyāruṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/trishanku.md) — (Harivaṃśa; Purāṇas)
- **Ucchi Piḷḷaiyār — struck on the crown** — the enraged Vibhīṣaṇa chases the fleeing boy up the Tiruchirāppaḷḷi rock and strikes him on the crown of the head (ucci) at the summit, whereupon Gaṇeśa reveals himself and stays enshrined at the apex _(actors: Vibhīṣaṇa, Gaṇeśa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/tiruchirappalli-rockfort-thayumanavar.md) — (Sirappaḷḷi sthala-purāṇa)
- **Urvaśī weds Purūravas under three conditions** — The apsarā Urvaśī marries the first Lunar king Purūravas on conditions including that she never see him naked _(actors: Purūravas, Urvaśī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/pururavas-urvashi.md) — (Ṛgveda X.95; Śatapatha Brāhmaṇa XI.5.1; Viṣṇu Purāṇa IV.6)
- **Vālī commends Aṅgada with his dying breath** — struck by Rāma's arrow, the reconciled king of Kiṣkindhā entrusts his son Aṅgada and queen Tārā to the protection of Rāma and Sugrīva, begging the boy be treated as a son _(actors: Vālī, Aṅgada, Tārā, Rāma, Sugrīva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/angada.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa 18, 22)
- **Vālmīki shelters Sītā and swears to her purity** — the sage raises Lava and Kuśa, teaches them the poem for Rāma's Aśvamedha, and stakes his sinless tapas on Sītā's innocence _(actors: Vālmīki, Sītā, Lava, Kuśa, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/valmiki-ratnakara-conversion.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara Kāṇḍa 96)
- **Vamana avatara & subduing of Bali** — Vishnu as a dwarf brahmana begs three paces from asura-king Bali, grows cosmic (Trivikrama), and pushes Bali to Patala _(actors: Vamana/Vishnu, Bali, Kashyapa, Aditi)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Bhagavata Purana, Vishnu Purana, Vamana Purana)
- **Vamana Subdues Bali (Boon of Future Indra-hood)** — Vishnu as Vamana reclaims the three worlds from the daitya king Bali in three strides, pushing him to Patala/Sutala but granting that Bali will become Indra in the future Savarni manvantara as recompense for his truthfulness _(actors: Vishnu; Vamana; Bali; Virochana)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-08-savarni.md) — (Vishnu Purana 3.2; Bhagavata Purana 8.13.15, 8.22-23)
- **Vamana subdues Bali (Trivikrama)** — Vishnu, born as the dwarf brahmana Vamana, begs three paces of land from the asura-king Bali, then grows cosmic (Trivikrama), covers earth and heaven in two strides, and sets his third foot on Bali's head pushing him to rule Patala _(actors: Vamana/Vishnu, Bali, Kashyapa, Aditi)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 8.15-23; Vishnu Purana; Vamana Purana)
- **Varuṇa grants Hariścandra a son to be sacrificed** — Counselled by Nārada, the sonless Hariścandra prays to Varuṇa, who grants him a son, Rohita, on condition the boy be sacrificed back to Varuṇa _(actors: Hariścandra, Varuṇa, Nārada, Rohita)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/hariscandra.md) — (Aitareya Brāhmaṇa 7.13–14)
- **Vasiṣṭha narrates the Cūḍālā-ākhyāna to Rāma** — At Daśaratha's court, Vasiṣṭha tells young Rāma the tale of Cūḍālā and Śikhidhvaja as the model of liberation-in-action: live and rule as they did _(actors: Vasiṣṭha, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/chudala-shikhidhvaja.md) — (Yoga-Vāsiṣṭha, Nirvāṇa Prakaraṇa Pūrvārdha VI.1)
- **Vasiṣṭha teaches Rāma the world is mind** — the sage Vasiṣṭha narrates the Līlā-upākhyāna to young Rāma at Daśaratha's court as the corpus's proof that creation is a projection in consciousness _(actors: Vasiṣṭha, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/lila-story-yoga-vasishtha.md) — (Yoga-Vāsiṣṭha, Utpatti Prakaraṇa III)
- **Vasiṣṭha's discourse to the despondent Rāma** — At Daśaratha's court, as Viśvāmitra arrives, Vasiṣṭha teaches the world-weary sixteen-year-old Rāma through nested parables over successive days _(actors: Vasiṣṭha, Rāma, Viśvāmitra, Daśaratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/yoga-vasishtha-tales-catalog.md) — (Yoga-Vāsiṣṭha, Vairāgya & Mumukṣu prakaraṇas I–II)
- **Vasiṣṭha's discourse to the world-weary Rāma (Yoga-Vāsiṣṭha frame)** — At Daśaratha's court, as Viśvāmitra arrives, sage Vasiṣṭha answers young Rāma's vairāgya with the six-prakaraṇa teaching of ajāta-vāda across hundreds of nested parables _(actors: Vasiṣṭha, Rāma, Viśvāmitra, Daśaratha)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/yoga-vasishtha.md) — (Yoga-Vāsiṣṭha, Vairāgya–Nirvāṇa prakaraṇas)
- **Vasiṣṭha's wrath at the Hariścandra ordeal** — returning after twelve years by the Gaṅgā, Vasiṣṭha learns Viśvāmitra has ruined his royal protégé and burns with anger fiercer than at his own sons' deaths _(actors: Vasiṣṭha, Viśvāmitra, Hariścandra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/adi-baka-bird-war.md) — (Mārkaṇḍeya Purāṇa IX; Devī-Bhāgavata VI.12)
- **Vāyu's wrath twists the hundred maidens** — Refused by Kuśanābha's daughters, who insist their father alone may give them in marriage, the wind-god Vāyu enters their limbs and bends them into hunchbacks (kubjā) — the traditional etymology of Kanyākubja/Kanauj _(actors: Vāyu, the hundred daughters, Kuśanābha)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/kushanabha-daughters-and-brahmadatta.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 32)
- **Vibhīṣaṇa defects to Rāma** — Spurned by Rāvaṇa for urging Sītā's return, Vibhīṣaṇa surrenders to Rāma, who rules that a refuge-seeker must never be turned away and pre-crowns him king of Laṅkā _(actors: Vibhīṣaṇa, Rāma, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 17–19)
- **Vibhīṣaṇa's tapas and boon of righteousness** — In joint austerities at Gokarṇa, Vibhīṣaṇa alone asks that his mind never stray from dharma; pleased, Brahmā additionally grants him immortality _(actors: Vibhīṣaṇa, Brahmā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vibhishana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara Kāṇḍa)
- **Vibhandaka sheds his seed and the doe conceives Rishyashringa** — The ascetic Vibhandaka, overcome at a lake, involuntarily sheds his seed; a doe (a cursed apsaras) drinks it and conceives, bearing a boy and being freed of her curse _(actors: Vibhandaka, the doe-apsaras, Urvashi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rishyashringa.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana-parva ~110)
- **Vidyujjihva slain by Rāvaṇa** — Śūrpaṇakhā's husband, the Kālakeya lord Vidyujjihva, is killed by Rāvaṇa in his world-conquest campaign, widowing his own sister _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Vidyujjihva, Śūrpaṇakhā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shurpanakha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara Kāṇḍa, Kālakeya episode)
- **Vihuṇḍa's revenge and the Kamodā coda** — Huṇḍa's kinsman Vihuṇḍa takes up the vendetta and is slain by Nahuṣa, and the cycle closes with the loosely-transmitted episode of the celestial maiden Kamodā _(actors: Nahuṣa, Vihuṇḍa, Kamodā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ashokasundari-nahusha-hunda.md) — (Padma Purāṇa, Bhūmi-khaṇḍa 120–121)
- **Virādha seizes Sītā in Daṇḍaka** — On entering the Daṇḍaka forest, the man-eating rākṣasa Virādha takes up Sītā in his arms — the first abduction-attempt of the epic _(actors: Virādha, Sītā, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/viradha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 2)
- **Vālmīki composes the first śloka** — Grief at seeing a hunter kill a krauñca bird bursts from Vālmīki as the first anuṣṭubh verse (śoka becoming śloka), birthing Sanskrit poetry _(actors: Vālmīki)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa)
- **Vālmīki witnesses the krauñca bird's death and utters the first śloka** — Grieving a hunter's killing of a mating curlew, Vālmīki's sorrow bursts out as metrical verse, birthing the anuṣṭubh meter and poetry itself _(actors: Vālmīki)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/1-bala-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 2)
- **Vānara host reaches the Setu-kshetra bridgehead** — Rāma marches the vānara army to the Pamban/Dhanuṣkoṭi shore opposite Laṅkā and petitions the ocean-god Sāgara for passage _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Sugrīva, the vānara host, Sāgara)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/rameswaram-setu-bandha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 19–22)
- **Vāyu fathers Hanumān upon Añjanā** — Vāyu fathers Hanumān upon the apsarā-turned-vānarī Añjanā by his wind-agency, making Hanumān Vāyuputra/Pavanaputra _(actors: Vāyu, Añjanā, Hanumān)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/vayu.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Kiṣkindhā 66, Uttara 35-36)
- **Aditi's Payovrata** — Grieving for her dispossessed sons, Aditi performs the twelve-day Payovrata milk-vow on Kaśyapa's instruction; Viṣṇu promises to be born as her son _(actors: Aditi, Kaśyapa, Viṣṇu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vamana-bali.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 8.16–8.17)
- **Agastya approaches and offers the secret** — The sage Agastya, watching the war among the devas, steps forward and offers Rāma an eternal secret for victory _(actors: Agastya, Rāma, the devas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/aditya-hridayam.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 105.2-3)
- **Agastya approaches Rāma** — The sage Agastya, present among the watching gods, draws near and offers Rāma an eternal secret _(actors: Agastya, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/aditya-hridayam.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha-kāṇḍa 105.1)
- **Agastya prescribes the Pāśupata vow** — Sage Agastya comes to the grieving Rāma and prescribes the Pāśupata vrata and worship of Śiva as the means to recover Sītā _(actors: Agastya, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shiva-gita.md) — (Padma Purāṇa, Śiva-Gītā 1–2)
- **A lifetime of waiting at Pampā** — Śabarī keeps the empty āśram ready and gathers fruit day after day for years, watching the road for the promised prince _(actors: Śabarī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shabari.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Araṇya-kāṇḍa 74; bhakti tradition)
- **Anaraṇya gives battle** — Unlike kings who submitted, Anaraṇya accepts battle to defend Ayodhyā and the honor of the Ikṣvāku line _(actors: Anaraṇya, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/anaranya-ravana-curse.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara Kāṇḍa 19)
- **Śarabhaṅga's self-immolation** — Having gained Rāma's darśana, the sage Śarabhaṅga enters the fire and ascends in a radiant body to Brahmaloka _(actors: Śarabhaṅga, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/3-aranya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya Kāṇḍa 5)
- **Arrival of Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa at Mithilā under Viśvāmitra** — Viśvāmitra escorts Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa to Janaka's court, where they are received by Janaka and his priest Śatānanda, and Viśvāmitra asks that the boys be shown Śiva's bow _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Viśvāmitra, Janaka, Śatānanda)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/janakpur-mithila-sita-janmabhumi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla-kāṇḍa 66)
- **Avatara of Mayureshvara** — Ganesha descends in Treta Yuga as the peacock-mounted Mayureshvara and slays the demon Sindhu(rasura); his Morgaon form heads the Ashtavinayaka shrines _(actors: Ganesha (Mayureshvara), Sindhurasura)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/ganesha-and-mudgala-upapuranas.md) — (Ganesha Purana (Krida-khanda))
- **Bharadvāja's miraculous feast for Bharata's army** — Bharata, marching with his army to bring Rāma back, halts at Bharadvāja's āśrama; the sage by ascetic power conjures a vast miraculous feast — divine food, palaces, music and apsaras — entertaining the whole host before Bharata proceeds to Chitrakūṭa _(actors: Bharadvāja, Bharata)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/prayagraj-triveni-sangam.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa sarga ~91)
- **Birth of Bharata** — Kaikeyī bears Bharata from her share of the sacrificial pāyasa _(actors: Kaikeyī, Bharata, Daśaratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kaikeyi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla 16–18)
- **Birth of Kauśika (Viśvāmitra), Gādhi's son** — Gādhi's son Kauśika, born a kṣatriya prince, renounces kingship and rises by tapas to brahmarṣi status as Viśvāmitra _(actors: Gādhi, Kauśika/Viśvāmitra)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/gadhi-and-the-caru-swap.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa; Mahābhārata Ādi & Vana Parva)
- **Birth of Rāma at Ayodhyā** — Rama, the seventh avatar of Vishnu, is born at Ayodhya on the Sarayu in the Treta Yuga _(actors: Rama)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/sapta-puri-and-sacred-cities.md) — (Garuda Purana; Valmiki Ramayana)
- **Birth of the horned Rishyashringa** — Because of his doe-mother the child is born with a single horn on his forehead, hence Rishyashringa, and is raised in total forest isolation ignorant of women _(actors: Rishyashringa, Vibhandaka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rishyashringa.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana-parva ~110-111)
- **Brahma Commissions the Ramayana** — Brahma appears to Valmiki and commissions him to compose the story of Rama, establishing the adi-kavya (first poem) _(actors: Brahma, Valmiki)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/README.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Bala Kanda 2)
- **Brahmā commissions Vālmīki to compose the Rāmāyaṇa** — Brahmā commands Vālmīki to compose Rāma's life in the new meter, granting divine insight into every event _(actors: Brahmā, Vālmīki)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/1-bala-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 2)
- **Brahmā creates the Fifth Veda from the four Vedas** — Brahmā draws recitation from Ṛg, song from Sāma, abhinaya from Yajus, and rasa from Atharva to form the Nāṭyaveda _(actors: Brahmā)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/natya-shastra-and-the-classical-arts.md) — (Nāṭya Śāstra 1.17–1.18)
- **Brahmā gives Ahalyā to Gautama in marriage** — Brahmā fashions Ahalyā as the most beautiful woman and awards her to Gautama (in a variant, via a contest to circle the worlds that Gautama wins over Indra), seeding Indra's resentment _(actors: Brahmā, Ahalyā, Gautama, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ahalya.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 48; Brahma Purāṇa)
- **Building of the Setu (Rāma-Setu)** — After Rāma's penance compels the ocean-god Sāgara, the vānara Nala directs the building of a hundred-yojana floating causeway in five days, and the army crosses to Laṅkā _(actors: Rāma, Nala, Sāgara)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 21–22)
- **Courtesans lure Ṛśyaśṛṅga to end Aṅga's drought** — King Lomapāda sends courtesans disguised as ascetics to lure the chaste sage to Aṅga; his arrival breaks the years-long drought with rain _(actors: Ṛśyaśṛṅga, Lomapāda, the courtesans, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rishyashringa.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva ~111-112)
- **Descent of Vishnu as Rama** — In the standard avatar-to-yuga mapping of this Mahayuga, Vishnu descends as Rama in the Treta Yuga _(actors: Vishnu; Rama)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/README.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 1.3; Valmiki Ramayana)
- **Forester's vow and Lakṣmaṇa's night-watch** — Rāma declines cooked food and beds, taking only water and fodder; Lakṣmaṇa keeps vigil with Guha through the first night by the sleeping Rāma and Sītā _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Guha)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/sringaverapura-guha-ganga-crossing.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa 50–52)
- **Gālava's Horses and Mādhavī's Loaning** — Pressed for 800 special horses as guru-dakṣiṇā, Gālava loans Yayāti's daughter Mādhavī to three kings and Viśvāmitra to earn sons and horses, the debt finally closed by Garuḍa _(actors: Gālava, Viśvāmitra, Mādhavī, Yayāti, Aṣṭaka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/minor-upakhyanas-catalog.md) — (Mahabharata Udyoga Parva 105-121)
- **Gārgī's first questioning — the warp-and-woof chain** — Gārgī Vācaknavī asks on what each cosmic level is woven 'warp and woof', driving Yājñavalkya up a regress from water through air, sun, moon, stars, the gods, Indra and Prajāpati to the worlds of Brahman _(actors: Gārgī Vācaknavī, Yājñavalkya)_ → [details](../07-acharyas-and-sampradayas/gargi-and-maitreyi.md) — (Bṛhadāraṇyaka Upaniṣad 3.6)
- **Hanumān passes Maināka and outwits Surasā** — On the crossing Hanumān courteously refuses the rest offered by the golden mountain Maināka and outwits the nāga-mother Surasā's mouth-test of strength and wit _(actors: Hanumān, Maināka, Surasā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/5-sundara-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 1)
- **Hanumān's leap from Mahendragiri to Laṅkā** — From the summit of Mahendra(giri) Hanumān makes his great ocean-leap to Laṅkā, treading the peak down under the force of his spring at the opening of the Sundara-kāṇḍa _(actors: Hanumān)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/meru-kailash-and-cosmic-mountains.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa (Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa, Sundara-kāṇḍa))
- **Hariścandra surrenders his kingdom** — To appease Viśvāmitra, Hariścandra gives away Ayodhyā and all its wealth, keeping only his wife, son, and bark-cloth, then owes a further gold dakṣiṇā _(actors: Hariścandra, Viśvāmitra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/hariscandra.md) — (Mārkaṇḍeya Purāṇa 7-8)
- **Hariscandra's vow to Varuna for a son** — Childless Ikshvaku king Hariscandra, counselled by Narada, prays to Varuna for a son and vows to sacrifice the boy back; Rohita is born _(actors: Hariscandra, Narada, Varuna, Rohita)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/vedas/brahmanas/aitareya-brahmana.md) — (Aitareya Brahmana 7.13)
- **Harishchandra defers the sacrifice and Varuna curses him with dropsy** — Harishchandra repeatedly postpones offering Rohita; for the broken vow Varuna seizes him with dropsy, and the grown Rohita flees to the forest _(actors: Harishchandra, Varuna, Rohita)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sunahshepa.md) — (Aitareya Brahmana 7.14)
- **Indra strikes the infant Hanuman's jaw** — Mistaking the sun for a fruit, infant Hanuman leaps to swallow it; Indra strikes him with the vajra, breaking his jaw and giving him his name _(actors: Hanuman, Indra, the sun)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/hanuman.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Uttara Kanda; Ramcharitmanas)
- **Śiva presses the mountain down** — Śiva pins Rāvaṇa beneath Kailāsa with the toe of His foot, crushing his arms _(actors: Śiva, Pārvatī, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/shiva-tandava-stotra.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Uttarakāṇḍa)
- **Jamadagni's marriage to Reṇukā** — Jamadagni weds Reṇukā, daughter of King Prasenajit/Reṇu, a model pativratā whose yogic merit lets her carry water in unbaked clay _(actors: Jamadagni, Reṇukā, Prasenajit/Reṇu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/jamadagni-parashurama.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 9.15; Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa)
- **Kabandha seizes Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa** — Searching south after Jaṭāyu's death, the brothers are caught one in each of Kabandha's yojana-long arms and drawn toward his belly-mouth _(actors: Kabandha, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kabandha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 69)
- **Kaikeyī's boons and Bharata's absence** — While Bharata and Śatrughna are away in Kekaya, Kaikeyī (prompted by Mantharā) extracts the boons exiling Rāma and crowning Bharata; the brothers return to find Daśaratha dead _(actors: Kaikeyī, Mantharā, Bharata, Śatrughna, Daśaratha, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bharata-shatrughna-and-their-wives.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa)
- **Kapila's Wrath Burns Sagara's Sons** — Sage Kapila's wrath burns Sagara's 60,000 sons to ash, the catastrophe that prompts the eventual descent of Gaṅgā to redeem them; his ashrama tradition is localized at Haridwar _(actors: Kapila, sons of Sagara)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/haridwar-rishikesh-ganga-dwara.md) — (Wikipedia, Haridwar)
- **Kausalyā receives the foremost half of the pāyasa** — Daśaratha gives the senior queen Kausalyā a full half of the divine pāyasa, from which Rāma is conceived _(actors: Daśaratha, Kausalyā, Sumitrā, Kaikeyī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kausalya.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 16)
- **Kāmadhenu at the hermitage** — Jamadagni's āśrama holds the wish-cow Kāmadhenu (Suśīlā/Surabhi) who furnishes endless hospitality _(actors: Jamadagni, Kāmadhenu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/parashurama.md) — (Mahābhārata 3.116; Bhāgavata Purāṇa 9.15)
- **Lakṣmaṇa weds Ūrmilā (fourfold wedding)** — At Mithilā, alongside Rāma's wedding to Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa weds Ūrmilā in a fourfold marriage binding Ayodhyā and Mithilā _(actors: Lakṣmaṇa, Ūrmilā, Rāma, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/lakshmana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla-kāṇḍa)
- **Lomapāda's brāhmaṇa-slight and the Aṅga drought** — Lomapāda, distracted in conversation with Śāntā, slights a visiting brāhmaṇa, whose departure brings on the drought (Indra withholding rain) that will require the chaste ascetic Ṛśyaśṛṅga _(actors: Lomapāda, Śāntā, an offended brāhmaṇa, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rishyashringa.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Bāla-kāṇḍa (variant frame); Mahābhārata Vana-parva ~111)
- **Mantharā alarmed for Bharata** — Seized with fury, she fears Kaikeyī and Bharata will be subordinated to Kausalyā under King Rāma _(actors: Mantharā, (re: Bharata, Kaikeyī, Kausalyā))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/manthara.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 7–8)
- **Mantharā poisons Kaikeyī's mind** — The hunchback maid Mantharā convinces Kaikeyī that Rāma's coronation will ruin her and Bharata, coaching her to invoke the two old boons _(actors: Mantharā, Kaikeyī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/2-ayodhya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 7–11)
- **Marriage to Sunda and birth of her sons** — Tāṭakā weds the yakṣa Sunda and bears two sons, Mārīca and Subāhu _(actors: Tāṭakā, Sunda, Mārīca, Subāhu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/tataka.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 25)
- **Maya fathers Mandodarī** — Maya's daughter Mandodarī becomes the chief queen of Rāvaṇa, binding the architect to the Laṅkā rākṣasa world _(actors: Maya, Mandodarī, Hemā, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/maya-danava.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa; Uttara Kāṇḍa tradition)
- **Nahuṣa's pride and lust for Śacī** — Corrupted by heaven's splendour, Nahuṣa covets Indra's queen Śacī, who stalls him by demanding he arrive in a palanquin borne by the Saptarṣis _(actors: Nahuṣa, Śacī, Bṛhaspati)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nahusha.md) — (Mahābhārata Udyoga Parva 13-17)
- **Nandī's warning and curse** — Nandī forbids passage as Śiva sports with Pārvatī; mocked by Rāvaṇa, he curses that monkey-like beings will destroy him _(actors: Nandī, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ravana-kailasa-ravananugraha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttarakāṇḍa 16)
- **Night vigil and matting of hair** — Rama sleeps on a grass-bed; Lakshmana and Guha keep watch through the night, and at dawn Guha supplies banyan sap to mat Rama's and Lakshmana's hair into ascetic jata _(actors: Guha, Lakshmana, Rama, Sita)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/guha.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana Ayodhya-kanda 50-52)
- **Parashurama avatara & Kartavirya Arjuna** — Vishnu as the brahmana-warrior Parashurama slays the thousand-armed king Kartavirya Arjuna who seized the wish-cow Kamadhenu _(actors: Parashurama, Jamadagni, Renuka, Kartavirya Arjuna)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata, Bhagavata Purana, Vishnu Purana)
- **Parashurama slays Kartavirya Arjuna** — The thousand-armed Haihaya king Kartavirya Arjuna seizes the wish-cow Kamadhenu from Jamadagni's hermitage, and Parashurama slays him; in revenge Kartavirya's sons kill the unarmed Jamadagni _(actors: Parashurama, Kartavirya Arjuna, Jamadagni, Kamadhenu)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata; Bhagavata Purana 9.15-16)
- **Paraśurāma's test of the Śārṅga bow on Rāma** — After Rāma breaks Śiva's Pināka at Sītā's svayaṃvara, Paraśurāma confronts him and challenges him to string Viṣṇu's bow Śārṅga as a test of his divinity; Rāma bends the bow, confirming him as Viṣṇu's portion, and Paraśurāma yields _(actors: Paraśurāma, Rāma)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divyastras-and-divine-weapons.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa (Bāla Kāṇḍa); mysutradhar (All About Divine Bows))
- **Purūravas founds the Lunar dynasty at Pratiṣṭhāna** — Purūravas performs penance to Brahmā, gains sovereignty, and becomes the first king of the Candravaṃśa, ruling from Pratiṣṭhāna (Prayāga) _(actors: Purūravas, Brahmā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/pururavas-urvashi.md) — (Vishnu Purana IV.6; Bhagavata Purana IX.14)
- **Raghu's duel with Indra over the sacrificial horse** — When Indra steals Dilipa's hundredth Ashvamedha horse, the boy Raghu pursues and duels Indra, winning his admiration and a boon _(actors: Raghu, Indra, Dilipa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/raghu.md) — (Raghuvamsha 3.38-66)
- **Rama enters Dandakaranya** — Rama, Sita and Lakshmana pass into the vast demon-haunted southern forest at the opening of the Aranya-kanda _(actors: Rama, Sita, Lakshmana)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Aranya-kanda 1)
- **Reṇukā's lapse on seeing Citraratha and loss of power** — Reṇukā glimpses the gandharva Citraratha (variant: a king) sporting with celestial women and is moved for an instant; her broken chastity-power dissolves the unbaked pot and she returns empty-handed, and Jamadagni perceives her lapse and rages _(actors: Reṇukā, Citraratha, Jamadagni)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/devi-forms-and-navadurga.md) — (Mahābhārata 3; folk Reṇukā Māhātmya)
- **Rāma breaks Śiva's bow and wins Sītā** — At Mithilā's bow-test Rāma lifts and snaps the colossal divine bow of Śiva, winning Sītā's hand; the four princes wed in one ceremony _(actors: Rāma, Sītā, Janaka, Viśvāmitra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sita.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa)
- **Rāma hurls Mārīca into the sea with the Mānavāstra** — Rāma looses the Mānavāstra at Mārīca, which spares his life but flings him a hundred yojanas into the ocean, while Subāhu is slain by the Āgneyāstra _(actors: Rāma, Mārīca, Subāhu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/maricha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla-kāṇḍa (Mārīca-Subāhu canto-block))
- **Rāma's lament at the lotus-lake Pampā** — Arriving at the blossoming spring lake Pampā near Kiṣkindhā, Rāma's grief for the abducted Sītā is sharpened, opening the Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kishkindha-hampi-anegundi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa 1)
- **Rāma teaches bhakti as gateway, jñāna as cause** — Rāma answers that devotion to him as supreme Lord is the gateway and that liberating knowledge is the direct cause of release _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rama-gita.md) — (Adhyātma Rāmāyaṇa 7.5)
- **Rohita flees and Indra's 'caraiveti' refrain** — Told he must die, Rohita flees to the forest for years while Indra, disguised, repeatedly urges him to keep wandering _(actors: Rohita, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sunahshepa.md) — (Aitareya Brāhmaṇa 7.14-15)
- **Rohita flees and Varuṇa curses Hariścandra** — Told he is to die, Rohita escapes to the forest and wanders six years urged on by Indra ('caraiveti'), while Varuṇa afflicts the king with dropsy for the broken vow _(actors: Rohita, Indra, Varuṇa, Hariścandra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/hariscandra.md) — (Aitareya Brāhmaṇa 7.14–15)
- **Rāvaṇa's Austerities at Rākṣas-tāl** — The rākṣasa king Rāvaṇa performs severe austerities at Rākṣas-tāl, offering his ten heads to Śiva to win boons, naming the lake Rāvaṇa-hrada _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Śiva)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/manasarovar-kailash-yatra.md) — (folk / sthala tradition; Valmiki Ramayana Uttara-kanda)
- **Rāvaṇa's tapas and Brahmā's boon** — After fierce penance, Rāvaṇa offers his heads one by one into the fire; Brahmā stops him and grants invulnerability to all celestial and demonic beings — but, in contempt, Rāvaṇa omits men and animals _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Brahmā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ravana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa)
- **Rāvaṇa's tapas and the boon of the Ātma-liṅga** — Rāvaṇa performs extreme austerities, offering his heads, until Śiva grants the Ātma-liṅga with the warning that it roots wherever it touches earth _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Śiva)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/gokarna-mahabaleshwar-atma-linga.md) — (Śiva Purāṇa; Gokarṇa-māhātmya)
- **Satyavrata's charity during the twelve-year famine** — The exiled prince sustains Viśvāmitra's starving family by hunting, freeing the son Gālava from being sold; the sage repays with gratitude _(actors: Satyavrata, Viśvāmitra's family, Gālava)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/trishanku.md) — (Harivaṃśa)
- **Sītā discovered beneath the śiṃśapā** — Hidden in an aśoka tree, Hanumān beholds the captive Sītā among rākṣasī wardresses and recognises her by Rāma's described ornaments _(actors: Hanumān, Sītā, rākṣasīs)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/lanka-ramayana-trail-ashoka-vatika.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 15–16)
- **Sītā's earth-birth in the furrow** — King Janaka ploughs the sacrificial field and uncovers the infant Sītā in the furrow — ayonijā, daughter of Earth _(actors: Janaka (Sīradhvaja), Sītā, Bhūmi (Earth))_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/janakpur-mithila-sita-janmabhumi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla-kāṇḍa 66; Adhyātma Rāmāyaṇa; Adbhuta Rāmāyaṇa)
- **Sumitrā receives a doubled share of the pāyasa** — Sumitrā is given two portions drawn from both co-queens' shares, and so conceives twins _(actors: Daśaratha, Sumitrā, Kausalyā, Kaikeyī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sumitra.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla-kāṇḍa 16)
- **Summons of Rāma before the abhiṣeka** — Sumantra is sent by Daśaratha to fetch Rāma to the assembly for the heir-apparent consecration, and again into the king's chamber past Kaikeyī as the crisis breaks _(actors: Sumantra, Daśaratha, Rāma, Kaikeyī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sumantra.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa 14–16)
- **The cow's army routs Kauśika** — Śabalā/Nandinī snorts forth armies of foreign peoples who defeat Kauśika's forces, and the sage's brahmadaṇḍa swallows every weapon _(actors: Vasiṣṭha, Śabalā, Kauśika)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vishwamitra-vasishtha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 54-56)
- **The demonesses threaten Sītā** — The other rākṣasīs cajole then threaten to devour Sītā, driving her to despair _(actors: Guard rākṣasīs, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/trijata.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 23–26)
- **The fire-witnessed friendship-pact of Rāma and Sugrīva** — Rāma and Sugrīva swear mutual aid with Agni as witness — Rāma to slay Vāli and restore Sugrīva, Sugrīva to mobilize the vānaras to find Sītā _(actors: Rāma, Sugrīva, Hanumān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/4-kishkindha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā Kāṇḍa 5)
- **The gandharvas' trick breaks the condition; Urvaśī departs** — Gandharvas steal Urvaśī's rams; Purūravas leaps up naked, a lightning-flash exposes him, and Urvaśī vanishes _(actors: Purūravas, Urvaśī, the gandharvas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/pururavas-urvashi.md) — (Śatapatha Brāhmaṇa XI.5.1; Viṣṇu Purāṇa IV.6)
- **The gods' revelation and boon to Śibi** — Indra and Agni reveal themselves, heal Śibi's wounds, declare his fame imperishable, and promise him a son Kapotaroma _(actors: Śibi, Indra, Agni, Kapotaroma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shibi.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva)
- **The lapse — loss of chastity by thought** — Reṇukā momentarily admires the gandharva Citraratha; her chastity-power breaks and the pot dissolves in her hands _(actors: Reṇukā, gandharva Citraratha)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/renuka-yellamma.md) — (Mahābhārata 3.116; folk Reṇukā Māhātmya)
- **The leaf-hut on the Mandākinī** — Lakṣmaṇa builds a parṇa-śālā at Citrakūṭa where the three live in forest-domesticity beside the Mandākinī river _(actors: Rāma, Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/chitrakoot-kamadgiri.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa 56)
- **The Śravaṇa-kumāra accidental killing and death-by-grief curse** — Young Daśaratha kills the ascetic boy Śravaṇa with a sound-aimed arrow and is cursed by the boy's blind parents to die of grief for his own son _(actors: Daśaratha, Śravaṇa-kumāra, the blind ascetic parents)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dasharatha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Ayodhyā 63–64; Kālidāsa Raghuvaṃśa 9)
- **The Setu-bandha (Nala-setu / Rāma-setu) is built** — Sāgara names the boon-blessed vānara Nala, who directs construction of the 100×10 yojana causeway in five days; the army crosses to Laṅkā _(actors: Nala (son of Viśvakarman), Sāgara, the vānaras, Rāma)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/rameswaram-setu-bandha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 22)
- **The slighted kings ambush Aja** — The rejected suitors waylay Aja's wedding party on the road to Ayodhyā; Aja defeats and (using the gāndharva/Saṃmohana weapon) stupefies them, then releases them _(actors: Aja, Indumatī, the rejected suitor-kings, gandharva Priyaṃvada)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/aja-indumati.md) — (Kālidāsa, Raghuvaṃśa cantos 5, 7)
- **The weapon-proof boon and capture of the brothers** — Virādha reveals Brahmā's boon making him invulnerable to weapons; the brothers' arrows fail and he seizes both Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa, one under each arm _(actors: Virādha, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/viradha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 3)
- **The wish-cow's army routs Kauśika** — Refused the cow and trying to seize her by force, Kauśika is routed when the cow snorts forth armies of foreign peoples and his hundred sons are burnt to ash _(actors: Kauśika, Vasiṣṭha, Śabalā/Nandinī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vishwamitra-vasishtha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 53-55)
- **Trishanku's charity during the twelve-year famine** — During a twelve-year famine the exiled Satyavrata hunts to feed Vishvamitra's destitute family and frees the son Galava from being sold, earning the sage's gratitude _(actors: Satyavrata, Vishvamitra, Galava)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/trishanku.md) — (Harivamsha)
- **Vamana grants Bali immortality and rule of Patala** — Vishnu as Vamana presses Bali to Patala in three strides but, moved by his truthfulness, grants him rulership of Sutala, immortality, and guards his door _(actors: Vamana, Bali)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/the-chiranjivis.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 8.15-23; Vamana Purana)
- **Vasiṣṭha's diagnosis — the slighted wish-cow** — Vasiṣṭha reveals that Dilīpa once passed the celestial cow Surabhi (Kāmadhenu) without homage and was cursed childless, prescribing service to her daughter Nandinī as expiation _(actors: Vasiṣṭha, Dilīpa, Surabhi/Kāmadhenu, Nandinī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/dilipa-nandini.md) — (Raghuvaṃśa 1.72–88 (Kālidāsa))
- **Vibhīṣaṇa's śaraṇāgati at the Kodaṇḍarāma site** — Local sthala tradition ties the Dhanuṣkoḍi Kodaṇḍarāma shrine to the spot where Vibhīṣaṇa surrendered to and took refuge with the bow-bearing Rāma and was anointed king-to-be of Laṅkā _(actors: Vibhīṣaṇa, Rāma)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/rameswaram-setu-bandha.md) — (Rāmanātha sthala-purāṇa)
- **Vibhīṣaṇa spurned in Rāvaṇa's court** — Counseling the return of Sītā and warning of ruin, Vibhīṣaṇa is enraged-spurned and insulted by Rāvaṇa in open court, driving the final break _(actors: Vibhīṣaṇa, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vibhishana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa)
- **Viśvāmitra names Mārīca and Subāhu and asks for Rāma** — Viśvāmitra seeks the boy-princes from Daśaratha because Mārīca and Subāhu repeatedly wreck his sacrifices _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Daśaratha, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Mārīca, Subāhu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/subahu.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 19–20)
- **Vālī kills Dundubhi and earns Mātaṅga's curse** — Vālī slays the buffalo-demon Dundubhi and hurls the carcass onto Mātaṅga's hermitage, earning a curse that his head will burst if he sets foot on Ṛṣyamūka _(actors: Vālī, Dundubhi, Mātaṅga)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sugriva-and-vali.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa)
- **Vāyu withdraws air at Hanumān's striking down** — When the infant Hanumān is struck down by Indra's vajra, the grieving Vāyu withdraws all air from the worlds, suffocating creation until the gods revive Hanumān and grant him boons _(actors: Vāyu, Hanumān, Indra)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/vayu.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Uttara Kāṇḍa 36; Śiva Purāṇa)
- **Accession of Raghu to Kosala** — Dilipa installs Raghu on the throne of Ayodhya and retires to the forest as a vanaprastha _(actors: Raghu, Dilipa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/raghu.md) — (Raghuvamsha 3.69-70)
- **Aṅgada's embassy to Rāvaṇa** — Rāma sends Vāli's son Aṅgada as envoy demanding Sītā's release; Rāvaṇa orders him seized, but Aṅgada shakes off the guards and departs defiantly _(actors: Aṅgada, Rāvaṇa, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 41)
- **Agastya's curse turns Nahuṣa into a python** — Yoking the Saptarṣis and kicking Agastya while crying 'sarpa', Nahuṣa is cursed by the sage to fall from heaven as a python _(actors: Nahuṣa, Agastya, the Saptarṣis)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/nahusha.md) — (Mahābhārata Udyoga Parva 17; Vana Parva 178)
- **Agastya teaches the Āditya-Hṛdayam** — Agastya imparts the ~30-śloka Sūrya-hymn, declaring it will let Rāma conquer all enemies in battle _(actors: Agastya, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/aditya-hridayam.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha-kāṇḍa 105.2–22)
- **Ajīgarta sells and binds his son Sunaḥśepa** — Rohita buys the middle son Sunaḥśepa for a hundred cows as a substitute victim; the father Ajīgarta agrees to bind and butcher his own child for more cattle _(actors: Rohita, Sunaḥśepa, Ajīgarta)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sunahshepa.md) — (Aitareya Brāhmaṇa 7.15-16)
- **Śatrughna beats Mantharā** — On returning, Śatrughna seizes and beats the hunchback Mantharā in fury until Bharata restrains him _(actors: Śatrughna, Mantharā, Bharata)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bharata-shatrughna-and-their-wives.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa)
- **Bharata's march to Citrakūṭa** — Refusing the throne after Daśaratha's death, Bharata leads all of Ayodhyā to Citrakūṭa to beg Rāma to return and reign _(actors: Bharata, Kaikeyī, Vasiṣṭha, the people of Ayodhyā)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/chitrakoot-kamadgiri.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa 99–112)
- **Birth and naming of Lakṣmaṇa and Śatrughna** — Sumitrā bears the twins Lakṣmaṇa and Śatrughna, named and horoscoped by Vasiṣṭha; each attaches to an elder brother _(actors: Sumitrā, Lakṣmaṇa, Śatrughna, Vasiṣṭha, Rāma, Bharata)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sumitra.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla-kāṇḍa 18)
- **Birth of Rama at Ayodhya** — After Dasharatha's putra-kameshti sacrifice, Rama and his brothers are born at Ayodhya on Rama Navami, the seventh avatara of Vishnu descending into Suryavamsha flesh _(actors: Dasharatha, Rama, Vasishtha)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/ayodhya.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Bala-kanda)
- **Birth of Rama via Putra-kameshti Sacrifice** — Dasharatha performs the putra-kameshti (son-desiring) sacrifice, resulting in the birth of Rama and his brothers — the avatara-descent of Vishnu _(actors: Dasharatha, Rama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/README.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Bala Kanda)
- **Birth of Rāma** — Kausalyā bears Rāma in Caitra under Punarvasu with planets exalted, marked with the signs of Viṣṇu (Rāma-Navamī) _(actors: Kausalyā, Rāma, Daśaratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kausalya.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 18)
- **Birth of Vāmana** — Viṣṇu is born to Aditi and Kaśyapa as the dwarf-brahmin Vāmana, fifth of the Daśāvatāra, and undergoes upanayana receiving staff, deerskin and water-pot _(actors: Vāmana (Viṣṇu), Aditi, Kaśyapa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vamana-bali.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 8.18; Vāmana Purāṇa)
- **Boons earned from Kaikeyī in the Devāsura (Śambara) war** — Wounded fighting as Indra's ally against the asura Śambara, Daśaratha is saved by Kaikeyī and grants her two boons to be claimed at will _(actors: Daśaratha, Kaikeyī, Śambara, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dasharatha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Ayodhyā 9 (retrospective))
- **Breaking and burying Virādha alive** — Unable to slay him by weapons, Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa hack off his arms, dig a pit, and bury him alive — circumventing the boon by interment _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Virādha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/viradha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 4)
- **Children of Gautama and Ahalyā** — The union produces Śatānanda (Janaka's purohita) and, in some traditions, Cirakārī and the daughter Añjanā _(actors: Gautama, Ahalyā, Śatānanda, Cirakārī, Añjanā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ahalya.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 51; Mahābhārata Śānti Parva)
- **Crossing the Ganga** — Guha provides a boat and personally has Rama, Sita, and Lakshmana ferried across the Ganga; Sita prays to the river for a safe return _(actors: Guha, Rama, Sita, Lakshmana, boatmen)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/guha.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana Ayodhya-kanda 52)
- **Daśaratha performs the Putrakāmeṣṭi sacrifice** — The childless Ikṣvāku king Daśaratha, on Vasiṣṭha's counsel, has the sage Ṛśyaśṛṅga conduct the son-desiring oblation after an Aśvamedha _(actors: Daśaratha, Ṛśyaśṛṅga, Vasiṣṭha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/1-bala-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 13–16)
- **Death of Sunda and Agastya's curse** — After Sunda is destroyed and Tāṭakā with Mārīca attacks his hermitage, Agastya curses her into a hideous man-eating rākṣasī and Mārīca into a rākṣasa _(actors: Agastya, Tāṭakā, Mārīca, Sunda)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/tataka.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 25)
- **Defeat and mortal wounding of Anaraṇya** — Rāvaṇa routs Anaraṇya's army and strikes the king down mortally wounded; Rāvaṇa taunts the famed might of the Ikṣvākus _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Anaraṇya)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/anaranya-ravana-curse.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara Kāṇḍa 19)
- **Driving the exiles' chariot out of Ayodhyā** — Sumantra yokes the royal chariot and drives Rāma, Sītā, and Lakṣmaṇa from the palace; caught between Daśaratha's plea to halt and Rāma's order to drive faster _(actors: Sumantra, Rāma, Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa, Daśaratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sumantra.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa 39–40)
- **Drought strikes Anga after Lomapada's offence** — Indra withholds rain from Anga because King Lomapada sinned against a brahmana; the brahmins counsel that only the chaste ascetic Rishyashringa can break the drought _(actors: Lomapada, Indra, Anga brahmins)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rishyashringa.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana-parva ~111)
- **Entry into Daṇḍaka and the hermits' plea** — Rāma, Sītā and Lakṣmaṇa enter Daṇḍaka, are shown the bones of brahmins devoured by rākṣasas, and Rāma vows to clear the forest of demons _(actors: Rāma, Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa, forest ṛṣis)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/dandakaranya-rama-forest.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 1, 5–6, 9)
- **Goddess slays Mahishasura (charter)** — The Devi Mahatmya charter in which the Goddess as primordial garbha-shakti/mula-prakriti slays the buffalo-demon Mahishasura, underwriting the womb-energy reading of the garbo _(actors: Durga/Devi, Mahishasura, Madhu-Kaitabha, Shumbha-Nishumbha)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/garba-and-dandiya-raas.md) — (Devi Mahatmya / Durga Saptashati)
- **Hanuman and the mermaid Suvannamaccha** — A pan-Southeast-Asian non-Valmiki episode in which the mermaid princess Suvannamaccha tries to sabotage the bridge to Lanka but is won over by Hanuman and bears him a fish-tailed son, Macchanu _(actors: Hanuman, Suvannamaccha (Sovanna Maccha), Macchanu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/recensions/southeast-asian-ramayanas.md) — (Ramakien; Reamker)
- **Hanumān kills Siṃhikā** — The shadow-seizing sea-monster Siṃhikā drags Hanumān down; he plunges into her, rends her organs from within, kills her, and flies on _(actors: Hanumān, Siṃhikā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/5-sundara-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 1)
- **Hariścandra sells his family and himself** — To pay the fee, Hariścandra sells queen Śaivyā and son Rohitāśva into bondage and sells himself to a Cāṇḍāla running the Kāśī cremation ground _(actors: Hariścandra, Śaivyā/Candramatī, Rohitāśva, the Cāṇḍāla (Yama))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/hariscandra.md) — (Mārkaṇḍeya Purāṇa 8)
- **Śibi gives away his eyes (Jātaka)** — In the Buddhist Sibi Jātaka, Sakka disguised as a blind brāhmaṇa begs an eye; Sivi gives both, and his sight is restored by an Act of Truth _(actors: Śibi/Sivi, Sakka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shibi.md) — (Sibi Jātaka no. 499; Jātakamālā 2)
- **Indra's caraiveti refrain keeps Rohita wandering** — Five times Indra appears as a brahmin urging the wandering Rohita onward with the verse 'keep wandering.' _(actors: Indra, Rohita)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sunahshepa.md) — (Aitareya Brahmana 7.15)
- **Institution of Navarātri; Rāma's Navarātri-pūjā** — The Devī Bhāgavata institutes Navarātri and recounts Rāma performing the Navarātri-pūjā to win the Goddess's aid _(actors: the Goddess, Rāma)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/devi-bhagavata-upapurana.md) — (Devī Bhāgavata, skandha III)
- **Śiva grants the axe** — Paraśurāma worships Śiva and receives the celestial battle-axe (paraśu) from which he takes his name, with martial mastery _(actors: Paraśurāma, Śiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/parashurama.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 9.15 (late text))
- **Śiva's theophany** — Pleased by Rāma's austerities, Śiva appears with Pārvatī and the gaṇas to answer Rāma's questions on suffering and the self _(actors: Śiva, Pārvatī, the gaṇas, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shiva-gita.md) — (Padma Purāṇa, Śiva-Gītā 3)
- **Jamadagni's order to behead her** — Enraged, Jamadagni commands each son to slay their mother; the four elders refuse and are cursed _(actors: Jamadagni, his five sons)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/renuka-yellamma.md) — (Mahābhārata 3.116; Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa)
- **Kabandha directs Rāma to Śabarī** — The freed headless rākṣasa Kabandha sends Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa to Pampā and to Śabarī's hermitage _(actors: Kabandha, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shabari.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Araṇya-kāṇḍa 72–73)
- **Kabandha revealed as the cursed gandharva Viśvāvasu** — On cremation the headless rākṣasa Kabandha reveals he was the gandharva Viśvāvasu (var. Danu son of Śrī), cursed and now released, and directs Rāma toward Sugrīva _(actors: Kabandha; Viśvāvasu; Rāma; Lakṣmaṇa; Sugrīva)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/gandharvas-and-kinnaras.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya Kāṇḍa)
- **Kaikeyī demands Bharata's crowning and Rāma's exile** — Binding Daśaratha to his oath, Kaikeyī claims her two boons: Bharata consecrated king and Rāma exiled to the Daṇḍaka forest for fourteen years _(actors: Kaikeyī, Daśaratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/2-ayodhya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 10–13)
- **Kauśika abandons kingship for tapas** — Humiliated by brahman-power, Kauśika renounces his kingdom to pursue ascetic ascent toward brahmarṣi status _(actors: Viśvāmitra (Kauśika))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vishwamitra-vasishtha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 56)
- **Kubera rules golden Lanka** — Kubera first rules the golden island-city of Lanka, built for him by Vishvakarma as the wealth-god's seat _(actors: Kubera, Vishvakarma)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/kubera.md) — (Ramayana)
- **Kumbhakarṇa's boon of endless sleep** — Sarasvatī twists Kumbhakarṇa's tongue so he asks for sleep instead of a world without gods, dooming him to sleep six months and wake one day _(actors: Kumbhakarṇa, Sarasvatī, Brahmā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ravana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa)
- **Lakṣmaṇa builds the leaf-hut on the Godāvarī** — Lakṣmaṇa raises the parṇa-śālā at Pañcavaṭī where the three settle into forest-domesticity through the seasons _(actors: Lakṣmaṇa, Rāma, Sītā)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/panchavati-nashik-godavari.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 15–16; Nāsik sthala-tradition (Tapovan))
- **Lakṣmaṇa insists on accompanying Rāma into exile** — When Kaikeyī's boons force Rāma into exile, Lakṣmaṇa first urges seizing the throne, then vows to accompany Rāma as servant and guard _(actors: Lakṣmaṇa, Rāma, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/lakshmana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa)
- **Mantharā wakes Kaikeyī and twists the news** — She rouses the delighted Kaikeyī and reframes Rāma's coronation as a catastrophe for her son _(actors: Mantharā, Kaikeyī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/manthara.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 8)
- **Marriage of Śāntā to Ṛśyaśṛṅga** — In gratitude for the rains brought by the horned ascetic, Lomapāda gives the foster-princess Śāntā in marriage to Ṛśyaśṛṅga, making the sage son-in-law of both Aṅga and (through her true parentage) Ayodhyā _(actors: Śāntā, Ṛśyaśṛṅga, Lomapāda, Daśaratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rishyashringa.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana-parva ~113; Rāmāyaṇa Bāla-kāṇḍa ~9–11)
- **Marriage of Satyavatī to Ṛcīka** — Gādhi's daughter Satyavatī (distinct from the Cedi Satyavatī) is given to the Bhārgava sage Ṛcīka, the conduit of kṣatriya nature into the brahmin clan _(actors: Gādhi, Satyavatī, Ṛcīka)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/gadhi-and-the-caru-swap.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 115; Viṣṇu Purāṇa IV.7; Bhāgavata IX.15)
- **Marriage of Ṛśyaśṛṅga and Śāntā** — The grateful Lomapāda weds his daughter Śāntā to Ṛśyaśṛṅga, turning the forest-celibate into a householder _(actors: Ṛśyaśṛṅga, Śāntā, Lomapāda)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rishyashringa.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva ~113)
- **Marriage, Raghu's retirement, birth of Daśaratha** — Aja brings Indumatī home and marries her; Raghu consecrates Aja king and retires to asceticism; Indumatī bears Daśaratha _(actors: Aja, Indumatī, Raghu, Daśaratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/aja-indumati.md) — (Kālidāsa, Raghuvaṃśa cantos 7–8)
- **Matting the hair with banyan-sap (jaṭā-bandhana)** — Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa mat their hair into ascetic jaṭā using the milky sap of the vaṭa/banyan, formally assuming the forest-hermit form for the exile _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/sringaverapura-guha-ganga-crossing.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa 52)
- **Mārīca and Subāhu defile the Siddhāśrama sacrifice** — During Viśvāmitra's six-day sacrificial session the two brothers pour flesh and blood upon the altar, defiling the rite _(actors: Mārīca, Subāhu, Viśvāmitra, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/subahu.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 29–30)
- **Mārīca's second defeat and retreat to asceticism** — Routed a second time by a Rāma-arrow in the forest, the terrified Mārīca renounces demon-life and lives as an abstemious hermit haunted by Rāma's name _(actors: Mārīca, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/maricha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 38-39)
- **Parashurama's twenty-one-fold kshatriya annihilation** — Enraged at his father's murder, Parashurama vows to rid the earth of the kshatriya class twenty-one times, filling five lakes at Samanta-panchaka with their blood _(actors: Parashurama, the kshatriyas)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata Vana/Shanti Parvan; Bhagavata Purana 9.16)
- **Paraśurāma's matricide and restoration boon** — Jamadagni orders each son to behead Reṇukā; all refuse and are cursed except Paraśurāma, who obediently strikes off his mother's head, then uses the boon Jamadagni grants to restore her to life (without memory of her death) and revive his cursed brothers _(actors: Jamadagni, Paraśurāma, Reṇukā, the brothers of Paraśurāma)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/devi-forms-and-navadurga.md) — (Mahābhārata 3.116; Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa)
- **Purchase of Śunaḥśepa as sacrificial substitute** — Rohita buys the middle son Śunaḥśepa from the starving ṛṣi Ajīgarta for a hundred cows to be sacrificed in his place, and Ajīgarta agrees for further cows to bind and butcher his own son _(actors: Rohita, Śunaḥśepa, Ajīgarta)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/hariscandra.md) — (Aitareya Brāhmaṇa 7.16)
- **Purūravas becomes a gandharva by the fire-ritual** — Grieving Purūravas wins the gandharvas' fire-boon, establishes the three sacred fires via the śamī-aśvattha araṇi, and ascends to the gandharva-world; son Āyu continues the line _(actors: Purūravas, Urvaśī, Āyu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/pururavas-urvashi.md) — (Śatapatha Brāhmaṇa XI.5.1; Bhāgavata IX.14)
- **Rāma gifts the vimāna to Vibhīṣaṇa** — After the Laṅkā war Rāma rewards Vibhīṣaṇa with the family deity to carry south to his island kingdom _(actors: Rāma, Vibhīṣaṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/srirangam-ranganatha.md) — (Śrīraṅga-māhātmya; Rāmāyaṇa)
- **Rāma incurs brahma-hatyā by killing Rāvaṇa** — Slaying Rāvaṇa, a Brahmin descended from Brahmā via Pulastya, burdens Rāma with the gravest sin, requiring Śiva-worship at the Setu-kṣetra to expiate _(actors: Rāma, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/rameswaram-setu-bandha.md) — (Rāmanātha sthala-purāṇa; Skanda Purāṇa Setu-māhātmya)
- **Rāma pierces the seven sāla trees** — To prove his prowess to a doubtful Sugrīva, Rāma kicks Dundubhi's skeleton ten yojanas and shoots a single arrow through seven sāla trees _(actors: Rāma, Sugrīva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/4-kishkindha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā Kāṇḍa 11–12)
- **Rāma's Jala-samādhi into the Sarayū** — At the close of his reign Rama makes his final departure by walking into the Sarayu river, the anchoring event of Ayodhya's sanctity _(actors: Rama)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/sapta-puri-and-sacred-cities.md) — (Garuda Purana; Valmiki Ramayana)
- **Rāma slays Tāṭakā and guards Viśvāmitra's sacrifice** — Sage Viśvāmitra takes the young Rāma to kill the demoness Tāṭakā and protect the sages' sacrifices _(actors: Rāma, Viśvāmitra, Tāṭakā)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa 1 (Bāla Kāṇḍa))
- **Rāma vows to clear Daṇḍaka of demons** — Shown the bones of ascetics devoured by rākṣasas, Rāma vows to rid the Daṇḍaka forest of demons, the dharmic pledge driving the kāṇḍa _(actors: Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/3-aranya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya Kāṇḍa 6)
- **Rohita defers and flees the promised sacrifice** — Hariscandra repeatedly postpones the offering; the grown Rohita refuses and flees to the forest as Varuna afflicts the king with dropsy _(actors: Hariscandra, Rohita, Varuna)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/vedas/brahmanas/aitareya-brahmana.md) — (Aitareya Brahmana 7.14)
- **Rāvaṇa's roar and naming** — Trapped in agony, Rāvaṇa roars so terribly that Śiva gives him the name 'Rāvaṇa' (maker of roars) _(actors: Śiva, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/shiva-tandava-stotra.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Uttarakāṇḍa)
- **Rāvaṇa's wooing and Sītā's refusal** — Rāvaṇa courts Sītā in the grove with threats and blandishments; she repels him with a blade of grass and he sets a death-deadline _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Sītā, rākṣasīs)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/lanka-ramayana-trail-ashoka-vatika.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 18–23)
- **Sagara's horse-sacrifice and Indra's theft** — Emperor Sagara performs an Ashvamedha; Indra, fearing his power, steals the sacrificial horse and hides it at sage Kapila's hermitage in Patala _(actors: Sagara, Indra, Kapila)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/ganga.md) — (Ramayana, Bala Kanda 38-40)
- **Sita and Rama bless Hanuman with deathlessness** — Sita blesses Hanuman with deathlessness and eternal youth and Rama decrees he shall live as long as the Rama-katha is told on earth _(actors: Sita, Rama, Hanuman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/the-chiranjivis.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana)
- **Sita's Agni-pariksha and Return to the Earth** — Sita, daughter of the earth and King Janaka, abducted by Ravana, undergoes the fire-ordeal and finally returns into the earth _(actors: Sita, Rama, Janaka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/README.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana)
- **Sita's curse on the Phalgu river** — A Ramayana-linked legend holds Sita cursed the Phalgu river at Gaya to run underground, a story tied to the ancestral pinda-offering circuit _(actors: Sita)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/shraddha-and-pitr-rites.md) — (Ramayana-linked tradition)
- **Slaying of Viradha** — The rakshasa Viradha seizes Sita; the brothers bury him alive, releasing him from a gandharva curse _(actors: Viradha, Rama, Lakshmana, Sita)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Aranya-kanda 2-4)
- **Sītā insists on accompanying Rāma into exile** — When Kaikeyī's boon sends Rāma into forest exile, Sītā refuses palace comfort and insists a wife's place is at her husband's side _(actors: Sītā, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sita.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa)
- **Sugrīva sights the brothers from Mount Ṛṣyamūka** — From Ṛṣyamūka — the one hill Vāli cannot enter due to Mataṅga's curse — the exiled Sugrīva spots two armed strangers and, fearing Vāli, sends Hanumān to investigate _(actors: Sugrīva, Hanumān, (Vāli, sage Mataṅga))_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kishkindha-hampi-anegundi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa 2–3)
- **Teaching of the Āditya-hṛdaya** — Agastya imparts the Sun-hymn as upadeśa, directing Rāma to worship Sūrya/Āditya and recite it thrice with a purified mind _(actors: Agastya, Rāma, Sūrya (Āditya))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/aditya-hridayam.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 105.3-26)
- **The Śambara war and origin of the two boons** — Kaikeyī rescues the wounded Daśaratha in the war against the asura Śambara and is granted two boons on demand _(actors: Kaikeyī, Daśaratha, Śambara, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kaikeyi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā 9; Pauranic, Telugu Dvipada, Kṛttivāsī Rāmāyaṇ variants)
- **The arms are severed** — Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa simultaneously hack off Kabandha's two arms at the shoulder, felling the monster _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Kabandha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kabandha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 69-70)
- **The brahmadaṇḍa swallows Kauśika's astras** — Kauśika hurls Śiva-given astras including the brahmāstra at the hermitage, but Vasiṣṭha's brahmin staff swallows them all, teaching him brahman-strength is supreme _(actors: Kauśika, Vasiṣṭha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vishwamitra-vasishtha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 55-56)
- **The cave, the sealing, and Vālī's usurpation** — Pursuing Māyāvī into a cave, Vālī is sealed in by Sugrīva who believes him dead; returning enraged, Vālī banishes Sugrīva, seizes his kingdom, and takes his wife Rumā _(actors: Vālī, Sugrīva, Māyāvī, Rumā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sugriva-and-vali.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa)
- **The clinging skull falls at Kapālamocana** — A skull-fragment of a rākṣasa, struck off by Rāma Dāśarathi's arrow and fastened to the thigh of the sage Mahodara, falls away when he bathes at Kapālamocana, freeing him and giving the ford power to absolve even brahminicide _(actors: Rāma (Dāśarathi), the sage Mahodara, a rākṣasa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kurukshetra-dharmakshetra.md) — (Mahābhārata, Vana-parva (Tīrtha-yātrā); Śalya-parva 38–39)
- **The Dhanuṣa-yajña — Rāma breaks Śiva's bow (Pināka)** — Rāma lifts and strings the colossal Śiva-dhanus (Pināka), snapping it with a thunderclap, and so wins Sītā's hand as the vīrya-śulka bride-price of valour _(actors: Rāma, Sītā, Janaka, Viśvāmitra)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/janakpur-mithila-sita-janmabhumi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla-kāṇḍa 66–67; Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa (Varuṇa-bow variant); Tulsīdās Rāmcaritmānas, Bāla-kāṇḍa (svayaṃvara/Phulwārī expansion))
- **The Dhanuṣa-yajña vow** — Janaka pledges Sītā as vīrya-śulka to any king who can string the colossal bow of Śiva (Pināka) held by his house _(actors: Janaka, the suitor-kings)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/janakpur-mithila-sita-janmabhumi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla-kāṇḍa 66-67)
- **The 'do not over-question' warning** — When Gārgī presses on what the worlds of Brahman are woven, Yājñavalkya warns her not to over-question (atipraśna) lest her head fall off; she falls silent for the moment _(actors: Yājñavalkya, Gārgī Vācaknavī)_ → [details](../07-acharyas-and-sampradayas/gargi-and-maitreyi.md) — (Bṛhadāraṇyaka Upaniṣad 3.6)
- **The gods appeal to Viṣṇu and Gaṇeśa** — Alarmed at an invincible Rāvaṇa, the devas beg Viṣṇu and Gaṇeśa to stop the liṅga reaching Laṅkā _(actors: Devas, Viṣṇu, Gaṇeśa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/gokarna-mahabaleshwar-atma-linga.md) — (Gokarṇa-māhātmya; Sahyādri tradition)
- **The gods revive Hanuman and grant boons** — After Vayu withdraws all air in wrath, the devas revive Hanuman and each grants a boon of invulnerability, strength, and longevity, making him effectively immortal _(actors: Hanuman, Vayu, Indra, Brahma, the devas)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/hanuman.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Uttara Kanda 36; Shiva Purana)
- **The leaf-hut on the Godāvarī** — Lakṣmaṇa builds the parṇa-śālā and the three settle into forest life at Pañcavaṭī _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Sītā)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/panchavati-nashik-godavari.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 15–16)
- **The qualified seeker (sādhana-catuṣṭaya)** — Rāma describes the adhikārin endowed with discrimination, dispassion, the six virtues, and longing for release, fit for brahma-vidyā _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rama-gita.md) — (Adhyātma Rāmāyaṇa 7.5)
- **The twenty-one-day service of Nandinī** — Laying aside royal state, Dilīpa tends the wish-cow Nandinī as a herdsman for twenty-one days while Sudakṣiṇā worships her morning and evening _(actors: Dilīpa, Sudakṣiṇā, Nandinī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/dilipa-nandini.md) — (Raghuvaṃśa 1.89–95, 2.1–25 (Kālidāsa))
- **The wish-cow at the āśrama** — Jamadagni's hermitage holds an all-yielding wish-cow (Kāmadhenu/Surabhi/Suśīlā/Nandinī) furnishing endless food and hospitality, the engine of the coming tragedy _(actors: Jamadagni, Kāmadhenu/Surabhi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/jamadagni-parashurama.md) — (Mahābhārata 3.116; Bhāgavata Purāṇa 9.15)
- **Transmission of the Nāṭyaveda to Bharata Muni** — Brahmā confers the Nāṭyaveda on Bharata Muni, who teaches it to his hundred sons with Apsarās and Gandharvas _(actors: Brahmā, Bharata Muni, his hundred sons, Apsarās, Gandharvas)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/natya-shastra-and-the-classical-arts.md) — (Nāṭya Śāstra ch. 1)
- **Triśaṅku's wish and Vasiṣṭha's refusal** — King Triśaṅku asks to ascend bodily to heaven; Vasiṣṭha refuses as adharma, and the sage's sons curse him to become a cāṇḍāla _(actors: Triśaṅku, Vasiṣṭha, Vasiṣṭha's sons)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/trishanku.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 57-59)
- **Twenty-one-fold kshatriya annihilation** — Enraged at his father's murder, Parashurama rids the earth of the kshatriya class twenty-one times, filling lakes at Kurukshetra _(actors: Parashurama)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata, Bhagavata Purana)
- **Uprooting of Mount Kailāsa** — Enraged Rāvaṇa thrusts his twenty arms under Kailāsa and heaves it up, shaking the mountain so Pārvatī clings to Śiva in alarm _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Pārvatī, Śiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ravana-kailasa-ravananugraha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttarakāṇḍa 16)
- **Urvaśī cursed to dwell among mortals** — The apsarā Urvaśī is sent to earth by a curse (of Mitra or Mitra-Varuṇa), occasioning her marriage to Purūravas _(actors: Urvaśī, Mitra, Varuṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/pururavas-urvashi.md) — (Vishnu Purana IV.6; Bhagavata Purana IX.14)
- **Vasishtha refuses the bodily-ascent wish** — Trishanku desires to ascend to Indra's heaven in his living body, but his purohita Vasishtha refuses the rite as impossible and against dharma _(actors: Trishanku, Vasishtha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/trishanku.md) — (Ramayana Bala Kanda 57)
- **Vibhīṣaṇa's śaraṇāgati and refuge with Rāma** — Rejected by Rāvaṇa, Vibhīṣaṇa surrenders to Rāma, who vows to never reject one who seeks refuge and at once consecrates him future king of Laṅkā _(actors: Vibhīṣaṇa, Rāma, Hanumān, Sugrīva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vibhishana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 17-19)
- **Viśvāmitra recites his Kuśika origin at the Śoṇa** — Camped on the Śoṇa en route to Mithilā, Viśvāmitra tells Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa the history of his ancestors, closing with "I am Kauśika, born in the line of Kuśa" _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/kushanabha-daughters-and-brahmadatta.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 31–34)
- **Viśvāmitra rises from kṣatriya to brāhmaṇa by tapas** — The kṣatriya sage Viśvāmitra attains brahminhood through austerity, adduced by Bhīṣma to show varṇa can be transcended by conduct _(actors: Viśvāmitra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/13-anushasana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 13)
- **Vālmīki shelters Sītā** — The sage Vālmīki, knowing Sītā by ascetic insight, takes the abandoned queen into his āśrama _(actors: Vālmīki, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttara-ramayana-lava-kusha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Uttara Kāṇḍa 49)
- **Śabarī receives Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa** — At last seeing Rāma, Śabarī rises in joy, touches their feet, and offers hospitality, her lifelong wait fulfilled _(actors: Śabarī, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shabari.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Araṇya-kāṇḍa 74)
- **Adhyātma Rāmāyaṇa — Rāma as Brahman with Māyā-Sītā** — The embedded Advaita Rāmāyaṇa retells Rāma's life as supreme Brahman with Sītā as Yoga-Māyā; a shadow Māyā-Sītā is abducted by Rāvaṇa while the real Sītā is hidden in fire _(actors: Rāma, Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/brahmanda-purana.md) — (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa (Adhyātma Rāmāyaṇa))
- **Agastya bestows the bow of Viṣṇu on Rāma** — The great sage Agastya gives Rāma the Viṣṇu-bow, inexhaustible quivers, a sword, and a divine arrow, and directs him to settle at Pañcavaṭī _(actors: Agastya, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/3-aranya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya Kāṇḍa 12–13)
- **Agastya digests the asura Vātāpi** — Agastya eats the goat-disguised Vātāpi and commands 'Be digested', dissolving the cannibal demon, then reduces his brother Ilvala to ash _(actors: Agastya, Vātāpi, Ilvala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/agastya.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva)
- **Ajigarta sells his middle son Sunahshepa** — Rohita buys the starving rishi Ajigarta's middle son Sunahshepa for a hundred cows to die in his place as the sacrificial victim _(actors: Rohita, Ajigarta, Sunahshepa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sunahshepa.md) — (Aitareya Brahmana 7.15)
- **Anaraṇya's dying curse foretelling Rāma** — The dying king replies that his defeat is fate's doing, then curses Rāvaṇa that a future king of his own Ikṣvāku line (Rāma) will slay him; the gods affirm the decree _(actors: Anaraṇya, Rāvaṇa, the gods)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/anaranya-ravana-curse.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara Kāṇḍa 19)
- **Anasūyā gifts Sītā celestial garments** — Visiting sages' hermitages during the forest exile, Sītā receives celestial garments and unguents from Anasūyā at Atri's āśrama _(actors: Sītā, Anasūyā, Atri)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sita.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā/Araṇya Kāṇḍa)
- **Bali's Aśvamedha and the Boon of Three Paces** — At Bali's horse-sacrifice on the Narmadā, Vāmana asks only for three paces of land measured by his own steps, declining all greater gifts _(actors: Vāmana, Bali, Bhṛgu priests)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vamana-bali.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 8.18–8.19)
- **Ballal's Devotion and the Boon at Pali** — In the Treta Yuga the boy Ballal, beaten and bound by his father for worshipping a stone as Ganesha, is freed and blessed by Ganesha, who grants that his devotee's name precede the Lord's as Ballaleshwar _(actors: Ballal, Kalyan-seth, Ganesha)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/ashtavinayaka.md) — (Ganesha Purana)
- **Beheading and restoration of Reṇukā** — At Jamadagni's command Paraśurāma beheads his mother Reṇukā for a momentary lapse of mind, then asks as a boon that she be restored to life and his brothers' wits returned _(actors: Paraśurāma, Reṇukā, Jamadagni, Citraratha (Gandharva))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/parashurama.md) — (Mahābhārata 3.116; Bhāgavata Purāṇa 9.16; Brahmāṇḍa & Padma Purāṇas; Reṇukā-Yellammā folk variant)
