- **Bharata's appeal at Citrakūṭa** — Bharata leads the court to Citrakūṭa to beg Rāma to return and rule; Rāma refuses, bound by his vow and his father's word _(actors: Bharata, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Sītā, the queens and ministers)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bharata-shatrughna-and-their-wives.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa)
- **Coronation proclaimed and Kausalyā's joy** — Rāma's consecration as yuvarāja is announced and Kausalyā prepares thanksgiving worship _(actors: Kausalyā, Rāma, Daśaratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kausalya.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 20)
- **Courtesans lure Rishyashringa to Anga and the rains fall** — Courtesans on a floating hermitage-barge approach the innocent Rishyashringa as fellow ascetics and entice him to Anga; the moment he enters, Indra sends torrential rain _(actors: Rishyashringa, the courtesans, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rishyashringa.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana-parva ~111-112)
- **Devastation of the Malada–Karūṣa country** — Enraged by her transformation, Tāṭakā lays waste the once-fertile Malada and Karūṣa lands near Agastya's grove into a shunned wasteland _(actors: Tāṭakā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/tataka.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 24-25)
- **Digvijaya — four-quarter world-conquest** — Raghu conquers the kings of all four directions (east, south, west, north) following the freed Ashvamedha horse and returns to Ayodhya laden with tribute _(actors: Raghu, Pandya/Kerala/Parasika/Yavana/Huna/Kamboja kings)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/raghu.md) — (Raghuvamsha 4.24-88)
- **Exposition of the mahāvākya 'tat tvam asi'** — Rāma resolves jīva and Īśvara into the one undivided Brahman, teaching that bondage is avidyā and liberation is direct knowledge of identity _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rama-gita.md) — (Adhyātma Rāmāyaṇa 7.5)
- **First drama staged at Indra's Banner Festival and disrupted by Asuras** — The inaugural play on the gods' victory over the Asuras is staged at the Dhvaja-mahā; the Asuras disrupt it, prompting Brahmā to ordain the protected stage _(actors: Bharata, Indra, Devas, Asuras, Brahmā)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/natya-shastra-and-the-classical-arts.md) — (Nāṭya Śāstra chs. 1, 3, 5)
- **Gārgī's two final arrows and the akṣara teaching** — Gārgī returns with two whetted-arrow questions; Yājñavalkya answers that all is woven on ākāśa and ākāśa on the Imperishable (akṣara), describing the akṣara by negation as the unseen seer and unknown knower _(actors: Gārgī Vācaknavī, Yājñavalkya, the assembled Brahmins)_ → [details](../07-acharyas-and-sampradayas/gargi-and-maitreyi.md) — (Bṛhadāraṇyaka Upaniṣad 3.8)
- **Hanuman Leaps to Lanka** — The vanara minister Hanuman, son of Vayu, leaps across the ocean to Lanka in the Sundara Kanda _(actors: Hanuman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/README.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Sundara Kanda)
- **Hanuman struck by Indra's vajra and granted boons as a child** — As a child Hanuman is struck down by Indra's vajra for leaping at the sun, and the devas each grant boons making him invulnerable _(actors: Hanuman, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/the-chiranjivis.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana)
- **Hanumān fells Laṅkinī at the gate** — The city's guardian goddess Laṅkinī challenges Hanumān; he fells her with a single blow, and she recalls the prophecy that her defeat means the rākṣasas' doom _(actors: Hanumān, Laṅkinī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/5-sundara-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 3)
- **Hanumān's embassy and meeting with Rāma** — Hanumān approaches disguised as a brāhmaṇa mendicant, wins Rāma's trust with eloquent speech, and carries both princes on his shoulders to Sugrīva — his entry into the epic's main action _(actors: Hanumān, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Sugrīva)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kishkindha-hampi-anegundi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa 3–4)
- **Hanumān sent to Kailāsa for a liṅga** — Rāma dispatches Hanumān to Kailāsa (or Vārāṇasī) to fetch a Śiva-liṅga, but he is delayed past the installation muhūrta _(actors: Rāma, Hanumān)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/rameswaram-setu-bandha.md) — (Rāmanātha sthala-purāṇa; Skanda Purāṇa Setu-māhātmya (post-Vālmīki))
- **Indrajit binds Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa with nāga-arrows** — Fighting invisibly, Indrajit binds Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa with serpent-arrows so that both fall as if slain, until Garuḍa descends and dissolves the bonds _(actors: Indrajit, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Garuḍa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 44–50)
- **Indra seduces Ahalyā in Gautama's form** — Indra disguises himself as Gautama and approaches Ahalyā; in the Vālmīki critical reading she knowingly yields, while Purāṇic/devotional versions make her wholly deceived and innocent _(actors: Indra, Ahalyā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ahalya.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 48; Brahma Purāṇa; Adhyātma Rāmāyaṇa; Rāmcaritmānas; Brahmavaivarta Purāṇa)
- **Instruction to recite thrice facing the Sun** — Agastya bids Rāma purify himself, face the rising Sun, and recite the hymn three times to gain victory _(actors: Agastya, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/aditya-hridayam.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha-kāṇḍa 105.23–25)
- **Śiva pins the mountain with a toe** — Śiva presses Kailāsa back down with the great toe of one foot, crushing Rāvaṇa's arms beneath its weight _(actors: Śiva, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ravana-kailasa-ravananugraha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttarakāṇḍa 16)
- **Jatayu's Cremation at Tirupullam Bhoothankudi** — The Ramayana site where Valvil Raman (Rama) performs the cremation of the vulture Jatayu, slain by Ravana while defending Sita _(actors: Rama, Jatayu)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/108-divya-desams.md) — (Ramayana / sthala-purana)
- **Kabandha recognizes Rāma and tells his story** — Learning he is held by Rāma, the maimed Kabandha rejoices, narrates his curse, and asks to be cremated so the curse may be discharged _(actors: Kabandha, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kabandha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 70-71)
- **Kaikeyi's Two Boons and Rama's Exile** — At Ayodhya Kaikeyi invokes her two boons, sending Rama into 14-year exile while Bharata rules as regent from Nandigrama with Rama's sandals on the throne _(actors: Kaikeyi, Rama, Bharata)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/ayodhya.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Ayodhya-kanda)
- **Kapila burns Sagara's 60,000 sons to ash** — Sagara's 60,000 sons accuse the meditating Kapila of the theft, and he incinerates all of them to ash with his ascetic fire _(actors: Kapila, the sons of Sagara)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/ganga.md) — (Ramayana, Bala Kanda 40-41)
- **Kauśika renounces kingship for tapas** — Humiliated, Kauśika abandons his kingdom to pursue tapas, the hinge that launches his multi-life quest for brahmarṣi status _(actors: Viśvāmitra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vishwamitra-vasishtha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 56)
- **Lakshmi incarnates as Sita with Rama** — When Vishnu descends as Rama, Lakshmi incarnates as Sita, born of the earth-furrow, his consort _(actors: Lakshmi (Sita), Rama)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/lakshmi.md) — (Vishnu Purana 1.9; Ramayana)
- **Marriage of Purūravas and Urvaśī under three conditions** — Urvaśī weds Purūravas on conditions (protect her two rams, never be seen naked, fed only ghee), the kernel of the oldest narrated Indian romance _(actors: Purūravas, Urvaśī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/pururavas-urvashi.md) — (Rgveda X.95; Shatapatha Brahmana XI.5.1; Vishnu Purana IV.6)
- **Mārīca hurled a hundred yojanas by the Mānavāstra** — Rāma looses the Mānavāstra at Mārīca, flinging him a hundred yojanas into the ocean without killing him _(actors: Rāma, Mārīca)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/subahu.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 30)
- **Parashurama beheads and restores Renuka** — At Jamadagni's command Parashurama beheads his own mother Renuka for a lapse of mind, then asks a boon to restore her to life _(actors: Parashurama, Renuka, Jamadagni)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata; Bhagavata Purana)
- **Paraśurāma beheads Reṇukā** — In filial obedience Paraśurāma strikes off his mother's head with the axe at one stroke _(actors: Paraśurāma, Reṇukā)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/renuka-yellamma.md) — (Mahābhārata 3.116)
- **Phra Ram orders Sida's death over a drawn portrait** — In Rama I's Ramakien a rakshasa tricks Sida into drawing Tosakanth's portrait, triggering Phra Ram's suspicion and an order for her death, which he later repents _(actors: Phra Ram, Nang Sida, a rakshasa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/recensions/southeast-asian-ramayanas.md) — (Ramakien)
- **Rama Installs the Rameswaram Linga** — Rama installs the Ramanatha linga at Rameswaram to expiate the sin of killing the brahmin Ravana, making it both a Char Dham point and a Jyotirlinga _(actors: Rama)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/char-dham-and-chota-char-dham.md) — (Skanda Purana; Ramayana tradition)
- **Rama Protects Vishvamitra's Yajna** — The boy-sages Rama and Lakshmana guard Vishvamitra's sacrifice, slaying the rakshasas who would defile it _(actors: Rama, Lakshmana, Vishvamitra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/README.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Bala Kanda)
- **Rama's Ashvamedha, Lava-Kusha, and Sita's earth-return** — The Padma Purana's Pataala-khanda Rama narration frames Rama-Sita as Vishnu-Lakshmi and recounts Rama's Ashvamedha, the Lava-Kusha episode, and Sita's return to the earth _(actors: Rama, Sita, Lava, Kusha)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/padma-purana.md) — (Padma Purana (Pataala Khanda))
- **Ravana usurps Lanka from Kubera** — After his own austerities and boons, Ravana drives his half-brother Kubera out and seizes Lanka, making it the rakshasa capital _(actors: Ravana, Kubera)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/kubera.md) — (Ramayana)
- **Release of the gandharva Tumburu** — Dying, Virādha reveals he is the gandharva Tumburu cursed by Kubera; freed by Rāma's act, he rises restored and ascends, directing Rāma onward to sage Śarabhaṅga _(actors: Virādha/Tumburu, Kubera (curse), Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/viradha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 4-5)
- **Renuka's beheading and restoration** — At Jamadagni's command Parashurama beheads his own mother Renuka, then wins a boon to restore her to life _(actors: Parashurama, Renuka, Jamadagni)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Mahabharata, Bhagavata Purana)
- **Reṇukā's lapse of purity** — Sent to the river, Reṇukā's mind strays at the sight of the Gandharva Citraratha sporting with apsarases; her yogic purity breaks and Jamadagni perceives it _(actors: Reṇukā, Citraratha/Citrasena, Jamadagni)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/jamadagni-parashurama.md) — (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa; Padma Purāṇa; cf. Mahābhārata 3.116)
- **Rāma accepts exile** — Bound by his father's word as dharma, Rāma serenely accepts the fourteen-year exile without anger toward Kaikeyī or Daśaratha _(actors: Rāma, Kaikeyī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/2-ayodhya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 16–19)
- **Rāma breaks Śiva's bow** — Brought by Viśvāmitra, Rāma lifts and bends the Pināka to string it; the bow snaps with a thunderclap and Sītā is won _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Viśvāmitra, Janaka, Śatānanda)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/janakpur-mithila-sita-janmabhumi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla-kāṇḍa 67; Rāmcaritmānas Bāla-kāṇḍa)
- **Rāma slays Vāli** — During Sugrīva's rematch with Vāli, Rāma shoots Vāli from concealment; the dying Vāli rebukes Rāma's stealth, hears his tenfold justification, and commends Aṅgada and Tārā to his care _(actors: Rāma, Vāli, Sugrīva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/4-kishkindha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā Kāṇḍa 16–18)
- **Rāma's Mahā-prasthāna and procession to the Sarayū** — Having installed Kuśa and Lava and lost Lakṣmaṇa, Rāma walks out of Ayodhyā in solemn procession with priests, women, ministers and citizens to the Sarayū bank as the gods watch _(actors: Rāma, Kuśa, Lava, Brahmā and the gods, the people of Ayodhyā)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/sarayu-ayodhya-river.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa)
- **Rāma's question on suffering and the highest good** — Rāma asks why the righteous suffer and what the highest good (śreyas) is — the Śaiva mirror of Arjuna's despondency _(actors: Rāma, Śiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shiva-gita.md) — (Padma Purāṇa, Śiva-Gītā 3)
- **Rāma's threefold recitation of the hymn** — Rāma sips water, gazes at the sun, and recites the Āditya-hṛdaya three times, becoming renewed and joyful _(actors: Rāma, Sūrya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/aditya-hridayam.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 105.27-30)
- **Rāma's Visit to the Prayāga Akṣayavaṭ** — Rama, Sita and Lakshmana visit the imperishable banyan at Prayaga during their exile, near sage Bharadvaja's ashram _(actors: Rama, Sita, Lakshmana)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/prayagraj-triveni-sangam.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Ayodhya-kanda))
- **Śūrpaṇakhā's suit to Rāma** — The rākṣasī Śūrpaṇakhā, smitten with Rāma, demands him as husband and is deflected toward Lakṣmaṇa _(actors: Śūrpaṇakhā, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/panchavati-nashik-godavari.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 17)
- **Rāvaṇa coerces Mārīca and Mārīca counsels against the abduction** — Rāvaṇa demands Mārīca become a golden deer; Mārīca twice warns him that Rāma is invincible and begs him to spare Sītā _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Mārīca)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/maricha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 38-40)
- **Rāvaṇa seizes Laṅkā from Kubera and takes the Puṣpaka** — Rāvaṇa defeats and expels his half-brother Kubera from golden Laṅkā and carries off the flying chariot Puṣpaka as his signature vehicle _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Kubera)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ravana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa)
- **Sages curse Hanuman to forget his powers** — For tormenting sages as a child, Hanuman is cursed to forget his own immense powers until reminded at the destined moment _(actors: Hanuman, the sages)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/hanuman.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Kishkindha/Sundara Kanda)
- **Sage Sharabhanga's self-immolation** — Sharabhanga, having awaited Rama's darshan, immolates himself in his sacrificial fire and ascends to Brahmaloka _(actors: Sharabhanga, Rama)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Aranya-kanda 5)
- **Shunahshepa bought as substitute victim** — Rohita buys the rishi Ajigarta's middle son Shunahshepa for a hundred cows to serve as substitute victim in the rajasuya sacrifice _(actors: Rohita, Ajigarta Sauyavasi, Shunahshepa)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/vedas/brahmanas/aitareya-brahmana.md) — (Aitareya Brahmana 7.15)
- **Soorasamharam (Skanda Shashti)** — The climax of the six-day Aippasi Skanda Shashti fast: Murugan slays the asura Surapadma with the Vel, splitting him in two so that one half becomes his peacock mount and the other his cock-banner; re-enacted on the Tiruchendur seashore, followed the next day by Murugan's wedding to Deivanai (Tirukalyanam) _(actors: Murugan (Skanda/Kartikeya), Surapadma (Soorapadman), Deivanai)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/regional-temple-festivals.md) — (Wikipedia (Skanda Sashti, Soorasamharam); Tiruchendur Murugan Temple [traditional])
- **Sumitrā's farewell counsel to Lakṣmaṇa** — As Lakṣmaṇa departs for the forest exile, Sumitrā blesses him, bidding him treat Rāma as father, Sītā as mother, and the forest as Ayodhyā, and to serve without faltering _(actors: Sumitrā, Lakṣmaṇa, Rāma, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sumitra.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa; Tulsīdās, Rāmcaritmānas, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa)
- **Sunaḥśepa freed by his hymns** — Bound to the stake, Sunaḥśepa prays in Ṛgvedic verses to Varuṇa, Uṣas and others; his bonds fall away and Hariścandra is cured of dropsy _(actors: Sunaḥśepa, Varuṇa, Uṣas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sunahshepa.md) — (Aitareya Brāhmaṇa 7.16-18; Ṛgveda 1.24-30)
- **The agni-sākṣi pact of Rāma and Sugrīva** — With fire as witness Rāma swears to kill Vālī and restore Sugrīva's throne, while Sugrīva vows to marshal the vānaras to find Sītā _(actors: Rāma, Sugrīva, Hanumān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sugriva-and-vali.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa)
- **The Bharata-milāp (reunion of the brothers)** — Rāma and Bharata meet in grief and love and debate kingship and dharma before the assembled sages; Rāma refuses to cut short the exile _(actors: Rāma, Bharata, Lakṣmaṇa, Vasiṣṭha, the sages)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/chitrakoot-kamadgiri.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa 100–111)
- **The boon of sleeplessness and Ūrmilā-nidrā** — Lakṣmaṇa begs the goddess Nidrā to stay awake for the fourteen years; she consents on condition someone sleep his share, and Ūrmilā sleeps continuously throughout _(actors: Lakṣmaṇa, Nidrā, Ūrmilā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/lakshmana.md) — (folk/devotional; Gupta's Sāket)
- **The dead son and the cremation-fee test** — Rohitāśva dies of snakebite; at the pyre Hariścandra, bound by duty, demands the cremation fee from his own grieving wife _(actors: Hariścandra, Śaivyā, Rohitāśva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/hariscandra.md) — (Mārkaṇḍeya Purāṇa 8)
- **The four-fold wedding at Mithilā (Vivāha Pañcamī)** — Daśaratha arrives with Vasiṣṭha; the Ikṣvāku and Videha genealogies are recited and the four sons of Daśaratha wed Sītā, Ūrmilā, Māṇḍavī and Śrutakīrti in one ceremony on Mārgaśīrṣa Śukla Pañcamī _(actors: Rāma–Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa–Ūrmilā, Bharata–Māṇḍavī, Śatrughna–Śrutakīrti, Daśaratha, Janaka, Kuśadhvaja, Vasiṣṭha)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/janakpur-mithila-sita-janmabhumi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla-kāṇḍa 70–73; Maithilī Vivāha Pañcamī tradition)
- **The Gaṅgā crossing and the Kevaṭ episode** — Guha summons a boat and his kinsmen ferry Rāma, Sītā and Lakṣmaṇa across the Gaṅgā; in Tulsīdās the boatman (Kevaṭ) first washes Rāma's feet before launching. Sītā prays to Gaṅgā and Sumantra is sent back _(actors: Rāma, Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa, Guha, the Kevaṭ (boatman), Sumantra, Gaṅgā)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/sringaverapura-guha-ganga-crossing.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa 52; Rāmcaritmānas, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa, Kevaṭ-prasaṅg)
- **The Kevat foot-washing episode (Tulsidas)** — In the Ramcharitmanas the boatman refuses to row Rama until allowed to wash his feet, then ferries them and asks only for devotion as payment _(actors: Kevat/Guha, Rama, Sita, Lakshmana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/guha.md) — (Ramcharitmanas, Ayodhya-kand)
- **The lion-test and Dilīpa's offer of his own body** — On the final day a lion (Śiva's servant) seizes Nandinī and freezes the king's arm; Dilīpa offers his own flesh in the cow's place, whereupon the illusion dissolves and Nandinī, satisfied, grants the boon of a son _(actors: Dilīpa, Nandinī, the lion (illusion), Śiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/dilipa-nandini.md) — (Raghuvaṃśa 2.26–75 (Kālidāsa))
- **The poisoning of Kaikeyī's mind** — Mantharā plays on jealousy and maternal fear, arguing Bharata must be enthroned and Rāma removed _(actors: Mantharā, Kaikeyī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/manthara.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 8–9)
- **The Putrakāmeṣṭi and birth of Rāma and his brothers** — Ṛśyaśṛṅga performs Daśaratha's son-desiring sacrifice; the divine pāyasa given to the queens produces Rāma, Bharata, Lakṣmaṇa and Śatrughna _(actors: Ṛśyaśṛṅga, Daśaratha, the queens, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rishyashringa.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa ~13-18)
- **The Putrakāmeṣṭi with Ṛṣyaśṛṅga and the divine pāyasa** — Childless Daśaratha performs the Aśvamedha and son-seeking Putrakāmeṣṭi under Ṛṣyaśṛṅga, receiving divine pāyasa carrying Viṣṇu's descending portions _(actors: Daśaratha, Ṛṣyaśṛṅga, Śāntā, Vasiṣṭha, the Prājāpatya being)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dasharatha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla 8–16)
- **The swapped outcaste body — Reṇukā/Yellammā split etiology** — In the Deccan version Paraśurāma mismatches the heads while restoring his mother, joining Reṇukā's head to an outcaste (Mātaṅgī/Cāṇḍāla) woman's body and vice versa, producing the two-natured goddess worshipped as brāhmaṇ-headed Reṇukā by upper castes and outcaste-bodied Yellammā/Māriyammaṉ by lower castes _(actors: Paraśurāma, Reṇukā, the outcaste (Mātaṅgī/Cāṇḍāla) woman)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/devi-forms-and-navadurga.md) — (South Indian folk variants; oral Yellamma/Mariyamman traditions)
- **The thousand-horse bride-price** — Ṛcīka wins Satyavatī by furnishing Gādhi a thousand white horses each with one black ear, obtained from Varuṇa _(actors: Ṛcīka, Gādhi, Varuṇa)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/gadhi-and-the-caru-swap.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 115; Viṣṇu Purāṇa IV.7)
- **The vīṇā strung from his own head** — In the Tamil Śaiva variant, Rāvaṇa tears off a head and strings a vīṇā from it with his own sinews to accompany the hymn _(actors: Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/shiva-tandava-stotra.md) — (Tamil Śaiva / Tevāram-associated tradition [regional][folk])
- **Trijaṭā restrains the rākṣasīs** — On the strength of the dream Trijaṭā forbids the demonesses to harm Sītā and urges them to seek her favour _(actors: Trijaṭā, guard rākṣasīs, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/trijata.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 27)
- **Trijaṭā's omen-dream** — The aged rākṣasī Trijaṭā restrains the others, recounting her dream of Rāma's victory and Laṅkā's doom _(actors: Trijaṭā, Sītā, rākṣasīs)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/lanka-ramayana-trail-ashoka-vatika.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 27)
- **Trivikrama Measures the Worlds (Ulagaḷanta form)** — Viṣṇu as Vāmana/Trivikrama strides the cosmos with raised foot, the deed enshrined in the Ulagaḷanta Perumāḷ temple of Kāñcī _(actors: Viṣṇu (Vāmana/Trivikrama))_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kanchipuram-city-of-temples.md) — (divyadesam.com)
- **Turned back at the Gaṅgā at Śṛṅgaverapura** — At the riverbank Rāma dismisses the grieving Sumantra with the empty chariot while Guha ferries the exiles across the Gaṅgā into the forest _(actors: Sumantra, Rāma, Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa, Guha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sumantra.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa 50–52)
- **Śunaḥśepa's self-rescue by hymn** — Bound to the stake, Śunaḥśepa prays in turn to the gods and his bonds fall away of themselves while Hariścandra is simultaneously cured of his illness _(actors: Śunaḥśepa, Hariścandra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/hariscandra.md) — (Aitareya Brāhmaṇa 7.16–17)
- **Vasishtha's sons curse Trishanku to chandala** — Insulted that he spurned their father, Vasishtha's hundred sons curse Trishanku to become a chandala and his form is instantly disfigured _(actors: Trishanku, Vasishtha's sons)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/trishanku.md) — (Ramayana Bala Kanda 58-59)
- **Vibhīṣaṇa reveals the secret of Rāvaṇa's death** — When Rāma's arrows fail, Vibhīṣaṇa discloses that amṛta stored in Rāvaṇa's navel must be dried up, prompting Rāma to loose the brahmāstra there _(actors: Vibhīṣaṇa, Rāma, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vibhishana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa)
- **Virādha revealed as the cursed gandharva Tumburu** — The Daṇḍaka rākṣasa Virādha, slain and buried by Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa, reveals at death that he was the gandharva Tumburu cursed by Kubera, and his death releases him back to heaven _(actors: Virādha; Tumburu; Kubera; Rāma; Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/gandharvas-and-kinnaras.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya Kāṇḍa)
- **Virādha-vadha** — The man-eating rākṣasa Virādha seizes Sītā; Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa maim and bury him alive, freeing the gandharva Tumburu from a curse _(actors: Virādha (Tumburu), Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Sītā)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/dandakaranya-rama-forest.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 2–4)
- **Viṣṇu agrees to incarnate as Daśaratha's sons** — The gods, oppressed by the boon-protected Rāvaṇa, petition Viṣṇu, who agrees to be born as Daśaratha's sons to slay Rāvaṇa as a mortal _(actors: Devas, Brahmā, Viṣṇu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/1-bala-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 14–15)
- **Viṣṇu hides the sun for a false dusk** — Viṣṇu conceals the sun with Sudarśana to force the punctilious Rāvaṇa to halt for his evening sandhyā prayers _(actors: Viṣṇu, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/gokarna-mahabaleshwar-atma-linga.md) — (Gokarṇa-māhātmya)
- **Viśvāmitra spends tapas on a counter-heaven** — the sage sends Triśaṅku bodily skyward and creates a duplicate southern heaven when Indra hurls him down _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Triśaṅku, Indra)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/tapas-boons-and-curses.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 1.57–60)
- **Viśvāmitra's yajña and the curse of the boycotting sages** — Viśvāmitra undertakes Triśaṅku's heaven-rite and curses Vasiṣṭha's sons and Mahodaya, who refuse to attend, to become outcastes _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Triśaṅku, Mahodaya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/trishanku.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 59-60)
- **Agastya and Lopāmudrā** — Agastya fashions and weds the princess Lopāmudrā; at her condition he gathers wealth, and they beget the Veda-chanting son Dṛḍhasyu _(actors: Agastya, Lopāmudrā, Dṛḍhasyu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/agastya.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva; Ṛgveda 1.179)
- **Ajigarta binds and offers to butcher his own son** — After the four priests refuse to bind the boy, Ajigarta binds Sunahshepa for more cattle and offers to slaughter him for yet more, drunk on the promised cows _(actors: Ajigarta, Sunahshepa, Vishvamitra, Jamadagni, Vasishtha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sunahshepa.md) — (Aitareya Brahmana 7.16)
- **Ajigarta offers to bind and slay his own son** — When no one will bind or slay the boy, the father Ajigarta offers to bind and then slay his own son for further hundreds of cows _(actors: Ajigarta, Shunahshepa, Varuna)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/vedas/brahmanas/aitareya-brahmana.md) — (Aitareya Brahmana 7.16)
- **Śatrughna Slays Lavaṇa and Founds Mathurā** — Rama's brother Shatrughna kills the demon Lavana and founds Mathura on the site of the older Madhuvana _(actors: Shatrughna, Lavana)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/sapta-puri-and-sacred-cities.md) — (Ramayana tradition)
- **Bali Subdued by Vāmana (Trivikrama)** — The dharma-keeping asura-emperor Bali, having conquered the three worlds, is begged three paces of land by Viṣṇu as the dwarf Vāmana, who grows to Trivikrama, strides over creation and pushes Bali to Pātāla while blessing him as a future Indra _(actors: Bali, Viṣṇu (Vāmana/Trivikrama))_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/asura-wars-and-roster.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 8.15-23)
- **Battle of Janasthāna (Khara–Dūṣaṇa)** — Rāma alone destroys Khara, Dūṣaṇa, Triśiras and fourteen thousand rākṣasas of Janasthāna _(actors: Rāma, Khara, Dūṣaṇa, Triśiras)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/panchavati-nashik-godavari.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 19–30)
- **Bhagiratha's penance brings Ganga down** — After three generations fail, King Bhagiratha performs a thousand years of tapas; Brahma grants Ganga's descent but warns only Shiva can bear her fall _(actors: Bhagiratha, Brahma, Ganga)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/ganga.md) — (Ramayana, Bala Kanda 42-43)
- **Bharata at Śṛṅgaverapura; Guha's suspicion and guidance** — Bharata's army halts at Śṛṅgaverapura; Guha first suspects hostile intent then, reassured, shows Bharata the grass-bed where Rāma slept and helps ferry the host across the Gaṅgā _(actors: Bharata, Guha, Niṣādas)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/sringaverapura-guha-ganga-crossing.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa 84–86)
- **Breaking of Shiva's Bow** — Rama breaks the bow of Shiva at Janaka's court, winning the contest that qualifies him to marry Sita _(actors: Rama, Janaka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/README.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Bala Kanda)
- **Ṛcīka prepares two charged charus** — Ṛcīka cooks one brāhmaṇa-tejas caru for Satyavatī and one kṣatriya-tejas caru for her mother, to seed a brahmin heir and a warrior heir respectively _(actors: Ṛcīka, Satyavatī, Gādhi's queen)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/gadhi-and-the-caru-swap.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 115–116; Bhāgavata IX.15)
- **Cremation and celestial release** — The brothers burn Kabandha's body in a pit, and a luminous gandharva rises from the pyre on a celestial chariot, the curse undone _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Kabandha (as restored gandharva))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kabandha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 71)
- **Daśaratha's death and the queens' mourning** — Daśaratha dies of grief after Rāma's exile; Sumitrā shares the widowed household's vigil, deferring to Kausalyā _(actors: Daśaratha, Sumitrā, Kausalyā, Kaikeyī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sumitra.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa)
- **Delivery of Rāma's signet-ring** — Hanumān drops Rāma's name-engraved signet-ring before Sītā, who accepts him as Rāma's true envoy _(actors: Hanumān, Sītā)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/lanka-ramayana-trail-ashoka-vatika.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 36)
- **Discrimination of Self from the sheaths** — Rāma teaches dṛg-dṛśya-viveka and negation of the five kośas and three bodies, identifying the Self as the unchanging witness _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rama-gita.md) — (Adhyātma Rāmāyaṇa 7.5)
- **Division of the pāyasa and birth of the four sons** — Daśaratha apportions the pāyasa among his queens, and Rāma, Bharata, and the twins Lakṣmaṇa and Śatrughna are born as Viṣṇu's aṃśas _(actors: Daśaratha, Kausalyā, Kaikeyī, Sumitrā, Rāma, Bharata, Lakṣmaṇa, Śatrughna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dasharatha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla 16–18; Bhāgavata Purāṇa IX.10)
- **Fall of the rākṣasa champions and Prahasta** — In successive sorties Rāvaṇa's commanders Dhūmrākṣa, Akampana, Vajradaṃṣṭra, and the chief general Prahasta are slain by Hanumān, Aṅgada, and Nīla _(actors: Hanumān, Aṅgada, Nīla, Prahasta)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 51–58)
- **Gaṇeśa as the brahmin boy tricks Rāvaṇa** — Gaṇeśa, as a cowherd boy, agrees to hold the liṅga but sets it down after calling out three times when Rāvaṇa's prayers run long _(actors: Gaṇeśa, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/gokarna-mahabaleshwar-atma-linga.md) — (Gokarṇa-māhātmya; Śiva Purāṇa)
- **Gandharvas steal the rams and break the condition** — The Gandharvas steal Urvaśī's rams by night; Purūravas leaps up naked in pursuit and a lightning-flash reveals him, breaking the taboo, so Urvaśī vanishes _(actors: Purūravas, Urvaśī, Gandharvas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/pururavas-urvashi.md) — (Shatapatha Brahmana XI.5.1; Vishnu Purana IV.6; Bhagavata Purana IX.14)
- **Gautama curses Indra** — Gautama curses Indra to lose his testicles (later replaced with a ram's), or in Purāṇic versions to bear a thousand marks softened into a thousand eyes (Sahasrākṣa) _(actors: Gautama, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ahalya.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 48; Brahma Purāṇa; Padma Purāṇa)
- **Gautama curses the Moon** — In a Purāṇic strand Candra, implicated as Indra's accomplice in the dawn-ruse, is cursed to wane or bear the lunar blemish _(actors: Gautama, Candra (the Moon))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ahalya.md) — (Purāṇic tradition)
- **Gift of the pādukā (sandals) as regent-token** — Rāma surrenders his gold sandals; Bharata enthrones them and rules Kosala as their servant from Nandigrāma for the remaining exile _(actors: Rāma, Bharata)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/chitrakoot-kamadgiri.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa 112)
- **Gārgī concedes Yājñavalkya's supremacy** — Satisfied, Gārgī tells the assembled Brahmins that none of them will defeat Yājñavalkya in debate about Brahman, withdrawing her challenge with honor _(actors: Gārgī Vācaknavī, the assembled Brahmins, Yājñavalkya)_ → [details](../07-acharyas-and-sampradayas/gargi-and-maitreyi.md) — (Bṛhadāraṇyaka Upaniṣad 3.8.12)
- **Guha suspects and then receives Bharata** — When Bharata's army halts at Shringaverapura, Guha first musters his Nishadas fearing harm to Rama, then, finding Bharata grief-worn, honours him as a guest _(actors: Guha, Bharata, Nishadas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/guha.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana Ayodhya-kanda 84-85)
- **Hanuman brokers the Rama-Sugriva alliance** — Serving Sugriva, Hanuman first meets Rama and Lakshmana and brokers their alliance, impressing Rama with his flawless speech _(actors: Hanuman, Rama, Lakshmana, Sugriva)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/hanuman.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Kishkindha Kanda 3-4)
- **Hanumān finds Sītā in the Aśoka grove** — After searching Laṅkā's mansions in vain, Hanumān spots the wasted, sorrowful Sītā guarded by rākṣasīs in the Aśoka-vana _(actors: Hanumān, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/5-sundara-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 14–15)
- **Hariścandra vindicated and son revived** — The gods reveal the ordeal as a test Hariścandra passed without a lie; Rohitāśva is revived, the family and kingdom restored, and heaven won and shared with his people _(actors: Hariścandra, Indra, Yama/Dharma, Viśvāmitra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/hariscandra.md) — (Mārkaṇḍeya Purāṇa 8)
- **Indra seduces Ahalya and is cursed** — Indra takes sage Gautama's form to lie with Ahalya; the returning sage curses Indra (losing his testicles or bearing a thousand marks that become eyes, hence Sahasraksha) _(actors: Indra, Ahalya, Gautama)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/indra.md) — (Ramayana, Bala Kanda; Brahma Purana)
- **Śiva and Pārvatī complete the art with Tāṇḍava and Lāsya** — Śiva contributes the vigorous Tāṇḍava (via Taṇḍu) and Pārvatī the graceful Lāsya, completing the dance dimension of drama _(actors: Śiva, Taṇḍu, Pārvatī, Bharata)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/natya-shastra-and-the-classical-arts.md) — (Nāṭya Śāstra ch. 4; Abhinavabhāratī)
- **Jaṭāyu pledges to guard Sītā** — On the way to Pañcavaṭī the family meets the aged vulture-king Jaṭāyu, friend of Daśaratha, who pledges to protect Sītā in the brothers' absence _(actors: Jaṭāyu, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/3-aranya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya Kāṇḍa 14)
- **Kaikeyī retreats to the krodhāgāra** — Persuaded, Kaikeyī casts off her ornaments and lies in the anger-chamber to ambush the king _(actors: Kaikeyī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kaikeyi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā 9–10)
- **Kaikeyī's boons and Rāma's exile** — On the eve of his coronation Kaikeyī invokes two boons sending Rāma into 14-year exile and Bharata to the throne; Rāma departs with Sītā and Lakṣmaṇa _(actors: Kaikeyī, Mantharā, Rāma, Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa, Bharata)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa 2 (Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa))
- **Kausalyā's lament at the exile** — Learning of the fourteen-year exile, Kausalyā grieves and would at first forbid Rāma to go _(actors: Kausalyā, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kausalya.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 20–21)
- **Kārtavīrya Arjuna seizes Kāmadhenu** — The thousand-armed Haihaya king Kārtavīrya Arjuna (Sahasrārjuna) of Māhiṣmatī seizes the wish-cow by force and wrecks the hermitage _(actors: Kārtavīrya Arjuna, Kāmadhenu, Jamadagni)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/parashurama.md) — (Mahābhārata 3.115-116; Bhāgavata Purāṇa 9.15)
- **Kubera founds Alaka** — Dispossessed of Lanka, Kubera builds his magnificent new capital Alakapuri in the Himalayas near Kailasa, the archetypal city of inexhaustible riches _(actors: Kubera)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/kubera.md) — (Ramayana; Meghaduta)
- **Mantharā recalls the two boons** — She reminds Kaikeyī of Daśaratha's two boons and counsels demanding Bharata's coronation and Rāma's fourteen-year exile _(actors: Mantharā, Kaikeyī, (re: Daśaratha))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/manthara.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 9–11)
- **Marriage of Rishyashringa to Shanta** — The grateful Lomapada gives his daughter Shanta (originally Dasharatha's daughter) to Rishyashringa, turning the forest-celibate into a householder, and pacifies the wrathful Vibhandaka _(actors: Lomapada, Shanta, Rishyashringa, Vibhandaka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rishyashringa.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana-parva ~113)
- **Mārīca's fatalist consent** — Threatened with death by Rāvaṇa, Mārīca chooses death-by-Rāma as the nobler end and consents to the deer-ruse, foreseeing and accepting his own doom _(actors: Mārīca, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/maricha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 40; Rāmcaritmānas (liberation framing))
- **Mutilation of Śūrpaṇakhā** — When the rākṣasī Śūrpaṇakhā lunges at Sītā, Rāma orders Lakṣmaṇa to disfigure her; he cuts off her nose (and ears), the immediate spark of the war _(actors: Lakṣmaṇa, Śūrpaṇakhā, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/lakshmana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa)
- **Mutilation of Śūrpaṇakhā (nose & ears cut)** — Lakṣmaṇa, at Rāma's word, cuts off Śūrpaṇakhā's nose and ears as she lunges at Sītā — the act that names Nāsik and ignites the war _(actors: Lakṣmaṇa, Śūrpaṇakhā, Rāma, Sītā)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/panchavati-nashik-godavari.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 18; Nāsik regional tradition)
- **Paraśurāma slays Kārtavīrya and the kṣatriyas** — The axe-bearing avatāra Paraśurāma, after the Kāmadhenu theft and his father Jamadagni's death, slays the thousand-armed Kārtavīrya Arjuna and vows to clear the earth of kṣatriyas twenty-one times _(actors: Paraśurāma, Jamadagni, Reṇukā, Kārtavīrya Arjuna)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/brahmanda-purana.md) — (Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa (Paraśurāma cycle))
- **Rama crowns Vibhishana king of Lanka** — After Ravana's fall Rama crowns the defector Vibhishana king of Lanka and grants him life lasting to the Kalpa's end _(actors: Rama, Vibhishana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/the-chiranjivis.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana Yuddha Kanda)
- **Rama's Darbha-Sayanam at Tirupullani** — At the Sethu site of Tirupullani, Rama performs darbha-sayanam (lying on sacred grass) before the crossing to Lanka _(actors: Rama)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/108-divya-desams.md) — (Ramayana / sthala-purana)
- **Rama's Triumphant Return to Ayodhya** — Rama returns to Ayodhya after exile and the war, the prototype celebration of Dipavali _(actors: Rama)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/ayodhya.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Yuddha-kanda)
- **Renewed advance and slaying of Rāvaṇa** — Strengthened by the Sun's grace, Rāma takes up his bow, advances on Rāvaṇa, and goes on to slay him _(actors: Rāma, Rāvaṇa, Sūrya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/aditya-hridayam.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 105.31 and following sargas)
- **Return to relay Rāma's parting words at Daśaratha's deathbed** — Sumantra returns with the empty chariot and recites Rāma's messages and serenity to the dying Daśaratha, the king's last contact with his son before his death _(actors: Sumantra, Daśaratha, Kausalyā, Sumitrā, Rāma (reported), Vasiṣṭha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sumantra.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa 57–77)
- **Rāma's jala-samādhi at Gopratāra — return as Viṣṇu** — At the Gopratāra (Guptār) ford Brahmā says 'Enter, O Viṣṇu'; Rāma casts off his human body, resumes his Vaiṣṇava form, and ascends to Vaikuṇṭha _(actors: Rāma, Brahmā, the gods)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/sarayu-ayodhya-river.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa; Skanda Purāṇa Ayodhyā-māhātmya; (variant) Svargadvāra ghāṭ)
- **Rāma slays Subāhu with the Āgneyāstra** — Rāma looses the fire-weapon Āgneyāstra at Subāhu, who is struck in the chest and falls dead — Rāma's second slaying _(actors: Rāma, Subāhu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/subahu.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 30)
- **Rāma's twin proofs of strength** — To convince the doubting Sugrīva, Rāma flicks Dundubhi's skeleton ten yojanas with his toe and splits seven sāla trees with a single arrow _(actors: Rāma, Sugrīva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sugriva-and-vali.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa)
- **Rāma worships Sūrya and slays Rāvaṇa** — Rāma recites the hymn, gazes at the Sun thrice, is filled with resolve, and—urged on by Sūrya himself—takes up his bow and kills Rāvaṇa _(actors: Rāma, Sūrya, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/aditya-hridayam.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha-kāṇḍa 105.26–31)
- **Rāvaṇa Lifts Mount Kailāsa** — The demon-king Ravana tries to uproot Kailasa; Shiva pins it with his toe crushing Ravana, who then composes the Shiva-tandava-stotra _(actors: Ravana, Shiva)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/meru-kailash-and-cosmic-mountains.md) — (Ramayana Uttara-kanda; Shiva Purana)
- **Rāvaṇa's digvijaya and Indrajit's victory over Indra** — Rāvaṇa subdues yakṣas, gandharvas, nāgas, and kings, even defeating Yama, while his son Meghanāda conquers Indra and earns the name Indrajit _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Meghanāda/Indrajit, Yama, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ravana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa)
- **Rāvaṇa sings the Śiva Tāṇḍava Stotra** — Rāvaṇa composes and sings the hymn praising Śiva's cosmic dance to win release and favor _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Śiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/shiva-tandava-stotra.md) — (Śiva-tāṇḍava-stotra; Rāvaṇānugraha legend)
- **Sagara's Aśvamedha and the theft of the horse** — Indra steals the horse of King Sagara's Aśvamedha, leading to the destruction of his 60,000 sons and ultimately framing the descent of the Gaṅgā _(actors: Sagara, Indra, Sagara's 60,000 sons)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/yajnas-and-vedic-rituals.md) — (Ramayana; Bhagavata Purana 9)
- **Shiva-Gita: Shiva instructs Rama and grants the Pashupata weapon** — In a Shaiva counterpart to the Bhagavad Gita, Shiva instructs Rama in self-knowledge and grants him the pashupata weapon _(actors: Shiva, Rama)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/padma-purana.md) — (Padma Purana (Uttara Khanda, Shiva-Gita))
- **Slaughter of Vasiṣṭha's hundred sons** — In the Mahābhārata telling Viśvāmitra engineers the death of Vasiṣṭha's hundred sons via the cannibal rākṣasa Kalmāṣapāda _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Vasiṣṭha, Śakti, Kalmāṣapāda)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vishwamitra-vasishtha.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva, Vasiṣṭha upākhyāna)
- **Sītā and Lakṣmaṇa insist on accompanying Rāma** — Sītā refuses to be left behind, declaring a wife's place is at her husband's side, and Lakṣmaṇa is granted leave to follow as servant-protector _(actors: Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/2-ayodhya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 26–31)
- **Sītā fashions the sand-liṅga (Rāmaliṅga)** — With the muhūrta lapsing, Sītā makes a liṅga from seashore sand which Rāma installs and worships as the Rāmanātha-liṅga, the temple's sanctum deity _(actors: Sītā, Rāma)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/rameswaram-setu-bandha.md) — (Rāmanātha sthala-purāṇa; Skanda Purāṇa Setu-māhātmya)
- **Sugrīva enthroned, Aṅgada made heir** — Lakṣmaṇa enters Kiṣkindhā and consecrates Sugrīva king of the vānaras, with Vāli's son Aṅgada installed as crown prince _(actors: Sugrīva, Aṅgada, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/4-kishkindha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā Kāṇḍa 25–26)
- **Sutikshna and Rama's vow to clear Dandaka** — At Sutikshna's ashram the rishis beg protection and Rama vows to rid the forest of the rakshasas devouring them _(actors: Sutikshna, Rama, the rishis of Dandaka)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Aranya-kanda 6-7)
- **Teaching on bhakti as precondition of knowledge** — Śiva declares single-minded devotion to Śiva, not rite or lineage, opens liberating knowledge _(actors: Śiva, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shiva-gita.md) — (Padma Purāṇa, Śiva-Gītā 4–5)
- **The beheading of Reṇukā** — Jamadagni orders his sons to slay their mother; the four elders refuse and are cursed to idiocy, while Paraśurāma alone obeys and beheads Reṇukā with the axe _(actors: Jamadagni, Paraśurāma, Reṇukā, four elder sons)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/jamadagni-parashurama.md) — (Mahābhārata 3.116; Bhāgavata Purāṇa 9.16)
- **The boon fulfilled — birth of Raghu** — Curse lifted, Dilīpa and Sudakṣiṇā return to Ayodhyā and bear the son Raghu, whose glory renames the senior Solar line the Raghuvaṃśa _(actors: Dilīpa, Sudakṣiṇā, Raghu, Nandinī, Vasiṣṭha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/dilipa-nandini.md) — (Raghuvaṃśa 2.69–75, 3.1–21 (Kālidāsa))
- **The fallen garland and Indumatī's sudden death** — Nārada's celestial garland falls from the sky onto Indumatī in a pleasure-garden; the touch of a heavenly object fulfills the curse-term and she dies instantly, Hariṇī released to heaven _(actors: Aja, Indumatī, Nārada)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/aja-indumati.md) — (Kālidāsa, Raghuvaṃśa canto 8)
- **The fire-witnessed Rāma-Sugrīva alliance** — Hanumān kindles fire; Rāma and Sugrīva circumambulate it and swear mutual aid with Agni as witness — Rāma to slay Vāli and restore Sugrīva's kingdom, Sugrīva to find Sītā _(actors: Rāma, Sugrīva, Hanumān, Agni)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kishkindha-hampi-anegundi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa 5)
- **The four-fold wedding at Mithilā** — Daśaratha arrives and all four sons wed together: Rāma-Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa-Ūrmilā, Bharata-Māṇḍavī, Śatrughna-Śrutakīrti, on Vivāha Pañcamī _(actors: Rāma, Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa, Bharata, Śatrughna, Ūrmilā, Māṇḍavī, Śrutakīrti, Daśaratha, Janaka, Kuśadhvaja, Vasiṣṭha)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/janakpur-mithila-sita-janmabhumi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla-kāṇḍa 70-73; Maithilī tradition)
- **The golden deer lures Rāma away** — Mārīca takes the form of a dazzling golden deer at Rāvaṇa's order; the enchanted Sītā begs Rāma to capture it, drawing him from the hermitage _(actors: Sītā, Mārīca, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sita.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya Kāṇḍa)
- **The half-tasted berries offered to Rāma** — Śabarī offers forest fruit — in the devotional tradition, berries she has bitten first to test for sweetness — which Rāma eats with delight as the purest offering of love, over Lakṣmaṇa's dismay _(actors: Śabarī, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shabari.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Araṇya-kāṇḍa 74; Adhyātma Rāmāyaṇa; folk/bhakti retellings)
- **The pādukā and the Nandigrāma regency** — Bharata takes Rāma's golden sandals, enthrones them as sovereign, and rules as ascetic regent from Nandigrāma for fourteen years, vowing self-immolation if Rāma does not return on time _(actors: Bharata, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bharata-shatrughna-and-their-wives.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa)
- **The pāyasa distributed to the queens** — A divine being rises from the sacrificial fire bearing pāyasa for the queens; its distribution gives Sumitrā two sons _(actors: Daśaratha, Kausalyā, Kaikeyī, Sumitrā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/1-bala-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 16)
- **The reviving boon** — Pleased, Jamadagni grants boons; Paraśurāma asks that his mother be revived without memory and his brothers restored _(actors: Paraśurāma, Jamadagni, Reṇukā)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/renuka-yellamma.md) — (Mahābhārata 3.116; Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇa)
- **The roar and the naming of Rāvaṇa** — Pinned, the demon screams so the three worlds shudder; from this cry Śiva gives him the name Rāvaṇa ('he who makes roar/cry') _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Śiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ravana-kailasa-ravananugraha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttarakāṇḍa 16; Rāvaṇānugraha tradition)
- **The trials of Harishchandra** — The truthful Ikshvaku king Harishchandra is stripped of kingdom, wife and son to honor a debt to Vishvamitra and becomes a cremation-ground slave, yet never lies and is finally restored to heaven _(actors: Harishchandra, Vishvamitra, Shaivya, his son)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/markandeya-purana.md) — (Markandeya Purana (chs. 7-8))
- **Triśaṅku's ascent and Indra's kick** — By Viśvāmitra's tapas Triśaṅku rises bodily to heaven, but Indra and the devas kick him back down head-first _(actors: Triśaṅku, Viśvāmitra, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/trishanku.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 60)
- **Trijaṭā consoles Sītā** — Trijaṭā reassures the despairing Sītā that rescue is near, steadying her resolve _(actors: Trijaṭā, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/trijata.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara/Yuddha Kāṇḍa)
- **Śukra's Warning, Curse, and the Blinded Eye** — Recognizing Viṣṇu, Śukra forbids the gift and curses Bali; lodging in the kamaṇḍalu spout to block the consecration-water, he is pierced in one eye by Vāmana's kuśa-grass, becoming one-eyed _(actors: Śukrācārya, Bali, Vāmana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vamana-bali.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 8.19–8.20)
- **Vamana's three strides reclaiming the worlds from Bali** — The dwarf avatara takes three strides to reclaim the worlds from the asura king Bali, embedded in the Matsya Purana's deva-asura material _(actors: Vishnu (Vamana), Bali)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/matsya-purana.md) — (Matsya Purana)
- **Vasiṣṭha's grief and Parāśara's restrained vengeance** — Bereaved Vasiṣṭha survives repeated suicide attempts and dissuades grandson Parāśara from a world-destroying revenge sacrifice _(actors: Vasiṣṭha, Parāśara)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vishwamitra-vasishtha.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Vibhīṣaṇa performs Rāvaṇa's last rites** — At first refusing to perform funeral rites for the brother who wronged him, Vibhīṣaṇa is persuaded by Rāma that enmity ends at death and performs the obsequies _(actors: Vibhīṣaṇa, Rāma, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vibhishana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa)
- **Vishvajit sacrifice — giving away all wealth** — Raghu performs the Vishvajit rite, whose vow requires him to give away the entire accumulated wealth of his conquests, leaving himself only earthen vessels _(actors: Raghu, brahmins)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/raghu.md) — (Raghuvamsha 5.1-3)
- **Vishvamitra performs the ascent yajna and curses the boycotters** — Vishvamitra agrees to send Trishanku bodily to heaven and curses Vasishtha's sons and Mahodaya to ash/chandala rebirth when they refuse to attend _(actors: Vishvamitra, Trishanku, Mahodaya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/trishanku.md) — (Ramayana Bala Kanda 59-60)
- **Viśvāmitra adopts Sunaḥśepa as Devarāta** — Hotṛ-priest Viśvāmitra adopts the rescued boy as eldest son Devarāta; fifty of his own sons refuse and are cursed to become border peoples _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Sunaḥśepa/Devarāta)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sunahshepa.md) — (Aitareya Brāhmaṇa 7.17-18)
- **Viśvāmitra adopts Śunaḥśepa and curses his sons** — Viśvāmitra adopts Śunaḥśepa as eldest son, renaming him Devarāta, and curses the fifty of his own sons who refuse the foundling to become outcastes — an aetiology of border tribes _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Śunaḥśepa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/hariscandra.md) — (Aitareya Brāhmaṇa 7.17–18)
- **Viśvāmitra brings the princes to her forest** — Viśvāmitra leads Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa into the Tāṭakā-vana, recounts her story, and commands Rāma to slay her _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/tataka.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 24-26)
- **Vālmīki composes the Rāmāyaṇa and teaches it to the twins** — The ādi-kavi composes the 24,000-śloka Rāmāyaṇa (śoka turned to śloka) and trains the twins to chant it, making the hero's unrecognised sons its first reciters _(actors: Vālmīki, Lava, Kuśa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttara-ramayana-lava-kusha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Uttara Kāṇḍa 71, 93)
- **Vāmana reclaims the three worlds** — Viṣṇu as the dwarf Vāmana reclaims the three worlds from the asura king Bali in three strides _(actors: Vāmana, Bali)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/trimurti-and-tridevi.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa)
- **Śabalā's Army vs. Viśvāmitra** — When King Viśvāmitra tries to seize Vasiṣṭha's cow Śabalā by force, she generates whole foreign armies from her body that rout his forces _(actors: Śabalā, Viśvāmitra, Vasiṣṭha)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/kamadhenu-and-divine-cattle.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla-kāṇḍa ch. 54-55)
- **Śabarī awaits Rāma at the Pampā-sarovara** — The devotee Śabarī awaits Rāma at the Pampā lotus-lake and offers her tasted berries before he passes into Kiṣkindhā _(actors: Śabarī, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/sacred-rivers-and-descent-myths.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa)
- **Agastya equips Rāma in the South** — At his Daṇḍaka hermitage Agastya gifts Rāma the bow of Viṣṇu and divine arms, and later teaches him the Āditya-Hṛdayam before slaying Rāvaṇa _(actors: Agastya, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/agastya.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Araṇya & Yuddha Kāṇḍas)
- **Agastya gifts Rama divine weapons** — Agastya gives Rama the bow of Vishnu, an inexhaustible quiver and the arrows of Indra and Brahma, and directs him to Panchavati _(actors: Agastya, Rama)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Aranya-kanda 11-13)
- **Agastya narrates the condensed Rāmāyaṇa** — The sage Agastya recounts a compressed Rāma-carita — Rāma's birth, exile, Sītā's abduction by Rāvaṇa, the alliance with Sugrīva and Hanumān, the war at Laṅkā, and the return _(actors: Agastya, Rāma, Sītā, Rāvaṇa, Hanumān, Sugrīva)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/agni-purana.md) — (Agni Purāṇa (condensed Rāmāyaṇa))
- **A thousand years of praise (Śiva Tāṇḍava Stotra)** — Trapped, Rāvaṇa propitiates Śiva with praise (traditionally the Śiva Tāṇḍava Stotra, in one telling on a vīṇā strung from his own severed head) for a thousand years _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Śiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ravana-kailasa-ravananugraha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttarakāṇḍa 16; Śiva Tāṇḍava Stotra; Tēvāram tradition)
- **Atri and Anasūyā counsel Sītā** — At the Mandākinī-side hermitage, Anasūyā instructs Sītā on pati-vrata-dharma and gifts her divine ornaments; her tapas is credited with bringing down the Mandākinī _(actors: Atri, Anasūyā, Sītā)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/chitrakoot-kamadgiri.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa 117–118)
- **Bali Grants the Gift Despite the Warning** — Invoking Prahlāda and the supreme dharma of truth, Bali refuses to retract his word, accepts Śukra's curse, and pours the water to seal the three paces _(actors: Bali, Vindhyāvali, Vāmana, Śukrācārya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vamana-bali.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 8.20)
- **Brahmā orders the gods to father the vānaras** — Brahmā commands the gods to beget mighty vānaras (Vālin, Sugrīva, Hanumān, Nala) as Rāma's future army _(actors: Brahmā, Devas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/1-bala-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 17)
- **Counsel to ally with Sugrīva** — Out of gratitude the freed gandharva names Sugrīva at Ṛśyamūka, advises Rāma to swear fire-witnessed friendship with him, framing the reciprocal bargain that drives the Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa _(actors: Kabandha (gandharva), Rāma, Sugrīva (named))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kabandha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 72)
- **Creation of the counter-heaven (Triśaṅku-svarga)** — Enraged Viśvāmitra builds a parallel southern heaven with new stars and a second Indra; the gods relent, fixing Triśaṅku head-down as a constellation _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Triśaṅku, Brahmā, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/trishanku.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 60)
- **Death of Kumbhakarṇa** — Roused from his cursed long sleep, the colossal Kumbhakarṇa devours and scatters vānaras until Rāma severs his limbs and head with celestial arrows _(actors: Rāma, Kumbhakarṇa, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 60–67)
- **Departure from Ayodhyā into exile** — Donning bark garments, Rāma, Sītā, and Lakṣmaṇa leave on Sumantra's chariot as the grieving city follows _(actors: Rāma, Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa, Sumantra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/2-ayodhya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 37–40)
- **Exposing the illusory head of Rāma** — When Rāvaṇa conjures a false severed head of Rāma to break Sītā, Trijaṭā reveals the deception and assures her Rāma lives _(actors: Trijaṭā, Sītā, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/trijata.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa; later retellings)
- **First slaying of Kārtavīrya** — Paraśurāma pursues, severs Kārtavīrya's thousand arms and beheads him, recovering the cow _(actors: Paraśurāma, Kārtavīrya Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/parashurama.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 9.15-16)
- **Gaṅgādhara — descent of Gaṅgā** — Śiva catches the celestial Gaṅgā in his matted locks to break her world-shattering fall, brought down by Bhagīratha's penance to redeem Sagara's 60,000 sons _(actors: Śiva, Gaṅgā, Bhagīratha)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/shiva-myth-cycle.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa, Bhāgavata Purāṇa, Skanda Purāṇa)
- **Ganesha breaks his tusk against Parashurama** — In a variant, Ganesha blocks Parashurama at Kailasa and takes Shiva's axe on his tusk out of reverence, breaking it _(actors: Ganesha, Parashurama)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/ganesha-and-kartikeya.md) — (Brahmavaivarta Purana)
- **Gautama curses Ahalyā to stone/invisibility** — Gautama curses Ahalyā — in Vālmīki to invisible ash-strewn penance, in Purāṇic/devotional tradition to become stone — until released by Rāma's advent _(actors: Gautama, Ahalyā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ahalya.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 48; Brahma Purāṇa; Adhyātma Rāmāyaṇa; Rāmcaritmānas; Brahmavaivarta Purāṇa)
- **Guha shows Bharata Rama's grass-bed and guides him onward** — Guha points out the bed of grass and ingudi tree where Rama slept, then arranges Bharata's host across the Ganga and directs him toward Bharadvaja's ashrama _(actors: Guha, Bharata)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/guha.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana Ayodhya-kanda 85-86)
- **Hanuman leaps the ocean to Lanka** — Reminded of his powers by Jambavan, Hanuman expands to colossal size and bounds across the ocean to Lanka, defeating Surasa and slaying Simhika en route _(actors: Hanuman, Jambavan, Surasa, Simhika)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/hanuman.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Sundara Kanda 1)
- **Hanumān's Viśvaliṅga honored first by Śiva's decree** — Hanumān returns with the Kailāsa Viśvaliṅga to find worship done; Rāma decrees it be worshipped before the Rāmaliṅga, honoring Hanumān's devotion _(actors: Hanumān, Rāma, Śiva)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/rameswaram-setu-bandha.md) — (Rāmanātha sthala-purāṇa (folk))
- **Hariscandra the truthful** — Ikshvaku king Hariscandra surrenders kingdom, wife, and son to honor a debt to Vishvamitra, is tested to the extreme, and elevated to heaven _(actors: Hariscandra, Vishvamitra, Shaivya, Rohitashva)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Markandeya Purana, Aitareya Brahmana, Devi Bhagavata)
- **Śiva's grace (Rāvaṇānugraha) and the boon** — Pleased, Śiva frees Rāvaṇa, restores his strength, and grants a boon (often the sword Candrahāsa) _(actors: Śiva, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/stotras/shiva-tandava-stotra.md) — (Rāvaṇānugraha legend [folk])
- **Kautsa episode and the assault on Kubera** — The brahmin Kautsa begs fourteen crore gold coins for his guru's fee; the now-penniless Raghu prepares to attack Kubera, who rains gold into his treasury _(actors: Raghu, Kautsa, Varatantu, Kubera)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/raghu.md) — (Raghuvamsha 5.20-34)
- **Lakṣmaṇa recalls Sugrīva to his vow** — When Sugrīva lapses into pleasure and forgets his oath, an enraged Lakṣmaṇa is sent to Kiṣkindhā; Tārā's diplomacy rouses Sugrīva to mobilize the search _(actors: Lakṣmaṇa, Sugrīva, Tārā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/4-kishkindha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā Kāṇḍa 30–33)
- **Lakṣmaṇa returns as Śeṣa** — Lakṣmaṇa, who entered the Sarayū before Rāma, is taken up as Śeṣa/Ananta, the cosmic serpent on whom Viṣṇu reclines _(actors: Lakṣmaṇa (as Śeṣa/Ananta), Viṣṇu)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/sarayu-ayodhya-river.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa)
- **Maināka Rises to Aid Hanumān** — As Hanuman leaps the ocean to Lanka, Mainaka rises from the sea to offer him rest as a debt repaid to Vayu; Hanuman touches it courteously and flies on _(actors: Mainaka, Hanuman)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/meru-kailash-and-cosmic-mountains.md) — (Ramayana Sundara-kanda)
- **Marriage of Rama and Sita** — Having broken Shiva's bow, Rama marries Sita, princess of Mithila _(actors: Rama, Sita, Janaka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/README.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Bala Kanda)
- **Menakā breaks Viśvāmitra's tapas; birth of Śakuntalā** — Indra sends apsarā Menakā to seduce Viśvāmitra; they live together ten years and beget Śakuntalā, future mother of Bharata _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Menakā, Indra, Śakuntalā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vishwamitra-vasishtha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 63; Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Māyā and superimposition** — Rāma explains the world as the play of māyā, an apparent superimposition on Brahman like silver on nacre or snake on rope _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rama-gita.md) — (Adhyātma Rāmāyaṇa 7.5)
- **Rama's Jala-Samadhi into the Sarayu** — At his reign's close Rama appoints Kusha and Lava, walks into the Sarayu at Gopratara, takes his Vaishnava form and ascends to Vaikuntha _(actors: Rama, Kusha, Lava)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/ayodhya.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Uttara-kanda)
- **Rambhā assaulted by Rāvaṇa and Nalakūbara's curse** — Rāvaṇa assaults Rambhā despite her protests, prompting Nalakūbara to curse that Rāvaṇa's head would burst into seven pieces if he ever forced a woman — the curse that later protected Sītā _(actors: Rambhā, Rāvaṇa, Nalakūbara)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/apsaras.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Uttara Kāṇḍa)
- **Ravana steals the Pushpaka** — Ravana assaults Kubera again and carries off the self-willed flying palace-chariot Pushpaka _(actors: Ravana, Kubera)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/kubera.md) — (Mahabharata Ramopakhyana; Ramayana)
- **Reunion at Rāma's coronation** — On Rāma's return and coronation the three queens are reunited with their sons; Sumitrā recovers her twins honoured in service and kingship _(actors: Sumitrā, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Śatrughna, Kausalyā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sumitra.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa tradition)
- **Reunion at the swan-lake and the annual-night boon** — Wandering and lamenting, Purūravas finds Urvaśī among apsarās as aquatic birds at Anyataḥplakṣā; she grants him one night a year and the promise of a son _(actors: Purūravas, Urvaśī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/pururavas-urvashi.md) — (Shatapatha Brahmana XI.5.1)
- **Rāma instructs Hanumān on the 108 Upaniṣads** — In the Muktikā Upaniṣad, Rāma teaches Hanumān that liberation comes from studying the 108 Upaniṣads and enumerates them _(actors: Rāma, Hanumān)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/shruti-vedas-and-upanishads.md) — (Muktikā Upaniṣad)
- **Rāma reconciles his mother and receives her blessing** — Rāma argues that obeying a father's word is supreme dharma; Kausalyā yields and gives a protective benediction for the forest journey _(actors: Kausalyā, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kausalya.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 21, 24–25)
- **Rāma's Aśvamedha with the golden Sītā** — Refusing to remarry, Rāma performs the imperial horse-sacrifice using a golden image of Sītā in the wife's ritual seat _(actors: Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttara-ramayana-lava-kusha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Uttara Kāṇḍa 91)
- **Rāma's Śrāddha for Daśaratha at Gayā** — Rāma, Sītā and Lakṣmaṇa come to Gayā to perform śrāddha for Daśaratha; while the men bathe, the ancestor's hand appears demanding the piṇḍa _(actors: Rāma, Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa, Daśaratha)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/gaya-pitru-tirtha.md) — (local sthala-purana (Ramayana-derived))
- **Rāma's return and coronation** — Rāma flies home by the Puṣpaka vimāna; Hanumān reassures Bharata, who hands back the kingdom at the Rāma-rājya coronation _(actors: Rāma, Bharata, Hanumān, Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa, Śatrughna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bharata-shatrughna-and-their-wives.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha-kāṇḍa / Uttara-kāṇḍa)
- **Rāma teaches Śabarī the navadhā bhakti** — In the Rāmcaritmānas, Rāma tells the low-born Śabarī he reckons devotion alone above caste and expounds the nine forms of bhakti, declaring her dear to him _(actors: Rāma, Śabarī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shabari.md) — (Tulsīdās Rāmcaritmānas, Araṇya-kāṇḍ; Adhyātma Rāmāyaṇa)
- **Śūrpaṇakhā and Akampana rouse Rāvaṇa** — Survivors carry word of the slaughter to Laṅkā, steering Rāvaṇa toward abduction _(actors: Śūrpaṇakhā, Akampana, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/panchavati-nashik-godavari.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 31–34)
- **Rāvaṇa lifts Mount Kailāsa and receives Candrahāsa** — Affronted at Kailāsa, Rāvaṇa tries to uproot the mountain; Śiva pins him with a toe, and after Rāvaṇa sings his praises for a thousand years Śiva releases him and grants the sword Candrahāsa _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Śiva, Nandī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ravana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa)
- **Rāvaṇa threatens Sītā with two months** — Rāvaṇa courts Sītā with promises of queenship; she rejects him utterly, and he gives her two months to relent or be killed _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/5-sundara-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 18–23)
- **Sanatkumara's prophecy of Rishyashringa** — The sage Sanatkumara foretold that Vibhandaka's son Rishyashringa would come to Anga, end its drought, marry Shanta, and later enable an Ikshvaku king to obtain sons _(actors: Sanatkumara)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rishyashringa.md) — (Ramayana (Valmiki), Bala-kanda ~9)
- **Second demonic birth: Ravana and Kumbhakarna slain** — The cursed pair are reborn as the rakshasa brothers Ravana and Kumbhakarna of Lanka, both slain by the single avatara Rama in Treta-yuga _(actors: Ravana, Kumbhakarna, Rama, Sita)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/jaya-vijaya.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 7.1, 7.10; Valmiki Ramayana)
- **Shiva catches Ganga in his locks** — Shiva traps the proud, plunging Ganga entirely in his matted hair, then releases her in streams to earth, becoming Gangadhara _(actors: Shiva, Ganga, Bhagiratha)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/ganga.md) — (Ramayana, Bala Kanda 43-44)
- **Shunahshepa freed by praising the gods** — Bound to the stake, Shunahshepa praises Prajapati, Agni, Savitr, Varuna and the Vishvedevas with rc; his bonds loosen verse by verse and the king is cured _(actors: Shunahshepa, Prajapati, Agni, Savitr, Varuna, Vishvedevas, Hariscandra)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/vedas/brahmanas/aitareya-brahmana.md) — (Aitareya Brahmana 7.17; Rigveda 1.24-1.30)
- **Slaughter of Khara, Dūṣaṇa and 14,000 rākṣasas at Janasthāna** — Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa annihilate Khara, Dūṣaṇa, and 14,000 rākṣasas at Janasthāna, driving Rāvaṇa to plot Sītā's abduction _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Khara, Dūṣaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/lakshmana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa)
- **Slaughter of Khara, Dūṣaṇa and the host of Janasthāna** — Rāma alone destroys the fourteen-thousand-strong rākṣasa army of Janasthāna, clearing the district and goading Rāvaṇa toward abduction _(actors: Rāma, Khara, Dūṣaṇa, Triśiras, Śūrpaṇakhā)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/panchavati-nashik-godavari.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 19–34)
- **Sītā casts down her jewels as a trail** — Borne off by Rāvaṇa, Sītā drops her jewels to leave a trail later recovered with the vānaras, pointing the search toward Laṅkā _(actors: Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sita.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya/Kiṣkindhā Kāṇḍa)
- **Sītā's cūḍāmaṇi token** — Sītā gives Hanumān her crest-jewel and the private kākāsura recollection as recognition-tokens to carry back to Rāma _(actors: Sītā, Hanumān)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/lanka-ramayana-trail-ashoka-vatika.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 38)
- **Sītā's ornaments dropped over Ṛṣyamūka** — Sugrīva produces jewels Sītā cast from Rāvaṇa's flying chariot as it passed over Ṛṣyamūka; Rāma recognizes only her foot-ornaments and weeps — the first trace of the abduction route _(actors: Sugrīva, Rāma, (Sītā, Rāvaṇa))_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kishkindha-hampi-anegundi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa 6)
- **Sunahshepa's hymns free him and cure Harishchandra** — Bound to the post, Sunahshepa praises the gods with Rigvedic hymns; his fetters fall verse by verse and with the hymn to Ushas the last bond falls and Harishchandra is cured _(actors: Sunahshepa, Varuna, Ushas, Harishchandra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sunahshepa.md) — (Aitareya Brahmana 7.16-18; Rigveda 1.24-30)
- **Sutīkṣṇa's hermitage** — Rāma is received by the ascetic Sutīkṣṇa, who hosts the party and directs them deeper south toward Agastya _(actors: Sutīkṣṇa, Rāma, Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/dandakaranya-rama-forest.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 7–8, 11)
- **Teaching on the jīva and embodiment** — Śiva expounds the constitution of the body, the sheaths, transmigration, and the wretchedness of repeated birth _(actors: Śiva, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shiva-gita.md) — (Padma Purāṇa, Śiva-Gītā 6–9)
- **The Ajavilāpa (Aja's lament)** — Aja pours out the celebrated vilāpa lament over Indumatī's body — the flower-turned-thunderbolt, his loss of wife/counselor/pupil/companion at one stroke — and refuses all consolation, including Nārada's revelation _(actors: Aja, Indumatī (dead), Nārada)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/aja-indumati.md) — (Kālidāsa, Raghuvaṃśa canto 8)
- **The Ambarīṣa variant of the Sunaḥśepa rescue** — King Ambarīṣa procures Sunaḥśepa (son of Ṛcīka) as victim when Indra steals his sacrifice; Viśvāmitra teaches the boy mantras and Indra grants him long life _(actors: Ambarīṣa, Sunaḥśepa, Ṛcīka, Viśvāmitra, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sunahshepa.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 61-62)
- **The boon and restoration of Reṇukā** — Pleased by his obedience, Jamadagni grants boons; Paraśurāma asks that his mother be revived without memory, his brothers regain their wits, and for invincibility and long life _(actors: Jamadagni, Paraśurāma, Reṇukā, brothers)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/jamadagni-parashurama.md) — (Mahābhārata 3.116; Bhāgavata Purāṇa 9.16)
- **The caru-swap** — The queen-mother, coveting a finer son, persuades Satyavatī to exchange charus (and trees), so the brāhmaṇa-caru goes to the mother and the kṣatriya-caru to Satyavatī _(actors: Satyavatī, Gādhi's queen)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/gadhi-and-the-caru-swap.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 116; Viṣṇu Purāṇa IV.7)
- **The coronation plan and Mantharā's redemption of the boons** — Daśaratha resolves to crown Rāma yuvarāja, but Mantharā goads Kaikeyī to invoke her two dormant boons demanding Bharata's crown and Rāma's fourteen-year exile _(actors: Daśaratha, Rāma, Kaikeyī, Mantharā, Bharata)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dasharatha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Ayodhyā 1–11)
- **The death of Vālī** — After a garland marks Sugrīva in the rematch, Rāma shoots Vālī from concealment; the dying Vālī rebukes Rāma, then accepts the justice of it and commends his son and queen _(actors: Rāma, Vālī, Sugrīva, Hanumān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sugriva-and-vali.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa 14-18)
- **The dharma-debate over killing a woman** — Rāma hesitates to kill a woman; Viśvāmitra resolves that protecting the world from a predator overrides the prohibition, citing precedents _(actors: Rāma, Viśvāmitra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/tataka.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 26)
- **The golden deer lures Sītā and Rāma** — Mārīca takes the form of a jewel-like golden deer (Mārīca-mṛga) near Pañcavaṭī; Sītā longs for it and sends a reluctant Rāma to capture it _(actors: Mārīca, Sītā, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/maricha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 42-43)
- **The kopa-bhavana scripted** — On Mantharā's instruction Kaikeyī enters the chamber of wrath in soiled garments to confront Daśaratha _(actors: Mantharā, Kaikeyī, Daśaratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/manthara.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 9–10)
- **The rākṣasa host scattered and the sacrifice completed** — Rāma disperses the remaining night-rangers with the Vāyavyāstra, letting Viśvāmitra complete the sacrifice unpolluted _(actors: Rāma, Viśvāmitra, rākṣasa host)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/subahu.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 30)
- **The Ātma-liṅga roots as Mahābaleśvara** — The liṅga roots immovably at Gokarṇa; Rāvaṇa's vain great force (mahā-bala) to lift it names it Mahābaleśvara _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Gaṇeśa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/gokarna-mahabaleshwar-atma-linga.md) — (Gokarṇa-māhātmya; Śiva Purāṇa)
- **The two demands and Daśaratha's ruin** — Kaikeyī binds Daśaratha to his oath and demands Bharata's coronation and Rāma's fourteen-year exile, breaking the king _(actors: Kaikeyī, Daśaratha, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kaikeyi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā 10–13; Rāmcaritmānas)
- **Trishanku ascends and Indra kicks him down** — By Vishvamitra's tapas Trishanku rises bodily to heaven's gates, but Indra and the devas kick the cursed chandala back down and he plummets head-downward _(actors: Trishanku, Vishvamitra, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/trishanku.md) — (Ramayana Bala Kanda 60)
- **Vasiṣṭha's restraint and survival** — Bereaved, Vasiṣṭha attempts suicide in fire, sea and rivers which refuse to kill him, then dissuades grandson Parāśara from a world-destroying revenge sacrifice _(actors: Vasiṣṭha, Parāśara)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vishwamitra-vasishtha.md) — (Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Vibhīṣaṇa crowned king of Laṅkā** — After victory Rāma fulfils his pledge and has Vibhīṣaṇa formally crowned king of Laṅkā, establishing dharmic rule in place of Rāvaṇa's tyranny _(actors: Vibhīṣaṇa, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vibhishana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa)
- **Vishnu agrees to descend as Dasharatha's sons** — Oppressed by Ravana, the gods petition Brahma/Vishnu, and Vishnu agrees to be born as the four sons of Dasharatha to destroy the demon _(actors: Vishnu, Brahma, the devas, Dasharatha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramopakhyana.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana Parva 259-260)
- **Viśvāmitra demands Hariścandra's kingdom** — After Hariścandra disrupts his penance and promises him anything, Viśvāmitra claims the rājasūya dakṣiṇā and demands the entire kingdom, leaving the king only his body, wife, and son _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Hariścandra, Vasiṣṭha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/hariscandra.md) — (Mārkaṇḍeya Purāṇa 7)
- **Yājñavalkya's renunciation and division of his estate** — Resolving to leave householder life for renunciation, Yājñavalkya proposes to divide his property between his two wives, the brahmavādinī Maitreyī and the worldly Kātyāyanī _(actors: Yājñavalkya, Maitreyī, Kātyāyanī)_ → [details](../07-acharyas-and-sampradayas/gargi-and-maitreyi.md) — (Bṛhadāraṇyaka Upaniṣad 2.4.1, 4.5.1)
- **Śabarī's self-immolation and ascent** — Her purpose fulfilled, Śabarī enters fire by her own yogic power with Rāma's grace and ascends to the highest worlds to rejoin Mataṅga's disciples _(actors: Śabarī, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shabari.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Araṇya-kāṇḍa 74)
- **Agastya Humbles the Vindhya Mountain** — The Vindhya grows skyward in jealousy of Meru until sage Agastya asks it to bow until he returns from the south and never returns, fixing it low and marking Agastya's southward journey _(actors: Agastya, Vindhya)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/meru-kailash-and-cosmic-mountains.md) — (Mahabharata; Padma Purana)
- **Agastya's gift of Viṣṇu's bow** — At Agastya's āśrama the sage gives Rāma the bow of Viṣṇu, an inexhaustible quiver, a sword, and arrows of Indra and Brahmā _(actors: Agastya, Rāma)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/dandakaranya-rama-forest.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 11–12)
- **Aja's renunciation and death** — Wasting in grief, Aja rears Daśaratha to kingship, crowns him, then ends his own life by willed self-release to rejoin Indumatī, passing the Solar dynasty cleanly to Daśaratha _(actors: Aja, Daśaratha, Indumatī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/aja-indumati.md) — (Kālidāsa, Raghuvaṃśa cantos 8–9)
- **Atikāya and Rāvaṇa's sons slain** — Narāntaka, Devāntaka, Triśiras, Mahodara, Mahāpārśva, and the Brahmā-armored giant Atikāya (slain by Lakṣmaṇa with the brahmāstra) fall in turn _(actors: Lakṣmaṇa, Atikāya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 68–71)
- **Śatrughna slays Lavaṇāsura** — Rāma sends Śatrughna against the asura Lavaṇa; Śatrughna ambushes him when Lavaṇa has left behind his invincible Śiva-given trident and kills him with Rāma's divine arrow _(actors: Śatrughna, Lavaṇāsura, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bharata-shatrughna-and-their-wives.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa)
- **Ayodhya's Citizens Follow Rama into the River** — The people and even animals of Ayodhya enter the Sarayu behind their king and attain the higher worlds, the city dissolving en masse into liberation _(actors: citizens of Ayodhya, Rama)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/ayodhya.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Uttara-kanda)
- **Birth of Aja and Raghu's retirement** — Blessed by Kautsa, Raghu fathers his heir Aja, then in old age abdicates to Aja and dies as a yogin in contemplation; the senior Solar line is renamed Raghuvamsha after him _(actors: Raghu, Aja, Kautsa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/raghu.md) — (Raghuvamsha 5.36, 8.11-25; Vishnu Purana IV; Bhagavata Purana IX)
- **Birth of Rama and his brothers (Ramopakhyana)** — Vishnu is born as Rama, Bharata, Lakshmana, and Shatrughna to Dasharatha of Ayodhya, the childhood compressed without the putrakameshti elaboration _(actors: Rama, Bharata, Lakshmana, Shatrughna, Dasharatha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramopakhyana.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana Parva 259-260)
