- **Birth of Rāma and his brothers** — In Caitra the four princes are born — Rāma to Kausalyā, Bharata to Kaikeyī, and the twins Lakṣmaṇa and Śatrughna to Sumitrā — embodying portions of Viṣṇu _(actors: Rāma, Bharata, Lakṣmaṇa, Śatrughna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/1-bala-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 18)
- **Brahmā grants Viśvāmitra the title Rājarṣi** — After long southern austerity Viśvāmitra wins only the rank of rājarṣi, stinging him to redouble his penance _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Brahmā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vishwamitra-vasishtha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 57)
- **Ṛcīka's deferral boon** — Perceiving the swap by tapas, Ṛcīka relents at Satyavatī's plea and defers the misplaced kṣatriya ferocity from her son to her grandson _(actors: Ṛcīka, Satyavatī)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/gadhi-and-the-caru-swap.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 116; Viṣṇu Purāṇa IV.7)
- **Daśaratha bound by oath and the Raghu truth-vow forces the exile** — Sworn by oath before naming the price, Daśaratha collapses but cannot break the Raghu vow of word-keeping, and lets Kaikeyī pronounce Rāma's exile _(actors: Daśaratha, Kaikeyī, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dasharatha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Ayodhyā 10–19, 34)
- **Daśaratha's Aśvamedha** — King Daśaratha of Ayodhyā performs the great consecratory horse sacrifice that opens the Rāmāyaṇa, preceding the son-desiring rite _(actors: Dasharatha)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/yajnas-and-vedic-rituals.md) — (Ramayana Bala Kanda)
- **Dasharatha summons Rishyashringa for the putrakameshti** — The childless Dasharatha, on Sumantra's and Vasishtha's counsel, invites his son-in-law Rishyashringa from Anga to Ayodhya to officiate an heir-producing sacrifice _(actors: Dasharatha, Rishyashringa, Sumantra, Vasishtha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rishyashringa.md) — (Ramayana (Valmiki), Bala-kanda ~11)
- **Devastation of the Aśoka-vāṭikā** — Hanumān uproots and lays waste the grove, slays the warriors sent against him, and is bound by Indrajit's brahmāstra _(actors: Hanumān, Indrajit, rākṣasas)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/lanka-ramayana-trail-ashoka-vatika.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 41–48)
- **Ganga liberates the 60,000 sons** — Ganga flows behind Bhagiratha's chariot to the ocean and netherworld, washing the ashes of the 60,000 sons and granting them heaven _(actors: Ganga, Bhagiratha, sons of Sagara)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/ganga.md) — (Ramayana, Bala Kanda 44)
- **Garuḍa Frees Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa from the Nāgapāśa** — Garuḍa appears to Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa and dispels Indrajit's nāgapāśa serpent-arrows by his mere presence _(actors: Garuḍa, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Indrajit)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/garuda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa)
- **Grace and the boon of Candrahāsa** — Pleased, Śiva frees Rāvaṇa, restores his strength, and grants him the invincible sword Candrahāsa — the Rāvaṇānugraha grace _(actors: Śiva, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ravana-kailasa-ravananugraha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttarakāṇḍa 16)
- **Guha receives the exiles and they cross the Ganges** — The Niṣāda chief Guha hosts the exiles at Śṛṅgaverapura; Rāma mats his hair into ascetic locks, crosses the Ganges, and sends Sumantra back _(actors: Guha, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Sumantra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/2-ayodhya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 50–52)
- **Hariścandra sells his queen and son in Kāśī** — To raise the gold dakṣiṇā Viśvāmitra still demands, the destitute Hariścandra sells his queen Śaivyā and son Rohitāśva into bondage to an old brahmin in Kāśī _(actors: Hariścandra, Śaivyā, Rohitāśva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/hariscandra.md) — (Mārkaṇḍeya Purāṇa 8)
- **Identity of jīva and Brahman (Advaita core)** — Śiva teaches that the individual self is not other than Brahman/Śiva and liberation is realising 'I am That' _(actors: Śiva, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shiva-gita.md) — (Padma Purāṇa, Śiva-Gītā 10–11)
- **Kaikeyī delivers the exile sentence and the bark garments** — Kaikeyī announces the exile to Rāma's face, hurries his departure, and produces bark robes; Vasiṣṭha checks her attempt to make Sītā wear bark _(actors: Kaikeyī, Rāma, Sītā, Vasiṣṭha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kaikeyi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā 16–19, 37)
- **Kausalyā blesses Sītā** — The senior queen blesses Sītā as a daughter and instructs her in wifely dharma before the exile _(actors: Kausalyā, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kausalya.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 26, 39)
- **Kārtavīrya Arjuna's theft of the wish-cow** — The thousand-armed Haihaya king of Māhiṣmatī, feasted by the cow, covets and seizes her (or her calf) by force, wrecking Jamadagni's āśrama _(actors: Kārtavīrya Arjuna (Sahasrārjuna), Jamadagni, the wish-cow)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/jamadagni-parashurama.md) — (Mahābhārata 3.116; Bhāgavata Purāṇa 9.15)
- **Kubera's curse on the Pushpaka and Rama's homecoming** — Kubera curses that whoever slays Ravana shall ride the Pushpaka; after Ravana's death Rama flies home to Ayodhya in it with Sita, the homecoming celebrated at Dipavali, later restoring the vimana to Kubera _(actors: Kubera, Rama, Sita)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/kubera.md) — (Ramayana Yuddha Kanda; Ramopakhyana)
- **Maitreyī's question on wealth and immortality** — Maitreyī asks whether all the earth's wealth could make her immortal; Yājñavalkya replies there is no hope of immortality through wealth (amṛtatvasya tu nāśāsti vittena), and she asks instead for his knowledge _(actors: Maitreyī, Yājñavalkya)_ → [details](../07-acharyas-and-sampradayas/gargi-and-maitreyi.md) — (Bṛhadāraṇyaka Upaniṣad 2.4.2–3, 4.5.3–4)
- **Mārīca's dying cry in Rāma's voice** — Pierced by Rāma's arrow, the dying Mārīca mimics Rāma's voice crying for Sītā and Lakṣmaṇa, driving Lakṣmaṇa away and leaving Sītā unguarded for Rāvaṇa's abduction _(actors: Mārīca, Rāma, Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/maricha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 44-49)
- **Mārīca's golden-deer ruse** — Mārīca takes the form of a jewelled golden deer to lure Rāma away from the hut _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Mārīca, Sītā, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/panchavati-nashik-godavari.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 35–45)
- **Nishadraj Guha as model bhakta (devotional afterlife)** — Tulsidas and Kamban expand Guha into a model devotee whose low birth is dissolved by Rama's embrace, paired with Shabari and the Kevat as emblems that bhakti levels caste _(actors: Guha, Rama, Bharata)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/guha.md) — (Ramcharitmanas; Kamban Iramavataram)
- **Nārada frames the Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa** — Questioned by Vālmīki on the ideal man, Nārada narrates the Saṃkṣepa-rāmāyaṇa, instigating the epic _(actors: Nārada, Vālmīki)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/narada.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 1)
- **Purūravas institutes the three sacred fires and becomes a Gandharva** — By the gandharvas' boon Purūravas makes the śamī-aśvattha araṇi, kindles and establishes the three sacred fires, and ascends to gandharva-hood, rejoining Urvaśī _(actors: Purūravas, Gandharvas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/pururavas-urvashi.md) — (Shatapatha Brahmana XI.5.1; Bhagavata Purana IX.14; Vishnu Purana IV.6)
- **Putra-kameshti yajna & birth of Rama and brothers** — Childless Dasaratha performs a son-granting sacrifice; sacred payasa yields Rama, Bharata, Lakshmana, and Shatrughna _(actors: Dasaratha, Kausalya, Kaikeyi, Sumitra, Rishyashringa, Rama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/1-bala-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Bala Kanda))
- **Recognition of Lava and Kuśa by recitation** — The twins chant the Rāmāyaṇa at the sacrificial assembly and their song and faces reveal them as Rāma's sons in Vālmīki's gentle, non-martial climax _(actors: Lava, Kuśa, Rāma, Vālmīki)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttara-ramayana-lava-kusha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Uttara Kāṇḍa 93-94)
- **Rāma breaks the Setu at Dhanuṣkoṭi** — At Vibhīṣaṇa's request Rāma breaks the bridge with his bow-tip (or arrow) so it cannot be reused, naming the spot Dhanuṣ-koṭi _(actors: Rāma, Vibhīṣaṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/rameswaram-setu-bandha.md) — (Sethu Purāṇa; Rāmanātha sthala-purāṇa)
- **Rāma redeems Ahalyā** — Guided by Viśvāmitra, Rāma reaches the deserted hermitage and liberates Ahalyā — by his darśana in Vālmīki, by his foot-dust/touch in the dominant devotional tradition — the first overt sign of his redemptive divinity _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Viśvāmitra, Ahalyā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ahalya.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 48-49; Adhyātma Rāmāyaṇa; Rāmcaritmānas; Kamban Irāmāvatāram)
- **Rāma slays Khara, Dūṣaṇa, Triśiras and the 14,000** — Facing Khara's army single-handed, Rāma kills Dūṣaṇa, Triśiras, Khara, and all fourteen thousand demons of Janasthāna; the gods rain flowers _(actors: Rāma, Khara, Dūṣaṇa, Triśiras)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/3-aranya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya Kāṇḍa 24–30)
- **Rāvaṇa captured by Kārtavīrya Arjuna** — The thousand-armed Haihaya king Kārtavīrya Arjuna captures and binds Rāvaṇa until releasing him, showing a non-divine being could subdue him _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Kārtavīrya Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ravana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa)
- **Shurpanakha's mutilation and Khara-Dushana's destruction** — At Panchavati Lakshmana cuts off Shurpanakha's nose and ears, and Rama single-handedly annihilates Khara, Dushana and their host, clearing Janasthana (pivotal node detailed in the Panchavati file) _(actors: Shurpanakha, Lakshmana, Rama, Khara, Dushana)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kamyaka-dvaita-pandava-exile-forests.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Aranya-kanda 16-30)
- **Slaying of Tāṭakā — Rāma's first kill** — After disfiguring her arms, ears, and nose, Rāma kills the charging Tāṭakā with an arrow to the chest — his maiden slaying — as the gods rejoice _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Tāṭakā, Indra and the devas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/tataka.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 26; Rāmcaritmānas, Bāla Kāṇḍa)
- **Sītā captive and steadfast in the Aśoka grove** — Confined in the Aśokavanikā and guarded by rākṣasīs, Sītā fasts in grief, rejects Rāvaṇa's wooing and threats, and never wavers in fidelity to Rāma _(actors: Sītā, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sita.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa)
- **Sītā's Sand-Piṇḍa and Curse of the False Witnesses** — Sītā offers a piṇḍa of sand with five witnesses; when four lie and only the Akṣayavaṭa tells truth, she curses the Phalgu to run underground and the others, blessing the banyan with imperishability _(actors: Sītā, Rāma, Phalgu river, Akṣayavaṭa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/gaya-pitru-tirtha.md) — (local sthala-purana)
- **Sugrīva's coronation and Lakṣmaṇa's wrath** — Sugrīva is enthroned and Aṅgada made crown prince, but Sugrīva forgets his vow in pleasure until the enraged Lakṣmaṇa, cooled by Tārā, spurs him to mobilize _(actors: Sugrīva, Aṅgada, Lakṣmaṇa, Tārā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sugriva-and-vali.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa)
- **Surveying the battlefield with Sītā** — Trijaṭā takes Sītā to a high vantage to view the war, naming warriors and reporting Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa unharmed _(actors: Trijaṭā, Sītā, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/trijata.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa; Rāmcaritmānas)
- **The cow's-ear shape and place-name** — Rāvaṇa twists the fixed liṅga into a cow's-ear (go-karṇa) form, giving the liṅga its shape and the kṣetra its name _(actors: Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/gokarna-mahabaleshwar-atma-linga.md) — (Gokarṇa-māhātmya)
- **The exile plot succeeds** — Bound by his word, Daśaratha grants the boons; Rāma accepts exile, triggering the whole onward epic _(actors: Daśaratha, Rāma, Kaikeyī, Mantharā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/manthara.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 12–19)
- **The four-quarter search for Sītā launched** — Sugrīva dispatches vānara armies to the four directions to find Sītā within one month; Rāma gives Hanumān his signet ring as a token for Sītā _(actors: Sugrīva, Hanumān, Aṅgada, Jāmbavān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/4-kishkindha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā Kāṇḍa 40–44)
- **The golden deer (māyā-mṛga)** — Mārīca becomes a jewelled golden deer luring Rāma away from the hut while Lakṣmaṇa is posted to guard Sītā _(actors: Mārīca, Rāma, Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/panchavati-nashik-godavari.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 42–43)
- **The Jayanta-crow at Sphaṭik Śilā** — Indra's son Jayanta in crow-form pecks Sītā's foot at the crystal rock and is punished by Rāma's brahmāstra-reed, losing one eye _(actors: Rāma, Sītā, Jayanta (crow))_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/chitrakoot-kamadgiri.md) — (Rāmcaritmānas, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa; Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara-kāṇḍa 38)
- **The jīvanmukta described** — Rāma describes the one liberated-in-life, free of egoism and doership, untouched while prārabdha karma plays out _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rama-gita.md) — (Adhyātma Rāmāyaṇa 7.5)
- **The Lakṣmaṇa-rekhā and Lakṣmaṇa's departure** — Provoked by Mārīca's false cry in Rāma's voice and Sītā's accusation, Lakṣmaṇa leaves to find Rāma after drawing a protective line Sītā must not cross _(actors: Lakṣmaṇa, Sītā, Mārīca, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/lakshmana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa; Rāmcaritmānas)
- **The people of Ayodhyā enter the Sarayū and attain heaven** — The citizens of Ayodhyā — men, women, even animals — enter the Sarayū at Gopratāra behind their king and attain the higher worlds (the Santānaka heaven) _(actors: The people and animals of Ayodhyā, Rāma)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/sarayu-ayodhya-river.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa)
- **The Śrīraṅgam Ranganātha legend** — Carrying the Raṅga-vimāna icon of Viṣṇu gifted by Rāma, Vibhīṣaṇa sets it down at Śrīraṅgam, where it roots forever facing south toward Laṅkā _(actors: Vibhīṣaṇa, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/vibhishana.md) — (Śrīraṅgam Ranganāthaswāmy sthala-purāṇa)
- **The slaying of Vāli** — At the second challenge, ignoring Tārā's warning, Vāli fights Sugrīva and Rāma shoots him from concealment, triggering the epic's most-debated dharma-dilemma _(actors: Rāma, Vāli, Sugrīva, Tārā)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kishkindha-hampi-anegundi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa 16–18)
- **Trijaṭā's prophetic dream** — The aged rākṣasī Trijaṭā recounts an omen-dream foretelling Laṅkā's burning, Rāvaṇa's death, and Rāma's triumph, restraining the others from harming Sītā _(actors: Trijaṭā, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/5-sundara-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 27)
- **Trivikrama Measures the Worlds** — Vāmana swells into the cosmic Trivikrama and with two strides covers earth and the heavens; his toe pierces the universe's shell, releasing the celestial Gaṅgā, leaving no space for the third pace _(actors: Trivikrama (Viṣṇu), Bali)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vamana-bali.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 8.20–8.21; Ṛgveda 1.154)
- **Vishvamitra adopts Shunahshepa as Devarata** — The officiating Hotr Vishvamitra adopts the rescued boy as eldest son, renaming him Devarata, and curses the fifty resisting sons to outcaste descent _(actors: Vishvamitra, Shunahshepa/Devarata, Vishvamitra's hundred sons)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/vedas/brahmanas/aitareya-brahmana.md) — (Aitareya Brahmana 7.18)
- **Vishvamitra adopts Sunahshepa as Devarata** — Vishvamitra adopts the rescued boy, renames him Devarata, and makes him eldest heir to his Vedic line; Sunahshepa rejects Ajigarta as no father _(actors: Vishvamitra, Sunahshepa, Ajigarta)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sunahshepa.md) — (Aitareya Brahmana 7.17-18)
- **Vishvamitra builds the counter-heaven and suspends Trishanku** — Vishvamitra halts the fall mid-air and creates a parallel southern heaven with new stars and a new Indra; Brahma brokers a truce leaving Trishanku fixed head-down among the new stars _(actors: Vishvamitra, Trishanku, Brahma, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/trishanku.md) — (Ramayana Bala Kanda 60)
- **Viśvāmitra Renounces His Throne for Tapas** — Humiliated that brahma-tejas crushed his kṣatra-bala in the cow-quarrel, Viśvāmitra renounces his kingdom and undertakes the austerities that make him a Brahmarṣi _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Vasiṣṭha)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/kamadhenu-and-divine-cattle.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla-kāṇḍa)
- **Wayfinding to Pampā and Śabarī, then ascent** — Kabandha gives the route west then south to Pampā lake and Mataṅga's hermitage where Śabarī awaits, circles Rāma in reverence, and ascends to heaven _(actors: Kabandha (gandharva), Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Śabarī (referenced))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kabandha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 73)
- **Śabarī directs Rāma to Sugrīva** — Before departing, Śabarī shows Rāma the hermitage and directs him to Lake Pampā and Sugrīva on Ṛṣyamūka, handing the narrative on to the Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa _(actors: Śabarī, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/shabari.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Araṇya-kāṇḍa 74–75)
- **Abduction of Sītā and death of Jaṭāyu** — Mārīca's golden-deer ruse lures Rāma away; Rāvaṇa abducts Sītā to Laṅkā and the vulture Jaṭāyu dies trying to stop him _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Sītā, Mārīca, Jaṭāyu)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa 3 (Araṇya Kāṇḍa))
- **Abduction of Sītā by Rāvaṇa** — Disguised as a mendicant, Rāvaṇa seizes the undefended Sītā and carries her off in the puṣpaka _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Sītā)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/panchavati-nashik-godavari.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 46–49)
- **Śatrughna founds Madhurā/Mathurā** — On the Madhuvana site of Lavaṇa's realm, Śatrughna raises the city of Madhurā (Mathurā) and rules it for twelve years _(actors: Śatrughna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bharata-shatrughna-and-their-wives.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa)
- **Bali's Surrender and Exaltation to Sutala** — Bali offers his own head for the third stride; interceded for by Prahlāda and Vindhyāvali, he is sent to rule Sutala as an immortal and future Indra of the Sāvarṇi Manvantara, with Viṣṇu as his gatekeeper _(actors: Bali, Viṣṇu, Prahlāda, Vindhyāvali, Garuḍa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vamana-bali.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 8.21–8.23)
- **Bharata's wrath at Mantharā** — On learning the truth Bharata is enraged at Mantharā; in some versions she is seized and beaten before being spared _(actors: Bharata, Śatrughna, Mantharā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/manthara.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 78 (with regional amplifications))
- **Bhārgava line gains the warrior-seed** — Satyavatī bears the serene sage Jamadagni, whose son Paraśurāma manifests the deferred kṣatriya ferocity and exterminates the kṣatriyas _(actors: Satyavatī, Jamadagni, Reṇukā, Paraśurāma)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/gadhi-and-the-caru-swap.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 115–117; Viṣṇu Purāṇa IV.7, IV.11; Bhāgavata IX.15–16)
- **Birth of Āyu and the six sons** — Through the annual reunions Urvaśī bears Purūravas six sons, chief among them Āyu, the heir through whom the Lunar line continues to Nahuṣa and Yayāti _(actors: Purūravas, Urvaśī, Āyu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/pururavas-urvashi.md) — (Vishnu Purana IV.6; Bhagavata Purana IX.14)
- **Daśaratha's death** — Daśaratha dies of grief over the exile, his death laid at Kaikeyī's feet _(actors: Daśaratha, Kaikeyī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kaikeyi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā 64)
- **Daśaratha's death by grief and deathbed confession** — On the sixth night after Rāma's departure Daśaratha dies of grief crying Rāma's name, having confessed the Śravaṇa curse to Kausalyā as it comes to term _(actors: Daśaratha, Kausalyā, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dasharatha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Ayodhyā 63–64; Kālidāsa Raghuvaṃśa 12)
- **Death of Dasharatha** — Grief-stricken at Rama's departure into exile, King Dasharatha dies _(actors: Dasharatha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/README.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Ayodhya Kanda)
- **Descent of Ganga and Sagara's sons** — Among the Solar dynasty legends, Sagara's 60,000 sons perish and the descent of Ganga is brought about to redeem them _(actors: Sagara, Sagara's sons, Ganga)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/vishnu-purana.md) — (Vishnu Purana (Amsha 4))
- **Descent of the Gangā (Gangāvataraṇa)** — After Kapila burns Sagara's 60,000 sons to ash, their descendant Bhagiratha performs millennia of tapas to bring Ganga from heaven; Shiva catches her crashing flow in his matted locks to spare the earth and liberate the ashes _(actors: Bhagiratha, Shiva, Ganga, Sagara, Kapila)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/sapta-puri-and-sacred-cities.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Bala-kanda); Mahabharata; Bhagavata Purana)
- **Eleven-year residence and departure** — After eleven-plus years — the longest single exile residence — rākṣasa disturbances drive Rāma's party from Citrakūṭa toward Atri's āśrama and Daṇḍaka/Pañcavaṭī _(actors: Rāma, Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/chitrakoot-kamadgiri.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 1; pilgrimage tradition)
- **First Sañjīvanī flight: Hanumān carries the mountain** — After Indrajit's brahmāstra fells the host, Hanumān flies to the Himālaya and, unable to tell the healing herbs apart, carries the whole Droṇagiri mountain back to revive the fallen _(actors: Hanumān, Jāmbavān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 73–74)
- **First slaying of Kārtavīrya Arjuna** — Paraśurāma pursues to Māhiṣmatī, severs the king's thousand arms, beheads him, and recovers the cow — the opening blow of the kṣatriya war _(actors: Paraśurāma, Kārtavīrya Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/jamadagni-parashurama.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 9.15–16; Mahābhārata 3.116)
- **Hanuman conceived as Rudravatara** — The vayu-borne portion of Shiva is placed in Anjana's womb, conceiving the vanara hero Hanuman, the fullest womb-born Shiva-descent and supreme devotee of Rama _(actors: Hanuman (Shiva), Anjana)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/shiva-avatars-and-forms.md) — (Shiva Purana Satarudra Samhita; Ramayana)
- **Hanuman reveals himself to Sita with Rama's ring** — Hanuman presents Rama's signet ring as a token, offers to carry Sita back (she refuses), and receives her crest-jewel for Rama _(actors: Hanuman, Sita, Rama)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/hanuman.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Sundara Kanda 31-40)
- **Hanumān gives Sītā Rāma's signet ring** — Hanumān reveals himself, recites Rāma's story, and presents Rāma's named signet ring as proof; Sītā weeps with joy and questions him about Rāma _(actors: Hanumān, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/5-sundara-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 35–36)
- **Hanumān meets Sītā and exchanges tokens** — Hanumān finds Sītā in the grove, gives Rāma's signet ring as proof, and receives her crest-jewel; she refuses rescue, insisting Rāma win her honorably _(actors: Sītā, Hanumān, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sita.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa)
- **Hariścandra sells himself to a Cāṇḍāla** — The remainder of the fee is exacted by selling Hariścandra himself to a Cāṇḍāla keeper of the cremation ground — revealed to be Yama/Dharma in disguise — who sets him to collect the death-tax from every corpse _(actors: Hariścandra, Cāṇḍāla, Yama/Dharma, Viśvāmitra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/hariscandra.md) — (Mārkaṇḍeya Purāṇa 8)
- **Hosting Rama at the Prayaga ashram en route to Chitrakuta** — At his hermitage at the Ganga-Yamuna confluence, Bharadvaja welcomes the exiled Rama, Sita and Lakshmana and counsels them to settle at Chitrakuta _(actors: Bharadvaja, Rama, Sita, Lakshmana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bharadvaja.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Ayodhya Kanda ~54-56)
- **Jahnu swallows and re-releases Ganga** — When Ganga floods sage Jahnu's hermitage he drinks the whole river in one gulp, then releases her from his ear, fathering her anew as Jahnavi _(actors: Jahnu, Ganga)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/ganga.md) — (Ramayana, Bala Kanda 44)
- **Jñāna-yoga and the jīvanmukta** — Śiva expounds knowledge-yoga, dispassion, sense-control, and the marks of the liberated-in-life _(actors: Śiva, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shiva-gita.md) — (Padma Purāṇa, Śiva-Gītā 12–13)
- **Kausalyā rebukes Daśaratha** — In anguish after Rāma departs, Kausalyā reproaches Daśaratha for his weakness before Kaikeyī, yet does not abandon him _(actors: Kausalyā, Daśaratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kausalya.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 42–43)
- **Kārtavīrya-Arjuna Seizes Jamadagni's Cow** — The thousand-armed Haihaya emperor Kārtavīrya-Arjuna, feasted by Jamadagni's wish-cow, seizes her (or her calf) by force and wrecks the hermitage _(actors: Kārtavīrya-Arjuna, Jamadagni)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/kamadhenu-and-divine-cattle.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva; Bhāgavata, Padma, Brahmāṇḍa Purāṇas)
- **Kusha Founds the Nageshvaranatha Temple** — Rama's son Kusha founds the Shiva shrine Nageshvaranatha at Ayodhya after recovering an armlet from a naga-maiden of the Sarayu _(actors: Kusha)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/ayodhya.md) — (sthala-tradition)
- **Laṅkā-dahana (the burning of Laṅkā)** — His tail set alight, Hanumān burns Laṅkā, quenches the fire in the sea, and recrosses to report to Rāma _(actors: Hanumān, rākṣasas)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/lanka-ramayana-trail-ashoka-vatika.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 52–55)
- **Later tradition: daughter of Vibhīṣaṇa and bhakti reframing** — Later tradition makes Trijaṭā the daughter of Vibhīṣaṇa and Saramā and recasts her as a devotee of Rāma in the Adhyātma Rāmāyaṇa and Rāmcaritmānas _(actors: Trijaṭā, Vibhīṣaṇa, Saramā, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/trijata.md) — (Adhyātma Rāmāyaṇa; Rāmcaritmānas; later Rāmāyaṇa tradition)
- **Meghanada captures Indra (Indrajit)** — Ravana's son Meghanada captures Indra and earns the name Indrajit _(actors: Meghanada/Indrajit, Indra)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/indra.md) — (Ramayana)
- **Nalakubara curses Ravana** — After Ravana violates Rambha, Kubera's son Nalakubara curses Ravana that he can never force an unwilling woman on pain of his head bursting, a curse later credited with sparing Sita in captivity _(actors: Nalakubara, Ravana, Rambha, Sita)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/kubera.md) — (Ramayana Uttara Kanda)
- **Nārada prompts Vālmīki's composition of the Rāmāyaṇa** — Vālmīki questions Nārada about the perfectly virtuous man; Nārada recites the essence of Rāma's life, seeding the composition of the Rāmāyaṇa _(actors: Nārada, Vālmīki)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/narada.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 1–2)
- **Pampā-sarovar — Rāma meets Śabarī** — In the Kiṣkindhā country Rāma meets the devotee Śabarī at Pampā-sarovara and is guided toward Sugrīva and Hanumān in his search for Sītā _(actors: Rāma; Śabarī; Hanumān)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/panch-sarovar-sacred-lakes.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa, Āraṇya/Kiṣkindhā Kāṇḍas)
- **Parashurama annihilates the Kshatriyas twenty-one times** — Parashurama beheads and restores his mother at his father's word and destroys the Kshatriyas twenty-one times to avenge Jamadagni, filling the lakes of Samanta-panchaka _(actors: Parashurama, Jamadagni, Kartavirya Arjuna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/the-chiranjivis.md) — (Mahabharata; Bhagavata Purana)
- **Phala-śruti and close of the Gītā** — The Gītā closes with the fruit-declaration that whoever recites it crosses saṃsāra and attains Rāma's supreme abode _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rama-gita.md) — (Adhyātma Rāmāyaṇa 7.5)
- **Puñjikasthalā cursed to become Añjanā, mother of Hanumān** — The apsarā Puñjikasthalā is cursed to be born as the vānara princess Añjanā; Durvāsa softens the curse so she weds Kesari and bears Hanumān before being freed _(actors: Puñjikasthalā/Añjanā, Kesari, Durvāsa, Hanumān)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/apsaras.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa; Purāṇic Hanumān traditions)
- **Putrakāmeṣṭi and the birth of Rāma and his brothers** — Ṛṣyaśṛṅga performs the son-desiring sacrifice for childless Daśaratha; Agni rises with pāyasa divided among the queens, begetting Rāma, Bharata, Lakṣmaṇa, and Śatrughna as portions of Viṣṇu _(actors: Rishyashringa, Dasharatha, Agni, Kausalya, Kaikeyi, Sumitra, Rama, Bharata, Lakshmana, Shatrughna)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/yajnas-and-vedic-rituals.md) — (Ramayana Bala Kanda 14-18)
- **Rama's exile and Dasharatha's death (Ramopakhyana)** — Kaikeyi's two boons force Rama's banishment, Dasharatha dies, and Bharata takes up the regency, told in telescoped form _(actors: Rama, Kaikeyi, Dasharatha, Bharata)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramopakhyana.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana Parva 261)
- **Reunion of Ahalyā and Gautama** — Gautama returns, his wrath spent and the appointed time fulfilled, accepts the purified Ahalyā, and the couple resume married ascetic life _(actors: Gautama, Ahalyā, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ahalya.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 49)
- **Rishyashringa performs the Ashvamedha and Putrakameshti** — Rishyashringa conducts Dasharatha's Ashvamedha on the Sarayu's north bank, then the son-desiring Putrakameshti to obtain heirs _(actors: Rishyashringa, Dasharatha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rishyashringa.md) — (Ramayana (Valmiki), Bala-kanda ~13-14)
- **Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa bound by the Nāgapāśa** — Indrajit binds both Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa with the serpent-weapon Nāgapāśa; they are freed when Garuḍa arrives and the snakes flee _(actors: Indrajit, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Garuḍa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/lakshmana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha-kāṇḍa)
- **Rāma surveys from Gandhamādana Parvatam** — Rāma is said to have viewed the bridge-building and Laṅkā from the island's highest point, where his footprints (pādukā) are enshrined _(actors: Rāma, Hanumān)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/rameswaram-setu-bandha.md) — (Rāmanātha sthala-purāṇa (regional))
- **Śūrpaṇakhā incites Rāvaṇa** — Śūrpaṇakhā reaches Laṅkā and goads Rāvaṇa, describing Sītā's beauty and planting the desire that will doom him _(actors: Śūrpaṇakhā, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/3-aranya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya Kāṇḍa 32–34)
- **Settling at Chitrakūṭa** — On Bharadvāja's advice the exiles reach Chitrakūṭa on the Mandākinī, where Lakṣmaṇa builds a leaf hermitage and forest life begins _(actors: Rāma, Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa, Bharadvāja)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/2-ayodhya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 54–56)
- **Sugrīva enthroned, lapse, and Lakṣmaṇa's wrath** — Sugrīva is consecrated king of the vānaras with Aṅgada as heir, then lapses into pleasure forgetting his vow until Lakṣmaṇa's furious entry recalls him to duty _(actors: Sugrīva, Lakṣmaṇa, Aṅgada)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kishkindha-hampi-anegundi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa 25–33)
- **Svayamprabhā aids the southern party** — Trapped in the magical cave Ṛkṣabila, the famished southern party is fed by the ascetic Svayamprabhā, who transports them out to the seashore _(actors: Svayamprabhā, Aṅgada, Hanumān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/4-kishkindha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā Kāṇḍa 50–53)
- **Tapas for and grant of the Ātma-liṅga** — By fierce tapas offering his heads, Rāvaṇa wins from Śiva the Ātma-liṅga to make Laṅkā invincible, on condition it never touch the ground (with the Vaidyanātha head-healing variant) _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Śiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ravana-kailasa-ravananugraha.md) — (Gokarṇa-māhātmya; Skanda Purāṇa (Sahyādri-khaṇḍa); Śiva Purāṇa)
- **The cast-off coverings become subsidiary shrines** — Rāvaṇa hurls away the casket, lid, string, and cloth, which fall to form the Dhāreśvara, Guṇavanteśvara, Murudeśvara, and Śejjeśvara liṅgas _(actors: Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/gokarna-mahabaleshwar-atma-linga.md) — (Gokarṇa-māhātmya; coastal Karnataka tradition)
- **The false cry and Sītā sending Lakṣmaṇa away** — Dying Mārīca cries out in Rāma's voice; Sītā drives Lakṣmaṇa to leave her, the moment later elaborated as the Lakṣmaṇa-rekhā _(actors: Mārīca, Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/panchavati-nashik-godavari.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 44–45; Rāmcaritmānas-stream (Lakṣmaṇa-rekhā variant))
- **The four-quarter search and Sampāti's tidings** — Sugrīva dispatches vānara parties to the four quarters; the southern party, after the Svayamprabhā cave, learns from the vulture Sampāti that Sītā is held in Laṅkā _(actors: Sugrīva, Aṅgada, Hanumān, Jāmbavān, Sampāti)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sugriva-and-vali.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa)
- **The Lava-Kuśa war (capture of the sacrificial horse)** — In the Purāṇic/folk amplification the boys seize the wandering horse and defeat Rāma's army and brothers, binding Hanumān, before the truth emerges _(actors: Lava, Kuśa, Śatrughna, Lakṣmaṇa, Bharata, Hanumān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttara-ramayana-lava-kusha.md) — (Padma Purāṇa Pātāla-khaṇḍa 11, 39, 46)
- **The ātman discourse — 'for love of the Self'** — Yājñavalkya teaches Maitreyī that the husband, wife, wealth and worlds are dear not for their own sake but for love of the Self (ātman), which alone is to be seen, heard, reflected on and meditated upon; illustrated by the lump-of-salt simile _(actors: Yājñavalkya, Maitreyī)_ → [details](../07-acharyas-and-sampradayas/gargi-and-maitreyi.md) — (Bṛhadāraṇyaka Upaniṣad 2.4.5–14, 4.5.6–15)
- **Triśaṅku cursed to become a cāṇḍāla** — King Triśaṅku, wishing to ascend bodily to heaven, is refused by Vasiṣṭha and cursed to outcaste form by Vasiṣṭha's insulted sons _(actors: Satyavrata Triśaṅku, Vasiṣṭha's sons)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vishwamitra-vasishtha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 57-59)
- **Twenty-one-fold extermination of the kṣatriyas** — Paraśurāma vows to rid the earth of kṣatriyas twenty-one times and slaughters the tyrannical warrior kings worldwide _(actors: Paraśurāma, the kṣatriya kings)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/parashurama.md) — (Mahābhārata 3.117; Bhāgavata Purāṇa 9.16)
- **Vishvamitra's hundred sons split and the elder fifty cursed** — Vishvamitra orders his hundred sons to accept Devarata as eldest; the fifty younger agree and are blessed, the fifty elder refuse and are cursed to father the border peoples _(actors: Vishvamitra, Devarata)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sunahshepa.md) — (Aitareya Brahmana 7.18)
- **Vishvamitra's request & slaying of Tataka and Subahu** — Vishvamitra takes Rama and Lakshmana to guard his sacrifice; Rama kills the rakshasi Tataka, gets celestial astras, hurls Marica and slays Subahu _(actors: Vishvamitra, Rama, Lakshmana, Tataka, Marica, Subahu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/1-bala-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Bala Kanda))
- **Viśvāmitra confers the divine astras** — Pleased by the slaying, Viśvāmitra bestows on Rāma the full arsenal of celestial weapons and their recall mantras, initiating him into divine weapon-craft _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/tataka.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 27-28)
- **Viśvāmitra takes Rāma to guard his sacrifice** — The sage Viśvāmitra arrives at Ayodhyā and demands the boy Rāma to protect his sacrifice from rākṣasas; Daśaratha relents on Vasiṣṭha's counsel _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Daśaratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/1-bala-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 18–20)
- **Viśvāmitra wins the title Brahmarṣi** — Having conquered his wrath, Viśvāmitra is humbly acknowledged by Vasiṣṭha as a brahmarṣi, the sole kṣatriya to earn the rank _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Vasiṣṭha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vishwamitra-vasishtha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 65)
- **Vālī humiliates Rāvaṇa** — The vānara king Vālī tucks Rāvaṇa under his arm during his sandhyā worship, humbling the rākṣasa emperor _(actors: Vālī, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ravana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa)
- **Bharata's Gandharva conquest and city-foundings** — At Yudhājit's prompting Bharata wars on the Gandharvas of Gāndhāra and founds Takṣaśilā for his son Takṣa and Puṣkalāvatī for Puṣkala _(actors: Bharata, Māṇḍavī, Takṣa, Puṣkala, Yudhājit, the Gandharvas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bharata-shatrughna-and-their-wives.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa)
- **Bharata's Regency with Rama's Sandals** — Refusing the throne, Bharata installs Rama's sandals as regent and rules as caretaker until Rama's return _(actors: Bharata, Rama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/README.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Ayodhya Kanda)
- **Bharata's repudiation of Kaikeyī** — Bharata denounces his mother, refuses the throne, and installs Rāma's sandals as sovereign at Nandigrāma _(actors: Bharata, Kaikeyī, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kaikeyi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā 72–83, 112–115)
- **Birth of Vasiṣṭha and Agastya from Urvaśī's catalyst** — At the sight of Urvaśī the seed of Mitra-Varuṇa spills into a pot, producing the sages Vasiṣṭha and Agastya — a cross-myth with Urvaśī as catalyst _(actors: Mitra, Varuṇa, Urvaśī, Vasiṣṭha, Agastya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/pururavas-urvashi.md) — (Rgveda VII.33; Bhagavata Purana VI.18)
- **Daśaratha recalls the Śravaṇa-kumāra curse** — The dying king recalls killing the boy Śravaṇa by sound while hunting, and the boy's blind parents' curse that he too would die of grief for a son _(actors: Daśaratha, Śravaṇa Kumāra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/2-ayodhya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 63–64)
- **Daśaratha's confession of the Śravaṇa curse** — The dying king tells Kausalyā how he killed the ascetic boy Śravaṇa and was cursed to die of grief for a son _(actors: Daśaratha, Kausalyā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kausalya.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 63–64)
- **Death of Rohitāśva and the test at the pyre** — The boy Rohitāśva dies of snakebite, and at the cremation ground Hariścandra, bound by duty to his master, demands the cremation fee even from his unrecognized grieving wife — the supreme test of his truth _(actors: Rohitāśva, Hariścandra, Śaivyā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/hariscandra.md) — (Mārkaṇḍeya Purāṇa 8)
- **Defeat of her sons at Siddhāśrama** — At Viśvāmitra's sacrifice Rāma hurls Mārīca into the sea with the Mānava astra and kills Subāhu with the Āgneya astra, completing the confrontation with Tāṭakā's line _(actors: Rāma, Mārīca, Subāhu, Viśvāmitra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/tataka.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 30)
- **Five blood-lakes at Samanta-pañcaka** — At Samanta-pañcaka (Kurukṣetra) Paraśurāma fills five pools with kṣatriya blood and offers tarpaṇa to his ancestors, who bid him relent _(actors: Paraśurāma, the Bhṛgu ancestors)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/parashurama.md) — (Mahābhārata 3.117; cf. 9.53)
- **Gaṇeśa's trick and the rooting at Gokarṇa** — The devas send Gaṇeśa as a cowherd-boy and Viṣṇu fakes dusk; the boy sets the liṅga down, rooting it at Gokarṇa as Mahābaleśvara, with the scattered-wrappings coastal liṅga-chain as a variant _(actors: Gaṇeśa, Viṣṇu, Rāvaṇa, devas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ravana-kailasa-ravananugraha.md) — (Gokarṇa-māhātmya; Skanda Purāṇa (Sahyādri-khaṇḍa))
- **Hanuman burns Lanka** — After uprooting the Ashoka grove, slaying Aksha, and being captured by Indrajit's Brahmastra, Hanuman has his tail set ablaze and uses it to set the golden city aflame _(actors: Hanuman, Aksha, Indrajit, Ravana)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/hanuman.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Sundara Kanda 41-54)
- **Hanumān's leap to Laṅkā** — Reminded of his powers by Jāmbavān, Hanumān leaps the sea to Laṅkā, carrying the epic into the Sundara-kāṇḍa _(actors: Hanumān, Jāmbavān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sugriva-and-vali.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa)
- **Indrajit beheads the illusory Māyā-Sītā** — To demoralize the army Indrajit conjures an illusory Sītā and beheads her before Hanumān, until Vibhīṣaṇa reveals it was sorcery _(actors: Indrajit, Hanumān, Vibhīṣaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 80)
- **Indra steals Ambarisha's sacrificial victim (Ramayana variant)** — In the epic retelling, Indra carries off King Ambarisha's consecrated victim and the priest demands a human substitute be procured _(actors: Indra, Ambarisha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sunahshepa.md) — (Ramayana Bala-kanda 61)
- **Kuśika line gains the sage-seed** — The queen-mother bears Viśvāmitra, a kṣatriya by birth driven by brāhmaṇa-tejas to win brahmarṣi rank, completing the mirror cross-over of the two clans _(actors: Gādhi's queen, Viśvāmitra)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/gadhi-and-the-caru-swap.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva 116–117; Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa)
- **Lakṣmaṇa felled and revived by the Sañjīvanī herb** — Indrajit's weapon leaves Lakṣmaṇa mortally wounded; Hanumān carries the entire Droṇagiri mountain so the physician Suṣeṇa can revive him with the Sañjīvanī herb _(actors: Lakṣmaṇa, Indrajit, Suṣeṇa, Hanumān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/lakshmana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha-kāṇḍa)
- **Launch of the four-direction search and Sampāti's revelation** — Sugrīva dispatches vānara parties to the four quarters to find Sītā; the southern party under Aṅgada, Hanumān and Jāmbavān learns from the dying vulture Sampāti that Sītā is held in Laṅkā _(actors: Sugrīva, Aṅgada, Hanumān, Jāmbavān, Sampāti)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kishkindha-hampi-anegundi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa 37–66)
- **Mārīca becomes the golden deer** — Coerced by Rāvaṇa, Mārīca becomes a dazzling golden deer to lure Rāma away from the hermitage _(actors: Mārīca, Rāvaṇa, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/3-aranya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya Kāṇḍa 40–43)
- **Murder of Jamadagni** — In Paraśurāma's absence the sons of Kārtavīrya behead the meditating, unresisting Jamadagni and carry off his head; Reṇukā strikes her breast twenty-one times, fixing the number of the vengeance _(actors: Sons of Kārtavīrya Arjuna, Jamadagni, Reṇukā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/jamadagni-parashurama.md) — (Mahābhārata 3.116–117; Bhāgavata Purāṇa 9.16)
- **Paraśurāma's Vengeance and the Annihilation of the Kṣatriyas** — Paraśurāma severs Kārtavīrya's thousand arms and beheads him; after the king's sons murder Jamadagni, he vows to rid the earth of kṣatriyas and exterminates the warrior race twenty-one times _(actors: Paraśurāma, Kārtavīrya-Arjuna, Jamadagni)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/kamadhenu-and-divine-cattle.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva; Bhāgavata Purāṇa)
- **Rāma allies with Sugrīva and kills Vālī** — Rāma allies with the monkey-king Sugrīva, slays his usurping brother Vālī from concealment, and gains the vānara army; Hanumān becomes chief agent _(actors: Rāma, Sugrīva, Vālī, Hanumān)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa 4 (Kiṣkindhā Kāṇḍa))
- **Rāma's Pañcavaṭī exile on the Godāvarī** — Rāma, Sītā and Lakṣmaṇa settle at Pañcavaṭī (Nāsik) on the Godāvarī, the stage of the Śūrpaṇakhā mutilation, golden-deer ruse and Sītā's abduction _(actors: Rāma, Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa, Śūrpaṇakhā, Mārīca, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/godavari-gautami-dakshina-ganga.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa (Araṇya-kāṇḍa))
- **Sampāti reveals Sītā's location** — Hearing of his brother Jaṭāyu's heroic death, the aged vulture Sampāti uses his far-sight to descry Sītā captive in Rāvaṇa's Aśoka grove in Laṅkā, and his wings regrow as a dharmic reward _(actors: Sampāti, Aṅgada)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/4-kishkindha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā Kāṇḍa 56–63)
- **Sarasvatī confounds Mantharā's tongue (deva-instrument variant)** — In Tulsīdās and devotional traditions the gods prompt Sarasvatī to turn Mantharā's intellect so the exile—needed for Rāvaṇa's death—is set in motion _(actors: Sarasvatī, the gods, Mantharā, (re: Rāma, Rāvaṇa))_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/manthara.md) — (Tulsīdās, Rāmacaritamānasa, Ayodhyā-kāṇḍa; deva-instrument traditions)
- **Shurpanakha mutilated and Sita abducted (Ramopakhyana)** — In the Dandaka forest Shurpanakha is mutilated, Khara and Dushana's host destroyed, Marica's golden-deer ruse draws Rama away, Ravana abducts Sita, and Jatayu falls defending her _(actors: Rama, Lakshmana, Shurpanakha, Khara, Dushana, Marica, Ravana, Sita, Jatayu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramopakhyana.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana Parva 262-263)
- **Sītā gives Hanumān the cūḍāmaṇi and secret sign** — Declining to be carried back, Sītā gives Hanumān her crest-jewel and recalls the Kākāsura crow-demon episode as a private sign only Rāma would know _(actors: Sītā, Hanumān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/5-sundara-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 36–38)
- **Sītā-haraṇa (abduction by Rāvaṇa)** — Rāvaṇa, disguised as a sannyāsin, seizes the undefended Sītā and carries her off in the puṣpaka — the pivot of the epic, seated at Pañcavaṭī _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Sītā)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/panchavati-nashik-godavari.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 46–49)
- **Sītā's agni-parīkṣā** — After Rāvaṇa's fall Rāma voices public doubt; Sītā enters a pyre to prove her purity, and Agni rises bodily to return her unharmed _(actors: Sītā, Rāma, Agni, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sita.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa)
- **Sītā's second public oath and return to the Earth** — Asked to swear her purity once more, Sītā calls on her mother the Earth, who rises on a nāga-borne throne and receives her forever, her unanswerable verdict _(actors: Sītā, Rāma, Bhūmi-Devī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttara-ramayana-lava-kusha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Uttara Kāṇḍa 96-97)
- **The divine payasa and birth of the four princes** — The Yajna-purusha rises from the fire bearing payasa, which divided among the queens produces Rama, Bharata, Lakshmana, and Shatrughna, Vishnu's incarnation to slay Ravana _(actors: Yajna-purusha, Kausalya, Kaikeyi, Sumitra, Rama, Bharata, Lakshmana, Shatrughna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/rishyashringa.md) — (Ramayana (Valmiki), Bala-kanda ~16-18)
- **The miraculous feast for Bharata's army** — When Bharata halts with the army of Ayodhya, Bharadvaja conjures by tapas a divine feast, palace and entertainers to host the entire host _(actors: Bharadvaja, Bharata, the army of Ayodhya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bharadvaja.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Ayodhya Kanda ~91)
- **The preserved body, Bharata's repudiation, and the funeral** — With heirs absent the king's body is preserved until Bharata returns, performs the rites, denounces Kaikeyī, and rules as regent for Rāma's sandals _(actors: Daśaratha (deceased), Bharata, Kaikeyī, Vasiṣṭha, the ministers)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/dasharatha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Ayodhyā 66–83, 104–112)
- **Śukra blinded blocking Vāmana's water-pot** — When Bali insists on granting Vāmana three paces, Śukra shrinks into the water-pot spout to void the vow, and Vāmana's grass blade pierces and blinds his eye, sealing the gift _(actors: Śukra, Vāmana, Bali)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/the-gurus-brihaspati-and-shukra.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 8; Vāmana Purāṇa)
- **Vedavatī's curse on Rāvaṇa** — Accosted by Rāvaṇa, the ascetic Vedavatī cuts off her defiled hair, enters the fire, and curses that she will be reborn (as Sītā) to destroy him _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Vedavatī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ravana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa)
- **Vision of Śiva's cosmic form (viśvarūpa)** — Śiva grants Rāma divine sight and reveals his all-pervading cosmic form containing all worlds — the Śaiva answer to Bhagavad Gītā ch. 11 _(actors: Śiva, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shiva-gita.md) — (Padma Purāṇa, Śiva-Gītā 14–15)
- **Viśvāmitra as guru of Rāma** — The now-serene brahmarṣi takes Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa to guard his sacrifice, gifts them divine astras, and leads them to Sītā's svayaṃvara _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Tāṭakā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vishwamitra-vasishtha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 18-77)
- **Viśvāmitra creates a counter-heaven for Triśaṅku** — Viśvāmitra raises Triśaṅku bodily skyward; rejected by Indra, the enraged sage begins a parallel universe of new stars until the gods fix Triśaṅku head-down as a constellation _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Triśaṅku, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vishwamitra-vasishtha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 59-60)
- **Agastya Equips Rama in Dandaka** — At his southern ashram Agastya gifts Rama the bow of Vishnu, an inexhaustible quiver, a divine arrow and sword, and directs the exiles to Panchavati _(actors: Agastya, Rama, Sita, Lakshmana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/agastya.md) — (Ramayana, Aranya Kanda)
- **Agastya teaches Rāma the Aditya Hṛdayam** — On the battlefield before the final duel with Rāvaṇa, sage Agastya teaches the fatigued Rāma the 31-verse solar hymn Aditya Hṛdayam, whose chanting restores him to victory _(actors: Agastya, Rāma)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/surya-and-his-family.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Yuddha Kāṇḍa 107)
- **Ambarisha buys Sunahshepa from Richika (Ramayana variant)** — Ambarisha buys the middle son Sunahshepa from the sage Richika for a hundred thousand cows to serve as the human victim _(actors: Ambarisha, Richika, Sunahshepa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sunahshepa.md) — (Ramayana Bala-kanda 61)
- **Bestowal of the Pāśupata weapon and boon** — Śiva confers the Pāśupata-astra on Rāma, assures him he will slay Rāvaṇa and recover Sītā, and departs _(actors: Śiva, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/shiva-gita.md) — (Padma Purāṇa, Śiva-Gītā 15–16)
- **Bharata curses Urvaśī (Vikramorvaśīya)** — In Kālidāsa's play, Urvaśī blurts 'Purūravas' instead of 'Puruṣottama' during a celestial drama and the sage Bharata curses her to fall to earth _(actors: Urvaśī, Bharata, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/pururavas-urvashi.md) — (Kālidāsa, Vikramorvaśīya)
- **Birth of Hanumān on Añjanādri (claimed)** — Regional and Vijayanagara-era tradition claims the Tuṅgabhadrā hill Añjanādri as the birthplace of Hanumān, son of Añjanā and Vāyu — one of several rival janma-sthāna claims _(actors: Hanumān, Añjanā, Vāyu)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kishkindha-hampi-anegundi.md) — (Hampi/Anegundi sthala-tradition [regional/disputed])
- **Birth of Kumbhakarṇa** — Second-born of the sage Viśravas and the daitya princess Kaikasī, the pot-eared Kumbhakarṇa is born so enormous he devours living creatures by the thousands as an infant _(actors: Kumbhakarṇa, Viśravas, Kaikasī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kumbhakarna.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Uttara Kāṇḍa)
- **Birth of Meghanāda (Indrajit)** — Born in Laṅkā to Rāvaṇa and Mandodarī as crown prince, named Meghanāda because his infant cry boomed like a thundercloud _(actors: Meghanāda, Rāvaṇa, Mandodarī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/indrajit-meghnad.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Uttara Kāṇḍa)
- **Birth of Rama and his brothers (Putra-kameshti yajna)** — Childless King Dasharatha of Ayodhya performs a son-granting sacrifice whose sacred porridge yields four sons — Rama, Bharata, Lakshmana, and Shatrughna _(actors: Dasharatha, Kausalya, Kaikeyi, Sumitra, Rama, Bharata, Lakshmana, Shatrughna, Rishyashringa)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Bala Kanda)
- **Death of Daśaratha** — Calling for Rāma, Daśaratha dies of grief at midnight, the Śravaṇa-kumāra curse fulfilled; his body is preserved in oil to await Bharata _(actors: Daśaratha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/2-ayodhya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 64–66)
- **Earth-gift to Kaśyapa and retirement to Mahendra** — Paraśurāma performs an Aśvamedha, gifts the whole earth to Kaśyapa as dakṣiṇā, and withdraws to Mahendra mountain _(actors: Paraśurāma, Kaśyapa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/parashurama.md) — (Mahābhārata 3.117; Bhāgavata Purāṇa 9.16)
- **Hanuman fetches the Sanjivani mountain** — When Lakshmana is felled in battle, Hanuman flies to the Himalayas and, unable to identify the herb, carries the entire Dronagiri mountain back to Lanka _(actors: Hanuman, Lakshmana)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/hanuman.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Yuddha Kanda 74)
- **Hanumān's leap to Laṅkā and burning of the city** — Hanumān leaps the ocean, finds Sītā in the Aśoka grove, delivers Rāma's ring, burns Laṅkā with his tail, and returns with news _(actors: Hanumān, Sītā)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa 5 (Sundara Kāṇḍa))
- **Hanumān wrecks the Aśoka grove and slays the guards** — To provoke the enemy, Hanumān lays waste the Aśoka grove and kills waves of guards including the 80,000 kiṅkaras, Jambumālī, and five generals _(actors: Hanumān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/5-sundara-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 41–46)
- **Jaṭāyu's fall** — Jaṭāyu attacks Rāvaṇa in mid-air and is felled when his wings are cut, surviving only to reveal Sītā's flight-direction to Rāma _(actors: Jaṭāyu, Rāvaṇa, Sītā, Rāma)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/panchavati-nashik-godavari.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 50–51, 67–68; Sarvatīrtha/Ṭākeḍ regional tradition)
- **Jāmbavān awakens Hanumān for the leap** — The bear-king Jāmbavān reminds Hanumān of his forgotten divine origin and might; roused, Hanumān swells to colossal size on Mount Mahendra to spring across the sea _(actors: Jāmbavān, Hanumān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/4-kishkindha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Kiṣkindhā Kāṇḍa 65–67)
- **Kaikeyī's remorse and the Citrakūṭa reconciliation** — Stricken with shame, Kaikeyī joins Bharata's procession to Citrakūṭa to beg Rāma's return; Rāma forgives her _(actors: Kaikeyī, Bharata, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kaikeyi.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā 99–112; Rāmcaritmānas)
- **Lakṣmaṇa slays Indrajit at Nikumbhilā** — Guided by Vibhīṣaṇa, Lakṣmaṇa disrupts Indrajit's invincibility-granting fire-sacrifice at Nikumbhilā and beheads him with the Aindrāstra _(actors: Lakṣmaṇa, Indrajit, Vibhīṣaṇa, Hanumān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 84–90)
- **Nalakūbara's curse for the rape of Rambhā** — After Rāvaṇa rapes the apsarā Rambhā, Nalakūbara curses that his head will shatter into seven pieces if he ever again forces an unwilling woman — why he cannot touch Sītā _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Rambhā, Nalakūbara)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ravana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa)
- **Rama allies with Sugriva and slays Valin (Ramopakhyana)** — Rama allies with the exiled vanara Sugriva, slays Valin, and gains the monkey army _(actors: Rama, Sugriva, Valin)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramopakhyana.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana Parva 264)
- **Rama's return halt at the ashram** — Flying home in the Pushpaka vimana after the war, Rama alights again at Bharadvaja's ashram, where the sage grants out-of-season fruiting along his path and confirms Ayodhya's welfare _(actors: Bharadvaja, Rama, Sita, Lakshmana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bharadvaja.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Yuddha Kanda ~124-127)
- **Rāma slays Śambūka** — Told a śūdra's out-of-order penance caused a brāhmaṇa boy's premature death, Rāma beheads the ascetic Śambūka and the boy revives, the cycle's most contested act _(actors: Rāma, Śambūka, Nārada)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttara-ramayana-lava-kusha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Uttara Kāṇḍa 73-76)
- **Rāma slays Mārīca and the false cry** — Rāma pierces the deer; dying, Mārīca resumes demon form and cries out in Rāma's voice, drawing Lakṣmaṇa away from Sītā _(actors: Rāma, Mārīca, Lakṣmaṇa, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/3-aranya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya Kāṇḍa 44–45)
- **Rāma slays Tāḍakā** — At Viśvāmitra's command Rāma kills the yakṣiṇī-turned-rākṣasī Tāḍakā ravaging the forest — his first kill and a famous dharmic crux of slaying a female _(actors: Rāma, Tāḍakā, Viśvāmitra)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rakshasas.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa)
- **Rāma's Penance for Brahmahatyā at Rishikesh** — Folk tradition has Rāma perform penance at Rishikesh to expiate the brahmahatyā of slaying Rāvaṇa, with Lakṣmaṇa crossing the Gaṅgā on a jute-rope bridge (the Lakṣmaṇ Jhūlā legend) _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/haridwar-rishikesh-ganga-dwara.md) — (local mahatmya / tourism tradition)
- **Śūrpaṇakhā's mutilation** — At Pañcavaṭī the rākṣasī Śūrpaṇakhā lusts after Rāma and lunges at Sītā; Lakṣmaṇa cuts off her nose and ears, igniting the war _(actors: Śūrpaṇakhā, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Sītā)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/dandakaranya-rama-forest.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 17–18)
- **Sampāti shields Jaṭāyu and loses his wings** — The young vulture brothers fly toward the sun; when Jaṭāyu's wings begin to scorch, Sampāti spreads his own wings as a shield, his wings are burnt away, and he falls to the Vindhyas _(actors: Sampāti, Jaṭāyu, Sūrya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/jatayu-and-sampati.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa 58)
- **Shukra blinded at the Vamana episode** — Shukra hides in the spout of Bali's water-pot to block the donation pour, and Vamana pierces it with a blade of grass, putting out Shukra's eye and making him one-eyed _(actors: Shukra, Bali, Vamana)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/navagraha.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 8)
- **Shurpanakha and the Golden Deer; Abduction of Sita** — In the Dandaka forest the episode of Shurpanakha and the golden deer Marica leads to Ravana's abduction of Sita _(actors: Shurpanakha, Marica, Ravana, Sita, Rama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/README.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Aranya Kanda)
- **Sita svayamvara & breaking of Shiva's bow** — At Mithila Rama lifts and breaks Shiva's bow Pinaka to win Janaka's earth-born daughter Sita; the four brothers marry _(actors: Rama, Sita, Janaka, Lakshmana, Bharata, Shatrughna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/1-bala-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Bala Kanda))
- **Slaying of Indrajit at the Nikumbhilā rite** — Vibhīṣaṇa reveals the secret Nikumbhilā yajña that would make Indrajit invulnerable; Lakṣmaṇa disrupts it and beheads Indrajit in the ensuing duel _(actors: Lakṣmaṇa, Indrajit, Vibhīṣaṇa, Hanumān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/lakshmana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha-kāṇḍa)
- **Sons of the brothers and the dynasty's passing** — Śatrughna and Śrutakīrti's sons (Subāhu and Śatrughātī/Śūrasena) are set over Mathurā and an adjacent realm as the brothers share in the dynasty's final departure from the world _(actors: Śatrughna, Śrutakīrti, Subāhu, Śatrughātī, Bharata, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/bharata-shatrughna-and-their-wives.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa)
- **Sītā becomes Mahākālī and slays Sahasra-Rāvaṇa** — In the Adbhuta Rāmāyaṇa the thousand-headed Sahasra-Rāvaṇa fells Rāma, so Sītā assumes the form of Mahākālī and severs his thousand heads, revealing her supreme Śakti _(actors: Sītā, Sahasra-Rāvaṇa, Rāma, Brahmā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sita.md) — (Adbhuta Rāmāyaṇa)
- **The gods restore Hariścandra and revive his son** — At the instant of his unbroken truth the devas led by Indra, Yama/Dharma, and Viśvāmitra descend, declare the ordeal passed, revive Rohitāśva, restore the family and kingdom, and Hariścandra wins heaven while sharing it with his subjects _(actors: Indra, Yama/Dharma, Viśvāmitra, Hariścandra, Rohitāśva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/hariscandra.md) — (Mārkaṇḍeya Purāṇa 8)
- **The vow and twenty-one-fold extermination of the kṣatriyas** — Paraśurāma vows to rid the earth of kṣatriyas twenty-one times and circuits the world slaughtering tyrannical warrior kings and their lines _(actors: Paraśurāma, the kṣatriya kings)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/jamadagni-parashurama.md) — (Mahābhārata 3.117; Bhāgavata Purāṇa 9.16)
- **Vamana subdues the three worlds (Bali story)** — Vishnu's manvantara-avatara Vamana subdues the three worlds and returns them to Indra Purandara, pushing the demon-king Bali to the netherworld _(actors: Vamana, Bali, Indra Purandara)_ → [details](../00-time-and-cosmology/manvantara-system.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 8.1, 8.15-23; Vishnu Purana 3.1)
- **Viśvāmitra confers the divine astras on Rāma** — Pleased, Viśvāmitra gives Rāma the entire array of celestial weapons and recall-mantras, which appear as embodied beings pledging service _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/1-bala-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 27–28)
- **Viśvāmitra eats dog-flesh in the twelve-year famine** — In a drought at the Tretā–Dvāpara junction the starving sage Viśvāmitra steals dog-meat from a Caṇḍāla, eats to preserve his life and the Veda he carries, then makes rain by penance and is purified, establishing that life-preservation can override caste and dietary law _(actors: Viśvāmitra, a Caṇḍāla)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/12-shanti-parva.md) — (Mahabharata 12.139)
- **Vāmana subdues Bali** — Viṣṇu as the dwarf Vāmana subdues the asura king Bali — the Treta-Yuga avatāra framing the Vāmana Purāṇa _(actors: Vāmana (Viṣṇu), Bali)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-puranas.md) — (Vāmana Purāṇa)
- **Widowhood and the fourteen-year wait** — Daśaratha dies of grief; Kausalyā endures as widowed queen-mother in Ayodhyā under Bharata's regency _(actors: Kausalyā, Bharata)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kausalya.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 66)
- **Abduction of Sītā and slaying of Jaṭāyu** — Inflamed by Śūrpaṇakhā, Rāvaṇa uses Mārīca's golden-deer ruse to draw Rāma away, abducts Sītā in the Puṣpaka, and slays Jaṭāyu who tries to stop him _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Sītā, Mārīca, Jaṭāyu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ravana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa)
- **Agastya Teaches Rama the Aditya-Hridaya** — On the eve of the final duel with Ravana, Agastya appears on the battlefield and teaches the war-weary Rama the Aditya-Hridayam hymn, by whose power Rama slays Ravana _(actors: Agastya, Rama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/agastya.md) — (Ramayana, Yuddha Kanda)
- **Anasūyā counsels and gifts Sītā** — At Atri's hermitage Anasūyā instructs Sītā in pātivratya-dharma and gifts her celestial unfading garments, ornaments, and cosmetics _(actors: Anasūyā, Sītā, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Atri)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/atri-anasuya.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 116–119)
- **Battle of Janasthāna** — Roused by Śūrpaṇakhā, Khara, Dūṣaṇa and Triśiras attack with fourteen thousand rākṣasas; Rāma alone destroys the host _(actors: Rāma, Khara, Dūṣaṇa, Triśiras)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/dandakaranya-rama-forest.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 19–30)
- **Bharata denounces Kaikeyī and refuses the throne** — Recalled to Ayodhyā, Bharata learns of his father's death and his mother's plot, condemns Kaikeyī, refuses the kingdom, and performs the funeral rites _(actors: Bharata, Kaikeyī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/2-ayodhya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 70–77)
- **Building of the bridge to Laṅkā** — The vānaras build the Setu (Rāma Setu) bridge across the ocean to invade Laṅkā _(actors: Rāma, vānaras)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa 6 (Yuddha Kāṇḍa))
- **Hanuman slays Ahiravana in Patala** — When Ahiravana abducts the sleeping Rama and Lakshmana to Patala, Hanuman assumes his five-faced Panchamukha form to extinguish the demon's five life-lamps at once and slays him _(actors: Hanuman, Ahiravana, Rama, Lakshmana)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/hanuman.md) — (Adbhuta Ramayana; Krittivasi & Bengali traditions)
- **Hanuman's leap to Lanka and Avindhya's protection of Sita** — Hanuman crosses the ocean, finds Sita in the Ashoka grove and comforts her, while the sympathetic old rakshasa minister Avindhya counsels restraint and protects Sita's morale _(actors: Hanuman, Sita, Avindhya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramopakhyana.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana Parva 264-266)
- **Hanumān kills Akṣa-kumāra** — Rāvaṇa's young son Akṣa attacks valiantly; Hanumān, admiring his courage, seizes him by the feet and dashes him to death _(actors: Hanumān, Akṣa-kumāra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/5-sundara-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 47)
- **Kumbhakarṇa's penance and the mis-spoken boon** — During Kumbhakarṇa's extreme penance, Sarasvatī ties his tongue at Indra's request so he asks for sleep instead of sovereignty/destruction of the gods, and Brahmā grants perpetual slumber _(actors: Kumbhakarṇa, Brahmā, Sarasvatī, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kumbhakarna.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Uttara Kāṇḍa)
- **Lakṣmaṇa declines the crown-prince office** — After the return to Ayodhyā and Rāma's coronation, Lakṣmaṇa declines the Yuvarāja position in deference to Bharata _(actors: Lakṣmaṇa, Rāma, Bharata)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/lakshmana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa)
- **Lakṣmaṇa struck by the Vāsavī Śakti and healed** — Rāvaṇa hurls Indra's infallible javelin at Lakṣmaṇa, who falls mortally wounded; Hanumān again brings the whole Droṇagiri and Suṣeṇa's Sañjīvanī heals him before dawn _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Lakṣmaṇa, Hanumān, Suṣeṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 100–101)
- **Meghanāda conquers and binds Indra** — Meghanāda overpowers the devas in war on Svarga, binds Indra and carries him captive to Laṅkā; Brahmā ransoms Indra and the gods hail him 'Indrajit.' _(actors: Meghanāda, Indra, Brahmā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/indrajit-meghnad.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Uttara Kāṇḍa)
- **Parashurama confrontation & transfer of power** — Parashurama challenges Rama over the broken bow; Rama strings the Vishnu-bow, draining his power, and the 6th avatara cedes to the 7th _(actors: Parashurama, Rama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/1-bala-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Bala Kanda))
- **Raghu's Digvijaya and the Vishvajit Sacrifice** — Raghu conquers the world and gives away all his wealth after the Vishvajit sacrifice, becoming so illustrious the line is renamed Raghuvamsha _(actors: Raghu, Kautsa)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/suryavamsha-and-chandravamsha.md) — (Raghuvamsha; Vishnu Purana)
- **Rama slays Tataka and guards Vishvamitra's sacrifice** — Sage Vishvamitra takes Rama and Lakshmana to guard his sacrifice; Rama kills the rakshasi Tataka, receives celestial weapons, routs Marica and slays Subahu _(actors: Rama, Lakshmana, Vishvamitra, Tataka, Marica, Subahu)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Bala Kanda)
- **Ravana's Tapas and Shiva Heals His Heads at Vaidyanath** — Ravana offers his ten heads one by one into fire to win Shiva's favor and the Atma-linga, and Shiva appears as the divine physician to reattach his heads, founding the Vaidyanath jyotirlinga where the linga was tricked into being set down _(actors: Ravana, Shiva, Ganesha)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/12-jyotirlingas.md) — (Shiva Purana)
- **Reclaiming the western coast** — Forbidden to dwell on the gifted earth, Paraśurāma hurls his axe into the sea; Varuṇa recedes and the Konkan/Kerala coast (Paraśurāma-kṣetra) emerges as his abode _(actors: Paraśurāma, Varuṇa/Samudra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/parashurama.md) — (Padma, Skanda, Brahmāṇḍa Puranas; Keralolpatti; Konkan/Tuluva legends (regional))
- **Rāma flings Mārīca into the sea** — Defending Viśvāmitra's sacrifice, Rāma hurls Mārīca 800 miles into the sea with the Mānavāstra and spares his life _(actors: Rāma, Mārīca)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rakshasas.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa)
- **Rāma hurls Mārīca and slays Subāhu** — Defending Viśvāmitra's sacrifice, Rāma flings Mārīca into the ocean with the Mānava astra and kills Subāhu with the Āgneya astra _(actors: Rāma, Mārīca, Subāhu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/1-bala-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 30)
- **Rāvaṇa abducts Sītā** — Disguised as a mendicant, Rāvaṇa reveals himself, seizes Sītā, places her in the Puṣpaka chariot, and flies south toward Laṅkā as she screams _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/3-aranya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya Kāṇḍa 46–49)
- **Sacrifice of Lakṣmaṇa** — To honour his word after Lakṣmaṇa breaks Kāla's sealed audience to spare the realm from Durvāsā's curse, Rāma abandons him; Lakṣmaṇa enters the Sarayū as Śeṣa/Ananta _(actors: Kāla, Durvāsā, Lakṣmaṇa, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttara-ramayana-lava-kusha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Uttara Kāṇḍa 103-106)
- **Savitri Wins Satyavan Back from Yama** — When Yama personally takes the soul of Satyavan, his devoted wife Savitri follows and cleverly extracts boons until Yama, bound by his own word, must restore Satyavan to life _(actors: Savitri, Satyavan, Yama, Narada)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/yama.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana Parva)
- **Sītā's banishment to Vālmīki's hermitage** — A washerman's slander prompts Rāma to send the pregnant Sītā away; Lakṣmaṇa leaves her near Vālmīki's āśrama, who shelters her _(actors: Sītā, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Vālmīki)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sita.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara Kāṇḍa)
- **The five blood-lakes at Samanta-pañcaka** — Paraśurāma fills five pools at Samanta-pañcaka (Kurukṣetra) with the blood of the slain and offers tarpaṇa; the pacified Bhṛgu ancestors appear and bid him relent _(actors: Paraśurāma, Bhṛgu ancestors, Ṛcīka)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/jamadagni-parashurama.md) — (Mahābhārata 3.117; cf. 9.53)
- **Urvaśī transformed into a vine in Kumāra's grove** — In Kālidāsa's play a jealous Urvaśī strays into Kārtikeya's forbidden grove and is turned into a creeper, later restored by the union-gem _(actors: Urvaśī, Purūravas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/pururavas-urvashi.md) — (Kālidāsa, Vikramorvaśīya)
- **Vishnu Measures the Worlds as Trivikrama (Sirkazhi)** — At Kazhicheerama Vinnagaram and other Trivikrama sthalas, Vishnu as Vamana/Trivikrama measures the three worlds with raised foot _(actors: Vishnu (Trivikrama/Vamana))_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/108-divya-desams.md) — (sthala-purana / Dashavatara tradition)
- **Vishvamitra teaches Sunahshepa hymns and Indra spares him (Ramayana variant)** — At Pushkara Vishvamitra teaches his nephew Sunahshepa secret mantras to Indra and Vishnu; reciting them, the boy is granted long life and spared, while Vishvamitra curses his own refusing sons _(actors: Vishvamitra, Sunahshepa, Indra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/sunahshepa.md) — (Ramayana Bala-kanda 62)
- **Viśvāmitra curses Rambhā to stone** — Detecting Indra's next seductress, Viśvāmitra curses Rambhā to become stone, but the angry curse burns up his tapas, his moral turning point toward conquering wrath _(actors: Indra, Rambhā, Viśvāmitra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vishwamitra-vasishtha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 64-65)
- **Agastya teaches Rāma the Āditya-Hṛdayam** — As Rāma flags in the final duel, the sage Agastya appears and teaches him the Āditya-Hṛdayam hymn to Sūrya, renewing his vigor _(actors: Agastya, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 105)
- **Ahalya cursed to stone and freed by Rama** — The sage Gautama curses his wife Ahalya to stone, and she is later freed by the touch of Rama _(actors: Gautama, Ahalya, Rama)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/saptarishi.md) — (Ramayana)
- **Aja and the Death of Indumati** — Raghu's son Aja wins Indumati at her svayamvara; her death from a falling celestial garland plunges Aja into grief _(actors: Aja, Indumati)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/suryavamsha-and-chandravamsha.md) — (Raghuvamsha)
- **Bharata marches to Chitrakūṭa to recall Rāma** — Bharata leads an army and the queens to Chitrakūṭa to bring Rāma back; the brothers reunite and Rāma performs water-offerings for their father _(actors: Bharata, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/2-ayodhya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 83–103)
- **Brahmā's boon of the fire-sacrifice chariot** — Refused immortality, Meghanāda is granted that whenever he completes a fire-sacrifice before battle a celestial chariot rises from the fire, and while fighting from it he cannot be slain _(actors: Meghanāda, Brahmā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/indrajit-meghnad.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Uttara Kāṇḍa)
- **Breaking Śiva's bow and the Rāmāyaṇa encounter** — Paraśurāma confronts Rāma Dāśarathi after the breaking of Śiva's bow, challenges him to draw Viṣṇu's Śārṅga, and yields his tejas to the younger avatāra _(actors: Paraśurāma, Rāma, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/parashurama.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa 1.74-76)
- **Death of Jaṭāyu** — Jaṭāyu challenges Rāvaṇa in mid-air, wounds him and wrecks his chariot, but Rāvaṇa severs his wings and the great bird falls dying _(actors: Jaṭāyu, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/3-aranya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya Kāṇḍa 49–51)
- **Hanuman's Leap to Lanka and Burning of Lanka** — Hanuman leaps across the ocean to Lanka, finds Sita in the Ashoka grove, and burns the city _(actors: Hanuman, Sita)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/README.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Sundara Kanda)
- **Hariścandra's truth-test (wager of the sages)** — In a wager over Hariścandra's truthfulness Viśvāmitra strips him of kingdom and forces him to sell wife, son and himself to a cāṇḍāla before his truth is vindicated and all restored _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Vasiṣṭha, Hariścandra, Śaivyā, Rohitāśva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vishwamitra-vasishtha.md) — (Mārkaṇḍeya Purāṇa; Devī-Bhāgavata)
- **Indrajit captures Hanumān with the Brahmāstra** — Rāvaṇa's eldest son Indrajit binds Hanumān with the Brahmāstra; Hanumān submits in reverence and feigns captivity to enter Rāvaṇa's court _(actors: Indrajit, Hanumān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/5-sundara-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 48)
- **Kaikeyi's two boons & the exile decree** — Manthara poisons Kaikeyi's mind; she invokes two old boons demanding Bharata's crown and Rama's 14-year exile _(actors: Kaikeyi, Manthara, Dasaratha, Rama, Bharata)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/2-ayodhya-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Ayodhya Kanda))
- **Lakṣmaṇa escorts the banished Sītā to the forest** — Charged by Rāma after the public slander, Lakṣmaṇa in anguish escorts the pregnant Sītā to Vālmīki's hermitage _(actors: Lakṣmaṇa, Sītā, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/lakshmana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa)
- **Liberation of Ahalya** — Rama's presence restores Ahalya, wife of Gautama cursed to stone for Indra's deception, to human form _(actors: Rama, Ahalya)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Bala Kanda)
- **Mārīca's deer and Sītā's abduction** — Rāvaṇa uses Mārīca as a golden deer to lure Rāma away, then seizes Sītā and bears her toward Laṅkā; Jaṭāyu falls fighting him _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Mārīca, Sītā, Rāma, Jaṭāyu)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/dandakaranya-rama-forest.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya-kāṇḍa 42–51)
- **Rama installs the Ramanatha jyotirlinga at Setu** — Rama builds the Rama-Setu bridge and installs the Ramanatha jyotirlinga at Rameshvaram, the founding legend of the Setu pilgrimage _(actors: Rama)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/puranas/skanda-purana-khanda-map.md) — (Skanda Purana (Brahma-khanda, Setu-mahatmya))
- **Rāma enthrones Kuśa and Lava** — Rāma installs Kuśa at Kuśāvatī (continuing the Ikṣvāku line) and Lava at Śrāvastī, settling his brothers' sons over many realms _(actors: Rāma, Kuśa, Lava)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttara-ramayana-lava-kusha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Uttara Kāṇḍa 107-108)
- **Rāma slays Rāvaṇa with the Brahmāstra** — After Rāvaṇa's heads regrow, Agastya teaches Rāma the Āditya-hṛdaya and Rāma looses the Brahmāstra into Rāvaṇa's navel/chest where the regenerating nectar was stored, killing him _(actors: Rāma, Rāvaṇa, Agastya, Mātali)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ravana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha-kāṇḍa 108-110)
- **Rāma slays Subāhu** — Subāhu, who with Mārīca rains flesh and blood on Viśvāmitra's yajña, is killed by Rāma's Āgneyāstra _(actors: Rāma, Subāhu)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rakshasas.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa)
- **Rāvaṇa softens the sleep-curse** — Dismayed his mighty brother would be useless asleep, Rāvaṇa pleads and the curse is commuted to a cycle of six months' sleep and a day's waking during which he can be roused for war _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Kumbhakarṇa, Brahmā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kumbhakarna.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Uttara Kāṇḍa)
- **Sita blesses Hanuman with immortality** — Sita blesses Hanuman to live as long as Rama's story is told on earth, making him a Chiranjivi who remains through the Kali Yuga _(actors: Sita, Hanuman, Rama)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/hanuman.md) — (Ramayana tradition; Ramcharitmanas)
- **The bridge to Lanka and Vibhishana's defection (Ramopakhyana)** — The vanaras build the causeway and cross, and Ravana's righteous brother Vibhishana defects to Rama, receiving his sovereignty from Kubera _(actors: Vanaras, Vibhishana, Kubera, Rama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramopakhyana.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana Parva 267, 273)
- **The earth-gift to Kaśyapa and retirement to Mahendra** — Paraśurāma performs an Aśvamedha, gifts the whole earth to Kaśyapa as dakṣiṇā, is bidden to quit the land, and withdraws to Mahendra mountain as a deathless ascetic _(actors: Paraśurāma, Kaśyapa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/jamadagni-parashurama.md) — (Mahābhārata 3.117; Bhāgavata Purāṇa 9.16)
- **Birth of Makaradhvaja at Bet Dvaraka** — Hanuman's son Makaradhvaja is born of Hanuman's sweat swallowed by a fish, enshrined at the Hanuman Dandi temple on Bet Dvaraka _(actors: Hanuman, Makaradhvaja)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/dwarka-bet-dwarka-saurashtra.md) — (local tradition; Wikipedia)
- **Bridge to Lanka and the Great War** — Rama's army builds the bridge to Lanka and wages the great war culminating in the slaying of Ravana _(actors: Rama, Ravana, vanara army)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/README.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Yuddha/Lanka Kanda)
- **Dasharatha Kills Shravana and Is Cursed** — Dasharatha accidentally kills Shravana Kumara and is cursed to die from grief at separation from his own son _(actors: Dasharatha, Shravana, Kaikeyi)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/suryavamsha-and-chandravamsha.md) — (Ramayana Ayodhya Kanda)
