- **Fall of the rakshasa champions and slaying of Ravana (Ramopakhyana)** — The siege brings the fall of Kumbhakarna, Indrajit, and the rakshasa champions, and Rama kills Ravana in single combat, fulfilling the man-and-monkey loophole _(actors: Rama, Kumbhakarna, Indrajit, Ravana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramopakhyana.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana Parva 268-274)
- **Hanumān burns Laṅkā** — His tail set alight on Vibhīṣaṇa's plea against killing an envoy, Hanumān leaps roof to roof torching the golden city, then quenches the flame in the sea _(actors: Hanumān, Rāvaṇa, Vibhīṣaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/5-sundara-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 53–54)
- **Hariścandra's vow and the Śunaḥśepa sacrifice** — Childless Hariścandra vows his first-born to Varuṇa; the purchased victim Śunaḥśepa is freed by Ṛgvedic hymns at the stake and adopted by Viśvāmitra as Devarāta _(actors: Hariścandra, Rohita, Śunaḥśepa, Ajīgarta, Viśvāmitra)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vishwamitra-vasishtha.md) — (Aitareya Brāhmaṇa 7.13-18)
- **Jābāli's heterodox argument rejected** — The minister-sage Jābāli advances a near-materialist argument that the dead father is gone and vows are empty, urging Rāma to take the throne; Rāma vehemently rejects it _(actors: Jābāli, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/2-ayodhya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 108–109)
- **Kuvalashva slays the demon Dhundhu** — At Uttanka's tapas Vishnu foretells and the Ikshvaku king Kuvalashva and his sons slay the sand-buried fire-breathing asura Dhundhu, earning the king the title Dhundhumara _(actors: Uttanka, Vishnu, Kuvalashva, Dhundhu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttanka.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 3.192-195)
- **Lakṣmaṇa's self-sacrifice and end in the Sarayū** — Guarding Rāma's private audience with Kāla, Lakṣmaṇa interrupts it to admit the threatening Durvāsas, accepts the death-sentence as banishment, and gives up his body in the Sarayū — precipitating Rāma's own departure _(actors: Lakṣmaṇa, Kāla/Yama, Durvāsas, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/lakshmana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa)
- **Mārīca becomes the golden deer; slain by Rāma** — Coerced by Rāvaṇa, Mārīca becomes the golden deer that lures Rāma away so Sītā can be abducted; slain by Rāma's arrow, he dies mimicking Rāma's voice _(actors: Mārīca, Rāvaṇa, Rāma, Sītā)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rakshasas.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Araṇya Kāṇḍa)
- **Rama Installs the Rameshwaram Linga** — To expiate the brahma-hatya of killing Ravana, Rama installs and worships a Shiva linga at Setubandha; with Hanuman delayed fetching one from Kailasa, Sita fashions a linga from sand, founding the Rameshwaram jyotirlinga _(actors: Rama, Hanuman, Sita)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/12-jyotirlingas.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana-derived; Skanda Purana)
- **Rama's forest departure & death of Dasaratha** — Rama accepts exile with Sita and Lakshmana; Dasaratha dies of grief, fulfilling the Shravana-kumara curse _(actors: Rama, Sita, Lakshmana, Dasaratha, Kausalya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/2-ayodhya-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Ayodhya Kanda))
- **Rāma finds the dying Jaṭāyu and performs his rites** — Returning to an empty hermitage, the grieving Rāma finds Jaṭāyu, who names Rāvaṇa as abductor and expires; Rāma performs his funeral rites and the bird attains the higher worlds _(actors: Rāma, Jaṭāyu, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/3-aranya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya Kāṇḍa 67–68)
- **Rāma slays Rāvaṇa** — With Indra's chariot and Mātali's aid, Rāma looses the Brahmāstra into Rāvaṇa's amṛta-filled navel, drying the nectar and finally killing him; Rāvaṇa is given honorable cremation _(actors: Rāma, Rāvaṇa, Mātali, Mandodarī)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 102–111)
- **Rāma's Mahāprasthāna (jala-samādhi)** — Rāma walks into the Sarayū, resumes his form as Viṣṇu and ascends to Vaikuṇṭha with all who followed, closing the avatāra in collective liberation _(actors: Rāma, Brahmā, the citizens, vānaras)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttara-ramayana-lava-kusha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Uttara Kāṇḍa 109-110)
- **Rāvaṇa's cremation and Vibhīṣaṇa's coronation** — Rāma honors the fallen Rāvaṇa with full cremation rites, declaring enmity ends with death, and crowns Vibhīṣaṇa king of Laṅkā _(actors: Rāma, Vibhīṣaṇa, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ravana.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha-kāṇḍa)
- **Sita svayamvara and breaking of Shiva's bow** — At Mithila Rama lifts and breaks Shiva's bow Pinaka to win Sita, daughter of Janaka, and marries her, while his brothers marry Janaka's and Kushadhvaja's daughters _(actors: Rama, Sita, Janaka, Lakshmana, Bharata, Shatrughna)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Bala Kanda)
- **Slaying of Rāvaṇa and the war's end** — In the great battle Kumbhakarṇa and Indrajit fall and Rāma finally kills Rāvaṇa, ending the war _(actors: Rāma, Rāvaṇa, Kumbhakarṇa, Indrajit)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa 6 (Yuddha Kāṇḍa))
- **Sītā's return to the Earth** — Summoned to swear her chastity again, Sītā instead calls on her mother the Earth, who opens, rises on a golden throne, and draws her down — her final vindication _(actors: Sītā, Bhūmi, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/sita.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara Kāṇḍa)
- **Aditi's Penance for the Birth of Vāmana (Kurukṣetra)** — The Vāmana Purāṇa's Kurukṣetra-māhātmya ties the field to Aditi's penance that brings about the birth of the Vāmana avatāra _(actors: Aditi, Vāmana)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/kurukshetra-dharmakshetra.md) — (Vamana Purana)
- **Bharata's refusal & the sandals (paduka)** — Bharata refuses the throne, takes Rama's sandals as tokens of sovereignty, and rules as regent from Nandigrama _(actors: Bharata, Rama, Shatrughna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/2-ayodhya-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Ayodhya Kanda))
- **Hanumān granted deathlessness** — In the Vaiṣṇava telling, at Rāma's departure Hanumān is granted cirañjīvitva to remain on earth as long as the Rāma-kathā is told _(actors: Rāma, Hanumān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/uttara-ramayana-lava-kusha.md) — (Vaiṣṇava devotional tradition)
- **Hanumān returns and reports to Rāma** — Hanumān leaps back, announces 'I have seen Sītā,' the vānaras raid the Madhuvana in joy, and he presents the cūḍāmaṇi to a weeping Rāma, who resolves on the march to Laṅkā _(actors: Hanumān, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/5-sundara-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Sundara Kāṇḍa 57–68)
- **Parashurama confronts Rama** — On the journey home Parashurama challenges Rama over the broken bow; Rama effortlessly strings Parashurama's Vishnu-bow, draining his power as the 6th avatar cedes to the 7th _(actors: Parashurama, Rama)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Bala Kanda)
- **Rama, 7th Avatara of Vishnu** — Rama's exile, the abduction of Sita by Ravana, alliance with Sugriva and Hanuman, war at Lanka, and the establishment of Ramarajya mark the apex of the Solar dynasty _(actors: Rama, Sita, Ravana, Hanuman, Sugriva)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/suryavamsha-and-chandravamsha.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana; Bhagavata IX.10-11)
- **Rama's return/coronation (Gudi Padwa charter)** — An alternate Gudi Padwa charter commemorates Rama's return and coronation, the raised Gudi serving as a banner of triumph _(actors: Rama)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/regional-temple-festivals.md) — (folk)
- **Reunion and Rama's coronation (Ramopakhyana ending)** — Sita is restored, the party returns to Ayodhya, and Rama is crowned king in a righteous reign, the tale ending at the coronation with no Uttara-Kanda banishment _(actors: Rama, Sita)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramopakhyana.md) — (Mahabharata, Vana Parva 275)
- **Rāma annihilates Khara, Dūṣaṇa, Triśiras and 14,000 rākṣasas** — After Śūrpaṇakhā's mutilation, Khara leads 14,000 rākṣasas with Dūṣaṇa and Triśiras against Rāma, who annihilates the whole host single-handedly _(actors: Rāma, Khara, Dūṣaṇa, Triśiras)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rakshasas.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Araṇya Kāṇḍa)
- **Rāma gives his sandals to Bharata** — Defeated in argument, Bharata takes Rāma's gold-adorned wooden sandals as tokens of sovereignty, vowing to rule only as their deputy and enter fire if Rāma does not return on time _(actors: Bharata, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/2-ayodhya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 112)
- **Rāvaṇa composes the Śiva Tāṇḍava Stotra** — The supreme Śiva-devotee Rāvaṇa composes the thunderous Śiva Tāṇḍava Stotra, traditionally sung while pinned beneath Kailāsa _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Śiva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ravana.md) — (Tamil Śaiva Tēvāram tradition; devotional)
- **Sita's Agni-pariksha and Rama's Coronation** — After the war Sita undergoes the fire-ordeal (agni-pariksha) and Rama is crowned at his pattabhisheka _(actors: Rama, Sita, Agni)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/README.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Yuddha Kanda)
- **Slaying of Kabandha** — The brothers sever the arms of the monster Kabandha and cremate him; from the pyre rises a gandharva who counsels Rāma to ally with Sugrīva _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Kabandha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/3-aranya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya Kāṇḍa 69–73)
- **Sītā's fire-ordeal and Rāma's coronation** — Sītā undergoes the agni-parīkṣā fire-ordeal; Rāma returns to Ayodhyā and is crowned, inaugurating Rāma-rājya _(actors: Rāma, Sītā)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa 6 (Yuddha Kāṇḍa))
- **Viśvāmitra's sons split over Devarāta** — Ordered to accept Devarāta as senior, Viśvāmitra's fifty elder sons refuse and are cursed to become border tribal peoples; the younger fifty obey _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Devarāta)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vishwamitra-vasishtha.md) — (Aitareya Brāhmaṇa 7.18)
- **Śabarī's devotion and ascent** — The aged tribal devotee Śabarī offers Rāma fruit, is blessed by his darśana, enters the fire and ascends, directing him to Lake Pampā and Sugrīva _(actors: Śabarī, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/3-aranya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Araṇya Kāṇḍa 74)
- **Akampana flees to incite Rāvaṇa** — The Janasthāna survivor Akampana flees to Laṅkā to inform Rāvaṇa and incite the abduction of Sītā _(actors: Akampana, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rakshasas.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Araṇya Kāṇḍa)
- **Banishment of Sītā** — Stung by a washerman's slander about a wife who dwelt in another's house, Rāma orders the pregnant Sītā conveyed to the forest and abandoned near Vālmīki's hermitage _(actors: Rāma, Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa, Vālmīki)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/7-uttara-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara Kāṇḍa 43–49)
- **Banishment of Sītā and birth of Lava and Kuśa** — Rāma banishes the pregnant Sītā over a washerman's gossip; in Vālmīki's hermitage she bears Lava and Kuśa, who later challenge Rāma's Aśvamedha horse _(actors: Rāma, Sītā, Lava, Kuśa, Vālmīki)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa 7 (Uttara Kāṇḍa))
- **Bharata's regency from Nandigrāma** — Bharata enthrones the sandals and governs as an ascetic from Nandigrāma, sharing Rāma's exile in spirit _(actors: Bharata)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/2-ayodhya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 115)
- **Kaikeyi's boons and the exile decree** — On the eve of Rama's coronation, the maid Manthara poisons Kaikeyi's mind; Kaikeyi invokes two old boons demanding Bharata be crowned and Rama exiled fourteen years _(actors: Kaikeyi, Manthara, Dasharatha, Rama, Bharata)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Ayodhya Kanda)
- **Kusha and Lava Inherit the Kingdom** — Rama's twin sons Kusha and Lava inherit Kosala and found Shravasti/Lavapura respectively _(actors: Kusha, Lava)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/suryavamsha-and-chandravamsha.md) — (Uttara Kanda; Raghuvamsha 16-17)
- **Rāvaṇa loses the Ātma-liṅga at Gokarṇa** — Śiva grants Rāvaṇa the Ātma-liṅga to carry to Laṅkā if never set down, but the boy Gaṇeśa tricks him into setting it at Gokarṇa, where it roots as Mahābaleśvara _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Śiva, Gaṇeśa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/ravana.md) — (Sthala-purāṇa of Gokarṇa)
- **Shiva descends as Hanuman (11th Rudra)** — In Shaiva tradition, when Vishnu was to descend as Rama, Shiva took birth as Hanuman, the 11th Rudra, to serve him on earth _(actors: Shiva, Hanuman, Vishnu/Rama)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/hanuman.md) — (Shiva Purana, Shatarudra Samhita)
- **Sita's Banishment and Birth of Lava and Kusha** — In the Uttara Kanda Sita is banished, shelters with Valmiki, and gives birth to the twins Lava and Kusha, who are taught the epic _(actors: Sita, Valmiki, Lava, Kusha, Rama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/README.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Uttara Kanda)
- **Slaying of Viradha & forest sages** — In Dandaka Rama buries the man-eating rakshasa Viradha, vows to clear the forest of demons, and meets Agastya and Jatayu _(actors: Rama, Lakshmana, Viradha, Agastya, Jatayu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/3-aranya-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Aranya Kanda))
- **Sītā's fire-ordeal (Agni-parīkṣā)** — After Rāma's harsh disavowal, Sītā enters the fire; Agni rises bearing her unburned and testifies to her chastity, and the gods reveal Rāma's divinity _(actors: Sītā, Rāma, Agni)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 115–118)
- **Viśvāmitra hailed Brahmarṣi by Vasiṣṭha** — Having conquered his anger, Viśvāmitra humbly approaches Vasiṣṭha, who embraces him and publicly confers the title brahmarṣi, the only kṣatriya so to rise _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Vasiṣṭha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vishwamitra-vasishtha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 65)
- **Anasūyā honors Sītā** — At Atri's hermitage the chaste sage-wife Anasūyā instructs Sītā in wifely dharma and gifts her divine unfading garments and ornaments _(actors: Anasūyā, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/2-ayodhya-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Ayodhyā Kāṇḍa 117–118)
- **Birth of Lava and Kuśa** — In Vālmīki's hermitage Sītā bears twin sons, Kuśa and Lava, whom the sage names and trains to chant the 24,000-verse Rāmāyaṇa, ignorant it is their father's tale _(actors: Sītā, Lava, Kuśa, Vālmīki)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/7-uttara-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara Kāṇḍa 66, 71, 93)
- **Rama's forest departure and Dasharatha's death** — Rama accepts exile and Sita and Lakshmana accompany him to the forest; Dasharatha dies of grief, recalling the curse of Shravana-kumara's blind parents _(actors: Rama, Sita, Lakshmana, Dasharatha)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Ayodhya Kanda)
- **Sita's Return to the Earth and Rama's Departure** — Sita returns into the earth and Rama departs the world, closing the Uttara Kanda _(actors: Sita, Rama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/README.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Uttara Kanda)
- **Sītā swallowed by the Earth; Rāma departs into the Sarayū** — Sītā is taken back by the Earth, her mother, and Rāma ends his earthly life by entering the Sarayū river, returning to Vaikuṇṭha _(actors: Sītā, Rāma)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa 7 (Uttara Kāṇḍa))
- **Surpanakha mutilated** — Rama's spurned suitor Surpanakha attacks Sita; Lakshmana cuts off her nose and ears, sparking the war _(actors: Surpanakha, Rama, Lakshmana, Sita)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/3-aranya-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Aranya Kanda))
- **The fallen vānaras restored to life** — The gods grant a boon by which the dead vānaras and ṛkṣas are restored to life _(actors: Devas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 119)
- **Viradha buried alive; redemption** — The giant Viradha seizes Sītā; unkillable by weapon, he is buried alive by Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa, and his death releases him back to heaven as the cursed gandharva Tumburu _(actors: Viradha, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rakshasas.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Araṇya Kāṇḍa)
- **Adbhuta-variant: Sītā as Mahākālī slays the thousand-headed Rāvaṇa** — In the Śākta Adbhuta Rāmāyaṇa, when Rāma faints Sītā becomes the active power (Mahākālī) and slays a thousand-headed Rāvaṇa _(actors: Sītā, Rāma, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Adbhuta Rāmāyaṇa)
- **Bharata's refusal and the sandals** — Bharata returns, refuses the throne, pursues Rama to Chitrakuta, and rules as regent in Rama's name with Rama's sandals on the throne at Nandigrama _(actors: Bharata, Rama)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Ayodhya Kanda)
- **Kabandha freed and directs Rāma to Sugrīva** — The headless rākṣasa Kabandha grabs Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa; they sever his arms, and cremated he resumes his celestial form and directs Rāma to Sugrīva and Hanumān _(actors: Kabandha, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rakshasas.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Araṇya Kāṇḍa)
- **Khara, Dushana & Trishiras army destroyed** — Rama single-handedly annihilates Khara, Dushana, Trishiras and the 14,000 rakshasas of Janasthana _(actors: Rama, Khara, Dushana, Trishiras)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/3-aranya-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Aranya Kanda))
- **Renuka beheaded and restored by Parashurama** — Renuka, chaste wife of sage Jamadagni, is beheaded on his order and restored by her son Parashurama; her severed-and-rejoined head is the etiology of the goddess Yellamma/Mariyamman _(actors: Renuka, Jamadagni, Parashurama)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/devi-forms-and-navadurga.md) — (Mahabharata/Purana Renuka tale; South Indian folk variants)
- **Rāma's Aśvamedha and the twins' recital** — Rāma performs a horse-sacrifice using a golden image of Sītā; at it Lava and Kuśa recite the Rāmāyaṇa, and their resemblance reveals them as his sons _(actors: Rāma, Lava, Kuśa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/7-uttara-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara Kāṇḍa 91–94)
- **Rāma's coronation at Ayodhyā** — Flying north in the reclaimed Puṣpaka, Rāma reunites with Bharata at Nandigrāma and is consecrated King of Ayodhyā, inaugurating the ideal age of Rāma-rājya _(actors: Rāma, Bharata, Vasiṣṭha, Hanumān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa 123–128)
- **Golden deer & abduction of Sita; death of Jatayu** — Marica as a golden deer lures Rama away; Ravana abducts Sita to Lanka, and Jatayu is mortally wounded trying to stop him _(actors: Marica, Ravana, Sita, Rama, Lakshmana, Jatayu)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/3-aranya-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Aranya Kanda))
- **Lakṣmaṇa disfigures Ayomukhī** — The rākṣasī Ayomukhī lusts after Lakṣmaṇa, who disfigures her, paralleling the Śūrpaṇakhā episode _(actors: Lakṣmaṇa, Ayomukhī)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rakshasas.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Araṇya Kāṇḍa)
- **Mutilation of Shurpanakha** — Ravana's sister Shurpanakha lusts after Rama and Lakshmana; spurned and attacking Sita, she has her nose and ears cut off by Lakshmana _(actors: Shurpanakha, Rama, Lakshmana, Sita)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Aranya Kanda)
- **Sītā returns to the Earth** — Asked to publicly swear her purity again, Sītā calls on her mother Bhūmi-Devī, who rises on a golden throne and receives her into the ground forever _(actors: Sītā, Bhūmi-Devī, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/7-uttara-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara Kāṇḍa 96–97)
- **Viśvāmitra guides the young Rāma** — The serene brahmarṣi takes Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa to guard his sacrifice, has them slay Tāṭakā and the rākṣasas, gifts divine astras and leads them to Sītā's svayaṃvara _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Tāṭakā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/vishwamitra-vasishtha.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 18-77)
- **Destruction of Khara and Dushana's army** — Shurpanakha's brothers attack with 14,000 rakshasas; Rama annihilates them, prompting Shurpanakha to incite Ravana _(actors: Rama, Khara, Dushana, Shurpanakha)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Aranya Kanda)
- **Hanumān slays Kālanemi** — Rāvaṇa's uncle Kālanemi is sent to delay Hanumān fetching the Saṃjīvanī herb by posing as a hermit, and is killed by Hanumān _(actors: Hanumān, Kālanemi)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rakshasas.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Yuddha Kāṇḍa)
- **Kabandha slain & Shabari's devotion** — Rama frees the cursed Kabandha, who directs him to Sugriva; the devotee Shabari offers fruit and ascends, pointing him to Pampa _(actors: Rama, Lakshmana, Kabandha, Shabari)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/3-aranya-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Aranya Kanda))
- **Sita Found in the Furrow by Janaka** — The philosopher-king Siradhvaja Janaka finds Sita in a plough-furrow and later hosts the bow-svayamvara won by Rama, joining the two Ikshvaku branches _(actors: Janaka, Sita, Rama)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/suryavamsha-and-chandravamsha.md) — (Ramayana; Shatapatha Brahmana; Brihadaranyaka Up.)
- **The Ramopakhyana retold** — To console Yudhishthira, Markandeya narrates the whole Ramayana in brief, from Ravana's boon to the slaying of Ravana and recovery of Sita _(actors: Markandeya, Rama, Sita, Ravana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/mahabharata/03-vana-parva.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva 272-291)
- **Fire-witnessed alliance with Sugriva** — Rama and the exiled vanara prince Sugriva swear mutual aid with Agni as witness, brokered by Hanuman _(actors: Rama, Sugriva, Hanuman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/4-kishkindha-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Kishkindha Kanda))
- **Indrajit binds Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa with the Nāgapāśa** — Fighting invisible from the air, Indrajit fires the Nāgapāśa whose serpent-arrows bind both Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa, dropping them senseless — the lowest point of the war _(actors: Indrajit, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/indrajit-meghnad.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Yuddha Kāṇḍa)
- **Jaṭāyu challenges Rāvaṇa over Sītā's abduction** — Roused by Sītā's cries as Rāvaṇa carries her south, the aged Jaṭāyu rebukes him for stealing another's wife and demands he release her or fight _(actors: Jaṭāyu, Rāvaṇa, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/jatayu-and-sampati.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Araṇya-kāṇḍa 50)
- **Lakṣmaṇa's doom and abandonment** — To save the realm from Durvāsā's curse, Lakṣmaṇa interrupts Rāma's sealed meeting with Kāla, accepts the death-penalty, enters the Sarayū, and is taken up as Śeṣa _(actors: Lakṣmaṇa, Durvāsā, Kāla, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/7-uttara-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara Kāṇḍa 103–106)
- **Nīla slays Prahasta** — Prahasta, Rāvaṇa's prime minister and commander-in-chief, is slain by the vānara Nīla in the Laṅkā war _(actors: Nīla, Prahasta)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rakshasas.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Yuddha Kāṇḍa)
- **Rama slays Ravana (Dussehra/Ramlila)** — Rama kills the ten-headed Ravana to recover Sita, the climax of the Yuddha Kanda commemorated as Vijayadashami with the burning of Ravana effigies _(actors: Rama, Ravana, Sita)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/festivals-and-vratas.md) — (Ramayana, Yuddha Kanda)
- **The awakening of Kumbhakarṇa** — Roused as the ultimate weapon after Rāvaṇa's humiliation, Kumbhakarṇa is woken only by mountains of food, war-drums, maces, and a herd of elephants trampling over his body, then consumes a colossal meal _(actors: Kumbhakarṇa, the rākṣasas)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kumbhakarna.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Yuddha Kāṇḍa)
- **The golden deer and abduction of Sita** — Marica as a golden deer lures Rama away and Lakshmana off too; Ravana disguised as an ascetic abducts Sita to Lanka, and the vulture Jatayu fights Ravana and is mortally wounded telling Rama the direction _(actors: Marica, Ravana, Sita, Rama, Lakshmana, Jatayu)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Aranya Kanda)
- **Vamana reclaims the three worlds from Bali** — The Dwarf avatara reclaims the three worlds from the asura-king Bali in three strides (Trivikrama) _(actors: Vamana/Viṣṇu, Bali)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/bhagavata-purana-skandha-map.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 8)
- **Alliance with Sugriva and slaying of Vali** — Rama meets Hanuman and the exiled vanara-prince Sugriva, allies with him, and kills Sugriva's brother Vali from concealment, restoring Sugriva to the throne of Kishkindha _(actors: Rama, Hanuman, Sugriva, Vali)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Kishkindha Kanda)
- **Garuḍa frees the princes from the serpent-bonds** — Garuḍa, enemy of all serpents, descends and at his mere approach the snake-bonds flee, freeing and healing Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa _(actors: Garuḍa, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/indrajit-meghnad.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Yuddha Kāṇḍa)
- **Jaṭāyu's aerial duel and mutilation by Rāvaṇa** — Jaṭāyu tears at Rāvaṇa's bow, armor and chariot, forcing him to the ground, until Rāvaṇa severs the great bird's wings, feet and sides and flies on with Sītā _(actors: Jaṭāyu, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/jatayu-and-sampati.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Araṇya-kāṇḍa 50-51)
- **Kumbhakarṇa counsels Rāvaṇa to return Sītā** — Roused for the war, the dharma-aware Kumbhakarṇa tells Rāvaṇa that abducting Sītā was adharma and that he should return her, warning that Rāma is no ordinary man, yet vows to fight for his brother _(actors: Kumbhakarṇa, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kumbhakarna.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Yuddha Kāṇḍa)
- **Maya-Sita Substitution (Shadow Sita)** — Foreknowing Ravana's plot, Rama has the true Sita hidden in Agni's fire and an illusory maya-Sita put in her place; only the shadow is abducted, and the true Sita re-emerges from the fire at the agni-pariksha _(actors: Rama, Sita, Agni, Ravana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/recensions/adhyatma-ramayana.md) — (Adhyatma Ramayana, Aranya & Yuddha Kanda)
- **Rāma's Great Departure (Mahāprasthāna)** — After enthroning Kuśa and Lava, Rāma walks into the Sarayū followed by his kin and people, resumes his form as Viṣṇu, and ascends to Vaikuṇṭha _(actors: Rāma, Brahmā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/7-uttara-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara Kāṇḍa 109–110)
- **Slaying of Vali** — Rama kills Sugriva's brother Vali with a concealed arrow, the epic's most-debated act of dharma, restoring Sugriva to the throne _(actors: Rama, Vali, Sugriva, Tara)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/4-kishkindha-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Kishkindha Kanda))
- **Four-quarter search for Sita & Sampati** — Vanara armies search the four directions; the dying vulture Sampati reveals Sita is captive in Ravana's Ashoka grove in Lanka _(actors: Sugriva, Angada, Hanuman, Jambavan, Sampati)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/4-kishkindha-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Kishkindha Kanda))
- **Indrajit wounds Lakṣmaṇa; the Sañjīvanī herb** — In a later sally Indrajit gravely wounds Lakṣmaṇa, who is saved when Hanumān flies to the Himālaya and brings back the life-restoring Sañjīvanī herb, lifting the whole mountain _(actors: Indrajit, Lakṣmaṇa, Hanumān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/indrajit-meghnad.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Yuddha Kāṇḍa)
- **Jaṭāyu's dying testimony and Rāma's last rites** — The dying Jaṭāyu tells Rāma that Rāvaṇa carried Sītā south; Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa cremate the bird and perform the filial funeral rites of a son toward a father, declaring he has won the heavenly worlds _(actors: Jaṭāyu, Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/jatayu-and-sampati.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Araṇya-kāṇḍa 67-68)
- **Kumbhakarṇa devastates the vānara army** — Awake and armed, Kumbhakarṇa storms the field slaughtering thousands of vānaras, stuns and carries off Sugrīva (who bites off his nose and ears to escape), and battles Hanumān and Lakṣmaṇa _(actors: Kumbhakarṇa, Sugrīva, Hanumān, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kumbhakarna.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Yuddha Kāṇḍa)
- **Rama avatara (condensed Ramayana)** — Rama, the apex of the Solar line, descends and enacts the condensed Ramayana narrative in skandha 9 _(actors: Rama)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/bhagavata-purana-skandha-map.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 9)
- **The search for Sita** — Vanara armies are dispatched in four directions; the southern party learns from Sampati that Sita is held in Lanka across the ocean _(actors: Hanuman, Sampati, the vanaras)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Kishkindha Kanda)
- **Hanuman's leap across the ocean** — Roused by Jambavan, Hanuman grows colossal and leaps 100 yojanas across the sea, passing the tests of Mainaka, Surasa, and Simhika _(actors: Hanuman, Jambavan, Surasa, Simhika)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/5-sundara-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Sundara Kanda))
- **Hanuman's leap to Lanka and burning of the city** — Hanuman leaps the ocean, finds Sita in the Ashoka-vatika, gives Rama's ring, burns Lanka with his tail after being captured, and returns with Sita's crest-jewel _(actors: Hanuman, Sita, Ravana)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Sundara Kanda)
- **Indrajit's illusory slaying of Sītā** — To break morale Indrajit conjures a phantom Sītā and beheads 'her' before the vānaras; Rāma collapses in grief until Vibhīṣaṇa exposes the illusion as cover for the Nikumbhilā yajña _(actors: Indrajit, Hanumān, Rāma, Vibhīṣaṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/indrajit-meghnad.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Yuddha Kāṇḍa)
- **Parasurama clears the kṣatriyas twenty-one times** — The warrior-brahmana avatara Parasurama rids the earth of the kṣatriya class twenty-one times _(actors: Parasurama)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/bhagavata-purana-skandha-map.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 9)
- **Rama Teaches the Rama-Hridaya to Hanuman** — In the inset Rama-Hridaya ('Heart of Rama'), Hanuman is taught Rama's true Brahman-nature _(actors: Rama, Hanuman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/recensions/adhyatma-ramayana.md) — (Adhyatma Ramayana, Bala Kanda)
- **Rāma slays Kumbhakarṇa** — Rāma severs Kumbhakarṇa's arms and legs with celestial astras and finally decapitates the giant with the supreme Indra-astra; his death breaks Rāvaṇa, marking the turn toward his own end _(actors: Rāma, Kumbhakarṇa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kumbhakarna.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Yuddha Kāṇḍa ch. 67-68)
- **Vamana's Three Steps and Bali's Surrender** — The dwarf-avatara Vamana measures the cosmos in three steps and Bali surrenders himself, ceding his sovereignty _(actors: Vamana, Bali)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavata-tales-catalog.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 8.15-23)
- **Hanuman finds Sita in the Ashoka grove** — Hanuman finds Sita in the Ashoka-vana, gives Rama's ring as proof, and receives her crest-jewel (chudamani) as a return token _(actors: Hanuman, Sita, Ravana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/5-sundara-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Sundara Kanda))
- **Kanyakumari Consecrated by Parashurama** — Consecration of the Kanyakumari shrine at the subcontinent's southern tip is attributed to Parashurama _(actors: Parashurama)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/sthala-puranas-major-temples.md) — (Skanda Purana)
- **Kumbha and Nikumbha die in the war** — Kumbhakarṇa's sons Kumbha and Nikumbha fight and die against Rāma's forces in the Laṅkā war _(actors: Kumbha, Nikumbha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/kumbhakarna.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Yuddha Kāṇḍa)
- **Rama Teaches the Rama-Gita to Lakshmana** — After the coronation Rama instructs Lakshmana in pure Advaita Vedanta in the inset Rama-Gita, modelled on the Bhagavad-Gita _(actors: Rama, Lakshmana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/recensions/adhyatma-ramayana.md) — (Adhyatma Ramayana, Uttara Kanda)
- **Setu-bandha (building the bridge to Lanka)** — The vanaras led by Nala build a causeway to Lanka over the ocean, which yields after Rama threatens it with arrows _(actors: Rama, Nala, the vanaras)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Yuddha Kanda)
- **Sītā's earth-birth from the furrow** — Janaka relates that Sītā was found ayonijā in the ploughed sacrificial field, a daughter of the Earth _(actors: Janaka, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/1-bala-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 66)
- **The Nikumbhilā sacrifice interrupted** — Guided by Vibhīṣaṇa, Lakṣmaṇa and Hanumān break into the Nikumbhilā grove and shatter Indrajit's sacrifice before the chariot manifests, denying him invulnerability _(actors: Lakṣmaṇa, Vibhīṣaṇa, Hanumān, Indrajit)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/indrajit-meghnad.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Yuddha Kāṇḍa)
- **Burning of Lanka** — Captured by Indrajit's Brahmastra and his tail set alight, Hanuman breaks free and torches the golden city of Lanka _(actors: Hanuman, Indrajit, Ravana, Vibhishana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/5-sundara-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Sundara Kanda))
- **Lakṣmaṇa slays Indrajit with the Aindrāstra** — In the final duel Lakṣmaṇa beheads Indrajit with the Aindrāstra, the weapon of the very god Indra the rākṣasa had once conquered, breaking the back of the rākṣasa army _(actors: Lakṣmaṇa, Indrajit)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/indrajit-meghnad.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Yuddha Kāṇḍa)
- **Rāma breaks the bow of Śiva and wins Sītā** — At Mithilā Rāma lifts and strings Janaka's colossal Śiva-bow (Pināka), snapping it with a thunderclap, and so wins Sītā by the bride-price of valour _(actors: Rāma, Janaka, Sītā)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/1-bala-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 66–67)
- **Vibhishana defects to Rama** — Ravana's righteous brother Vibhishana joins Rama after counselling Ravana in vain to return Sita _(actors: Vibhishana, Rama, Ravana)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Yuddha Kanda)
- **Deaths of Kumbhakarna and Indrajit; Lakshmana revived** — In the great battle Kumbhakarna and Indrajit/Meghanada fall, and Lakshmana, felled by Indrajit's weapon, is revived when Hanuman fetches the Sanjivani herb by carrying the whole Dronagiri mountain _(actors: Kumbhakarna, Indrajit, Lakshmana, Hanuman)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Yuddha Kanda)
- **Saubhari Weds Mandhata's Fifty Daughters** — The sage Saubhari, disturbed by mating fish, multiplies his body to wed all fifty daughters of King Mandhata, then renounces everything _(actors: Saubhari, Mandhata)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavata-tales-catalog.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 9.6)
- **Sulocanā becomes satī on Indrajit's pyre** — In later tradition Indrajit's wife Sulocanā, daughter of Śeṣa-nāga, becomes satī on his funeral pyre, celebrated as a model pativratā _(actors: Sulocanā, Indrajit)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/indrajit-meghnad.md) — (Rāmacaritamānasa; regional Rāmāyaṇas)
- **The four-fold marriage at Mithilā** — Rāma weds Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa weds Ūrmilā, and Bharata and Śatrughna wed Kuśadhvaja's daughters Māṇḍavī and Śrutakīrti in a quadruple fire-wedding _(actors: Rāma, Sītā, Lakṣmaṇa, Bharata, Śatrughna)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/1-bala-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 73)
- **Vibhishana's defection (sharanagati)** — Spurned by Ravana, his righteous brother Vibhishana surrenders to Rama, who accepts him and pre-crowns him king of Lanka _(actors: Vibhishana, Rama, Ravana, Sugriva)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Yuddha Kanda))
- **Building of the bridge (Nala-Setu / Rama-Setu)** — Under Nala's direction the vanaras build a 100-yojana causeway across the ocean, and the army crosses to Lanka _(actors: Rama, Nala, Sagara, vanaras)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Yuddha Kanda))
- **Paraśurāma confronts Rāma and yields his power** — The wrathful Paraśurāma challenges Rāma with Viṣṇu's Śārṅga bow; Rāma strings it, Paraśurāma recognizes him as Viṣṇu, surrenders his tapas, and withdraws _(actors: Paraśurāma, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/1-bala-kanda.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa 74–76)
- **Rama slays Ravana** — Rama kills the ten-headed Lanka-king Ravana with the Brahmastra, preceded by the Aditya-hridaya hymn taught by Agastya _(actors: Rama, Ravana, Agastya)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Yuddha Kanda)
- **Trishanku Hung Between Worlds** — King Trishanku seeks heaven in his mortal body and is left suspended between earth and sky _(actors: Trishanku)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavata-tales-catalog.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 9.7)
- **Death of Kumbhakarna** — Ravana's colossal sleeping brother Kumbhakarna is roused, rampages through the vanaras, and is dismembered and beheaded by Rama _(actors: Kumbhakarna, Rama, Ravana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Yuddha Kanda))
- **Sagara's Sons Burnt and the Descent of Ganga** — Sagara's 60,000 sons are burnt to ash by Kapila's glance, and Bhagiratha's tapas brings Ganga down to liberate their ashes _(actors: Sagara, Kapila, Bhagiratha, Ganga)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavata-tales-catalog.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 9.8-9)
- **Sita's fire ordeal (Agni-pariksha)** — Sita proves her chastity by entering fire, and Agni returns her unharmed _(actors: Sita, Agni, Rama)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Yuddha Kanda)
- **Agastya Releases the Kaveri** — In a famine, the gods via Ganesha as a crow tip Agastya's water-pot so the maiden Kaveri flows out to relieve the drought, becoming the life-river of Tamil country _(actors: Agastya, Kaveri, Ganesha)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/yamuna-and-river-goddesses.md) — (Skanda Purana, Kaveri-mahatmya; Tamil tradition)
- **Khatvanga's Liberation in One Muhurta** — King Khatvanga, granted a single muhurta of life on returning from aiding the devas, attains liberation in that hour by total surrender _(actors: Khatvanga)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavata-tales-catalog.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 9.9)
- **Kārtavīrya steals Kāmadhenu from Jamadagni** — Kārtavīrya Arjuna's theft of the wish-cow Kāmadhenu from the sage Jamadagni triggers Paraśurāma's vengeance _(actors: Kārtavīrya Arjuna, Jamadagni, Paraśurāma)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/sacred-flora-and-fauna.md) — (Kamadhenu (Wikipedia))
- **Rama's return and coronation (Rama-rajya)** — Rama flies home in the Pushpaka-vimana and is crowned at Ayodhya, inaugurating the ideal reign of Rama-rajya, later celebrated as Dipavali/Vijayadashami _(actors: Rama, Sita)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Yuddha Kanda)
- **Sanjivani herb & revival of Lakshmana** — Lakshmana felled by Indrajit's/Ravana's weapons is revived when Hanuman fetches the Sanjivani herb, carrying the whole Dronagiri mountain _(actors: Hanuman, Lakshmana, Jambavan, Sushena)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Yuddha Kanda))
- **Indrajit slain by Lakshmana** — Guided by Vibhishana, Lakshmana disrupts the Nikumbhila sacrifice and beheads Indrajit (Meghanada) with the Aindrastra _(actors: Lakshmana, Indrajit, Vibhishana, Hanuman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Yuddha Kanda))
- **Rāvaṇa wins the Ātma-liṅga and is tricked at Gokarṇa** — Rāvaṇa wins Śiva's Ātma-liṅga by tapas on condition it never touch ground before Laṅkā; Gaṇeśa as a cowherd-boy and Vishnu's false dusk make him set it down at Gokarṇa, where it roots immovably as Mahābaleśvara, and the hurled wrappings spring up as the Sajjeśvara, Dhāreśvara, Guṇavanteśvara and Mūrudeśvara liṅgas _(actors: Rāvaṇa; Śiva; Gaṇeśa; Vishnu)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/sthala-puranas-major-temples.md) — (Gokarṇa-māhātmya (Skanda Purāṇa, Sahyādri-khaṇḍa))
- **Sampāti reveals Sītā's location in Laṅkā** — Hearing the mourning vānaras name his dead brother, the wingless Sampāti descends and, with his far-sight, declares Sītā held across the sea in Rāvaṇa's Laṅkā in the Aśoka grove, converting a hopeless search into the ocean-leap mission _(actors: Sampāti, Aṅgada, Hanumān)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/jatayu-and-sampati.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa 56-63)
- **Vasiṣṭha's brahmadaṇḍa swallows Viśvāmitra's Brahmāstra** — Vasiṣṭha's defensive brahmadaṇḍa absorbs the Brahmāstra hurled by king Viśvāmitra, the humiliation that turns the warrior-king toward brahminhood _(actors: Vasiṣṭha, Viśvāmitra)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divyastras-and-divine-weapons.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa, Bāla Kāṇḍa)
- **Lava-Kusha and the Ashvamedha** — Lava and Kusha, taught the Ramayana by Valmiki, recite it at Rama's horse-sacrifice and capture the sacrificial horse, confronting their father _(actors: Lava, Kusha, Rama, Valmiki)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Uttara Kanda)
- **Nandinī conjures armies against Viśvāmitra** — When king Viśvāmitra (then Kauśika) tries to seize Vasiṣṭha's wish-cow Nandinī, she conjures whole armies (Pahlavas, Śakas, Yavanas, Kāmbojas) to rout him, setting him on the path to brahmin-hood _(actors: Nandinī, Viśvāmitra, Vasiṣṭha)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divine-objects-and-treasures.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa; Mahābhārata Ādi Parva)
- **Niśākara's prophecy and Sampāti's wings restored** — Fulfilling the sage Niśākara's prophecy that telling the vānaras of Sītā would be his great deed, Sampāti's feathers sprout and his wings regrow after he serves Rāma's cause, and he flies off rejoicing _(actors: Sampāti, Niśākara)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/characters/jatayu-and-sampati.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Kiṣkindhā-kāṇḍa 60-63)
- **Parashurama Clears the Earth of Kshatriyas** — The Bhrigu avatara Parashurama clears the earth of kshatriyas twenty-one times in the Kartavirya/Jamadagni cycle _(actors: Parashurama, Kartavirya, Jamadagni)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bhagavata-tales-catalog.md) — (Bhagavata Purana 9.15-16)
- **Rāma marks the Setu's start at Dhanuṣkoḍi** — At the south-eastern sandspit of Rāmeśvaram island Rāma marks the start of the Nala-setu to Laṅkā with the tip (koḍi) of his bow (dhanuṣ) — and in the return-telling breaks the now-needless bridge there at Vibhīṣaṇa's request — giving Dhanuṣkoḍi its name and its sankalpa-snāna tīrtha and Kodaṇḍarāma shrine _(actors: Rāma; Vibhīṣaṇa; Hanumān; Sītā)_ → [details](../06-sacred-geography/sthala-puranas-major-temples.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa; Skanda Purāṇa (Setu-māhātmya))
- **Slaying of Ravana** — Renewed by the Aditya-Hridayam, Rama looses the Brahmastra into Ravana's navel, drying the amrita that sustained him and killing the ten-headed king _(actors: Rama, Ravana, Matali, Agastya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Yuddha Kanda))
- **Agni-pariksha (fire ordeal)** — Sita enters fire to prove her chastity; Agni rises bearing her unharmed and testifies to her purity _(actors: Sita, Rama, Agni)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Yuddha Kanda))
- **Bharata enthrones Rāma's pādukās to rule Ayodhyā** — Bharata enthrones Rāma's sacred sandals (pādukās) to rule Ayodhyā in his stead during Rāma's exile, an emblem of surrender at the lotus-feet _(actors: Bharata, Rāma)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/sacred-symbols-and-emblems.md) — (Knowledge of India; Rāmāyaṇa)
- **Rama's departure into the Sarayu** — Rama renounces the world and walks into the Sarayu river to return to Vaikuntha as Vishnu, ending the avatar _(actors: Rama)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana, Uttara Kanda)
- **Return to Ayodhya & coronation (Rama-rajya)** — Rama flies home in the Pushpaka-vimana, reunites with Bharata, and is crowned king, inaugurating the ideal reign of Rama-rajya _(actors: Rama, Sita, Lakshmana, Bharata, Hanuman, Vibhishana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/6-yuddha-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Yuddha Kanda))
- **Treta Yuga — the Age of Three-Quarter Dharma** — Dharma declines by one leg; sacrifice (yajna), kingship, agriculture, and the first wars arise, with humans living ~10,000 years _(actors: humanity, Rama)_ → [details](../00-time-and-cosmology/yugas-and-mahayuga.md) — (Vishnu Purana 1.3, Bhagavata Purana 12.3)
- **Descent of the Ganga (Gangavatarana)** — King Bhagiratha does immense penance to bring the celestial Ganga down to liberate the ashes of Sagara's 60,000 sons; Shiva breaks the river's fall in his hair and Ganga flows to Patala to redeem the ancestors, earning the epithet Bhagirathi _(actors: Bhagiratha, Ganga, Shiva, Sagara, Kapila)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Ramayana Bala Kanda; Vishnu Purana; Bhagavata Purana 9.8-9; Mahabharata)
- **Rise of Ravana & Brahma's boon** — The rakshasa brothers do great tapas; Ravana wins invulnerability to all beings except men and monkeys, seizes Lanka from Kubera, and conquers the three worlds _(actors: Ravana, Kumbhakarna, Vibhishana, Brahma, Kubera)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/7-uttara-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Uttara Kanda))
- **Valmiki composes the Rāmāyaṇa** — The adi-kavi Valmiki composes the Rāmāyaṇa, the chronicle of Rāma's exile, Sītā's abduction, the war in Laṅkā and the return to Ayodhyā _(actors: Valmiki)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/README.md) — (Ramayana (Valmiki); Itihasa (Wikipedia))
- **Ahalya Cursed to Stone and Redeemed by Rama** — Gautama's wife Ahalya is cursed to stone and redeemed by the touch of Rama, linking the sage-line Gautama to the Solar epic _(actors: Ahalya, Gautama, Rama)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/suryavamsha-and-chandravamsha.md) — (Ramayana Bala Kanda)
- **Harishchandra's trial of truth** — The Ikshvaku king Harishchandra, to honor a debt to sage Vishvamitra, surrenders his kingdom, sells his wife and son, and becomes a chandala's servant at the cremation ground; his unbroken truthfulness restores all and elevates him to heaven _(actors: Harishchandra, Vishvamitra, Shaivya, Rohitashva)_ → [details](../01-manvantaras/manvantara-07-vaivasvata/mahayuga-28-current/02-treta-yuga.md) — (Markandeya Purana; Aitareya Brahmana; Devi Bhagavata)
- **Śiva gifts the Chandrahāsa sword to Rāvaṇa** — Śiva gifts his curved sword Chandrahāsa to Rāvaṇa for his devotion; it returns to harm the wielder if used unjustly _(actors: Śiva, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divyastras-and-divine-weapons.md) — (Astra (weapon) (Wikipedia))
- **Kārtavīrya Arjuna's sons slay Jamadagni** — The sons of Kārtavīrya Arjuna slay the sage Jamadagni over the wish-cow Kāmadhenu, triggering Paraśurāma's vengeance _(actors: Jamadagni, Kārtavīrya Arjuna)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rishi-lineages-and-gotras.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 9.15–16; Mahābhārata)
- **Vedavati's curse (reborn as Sita)** — Ravana molests the chaste ascetic Vedavati, who immolates herself vowing to be reborn as the cause of his death, i.e. as Sita _(actors: Ravana, Vedavati)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/7-uttara-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Uttara Kanda))
- **Banishment of Sita** — A washerman's slander prompts Rama to exile the pregnant Sita to Valmiki's hermitage, where she bears twins Lava and Kusha _(actors: Rama, Sita, Lakshmana, Valmiki)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/7-uttara-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Uttara Kanda))
- **Jaṭāyu fights Rāvaṇa and dies telling Rāma of Sītā** — The vulture-king Jaṭāyu fights Rāvaṇa to save the abducted Sītā, has his wings severed, and dies telling Rāma the direction of her captor; Rāma performs his funeral rites as for a father _(actors: Jaṭāyu, Rāvaṇa, Sītā, Rāma)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/sacred-flora-and-fauna.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa)
- **Paraśurāma clears the earth of kṣatriyas 21 times** — The axe-bearing 6th avatāra of Viṣṇu, Paraśurāma, avenges his father by clearing the earth of corrupt kṣatriyas 21 times and reclaims Konkan/Kerala from the sea _(actors: Paraśurāma)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rishi-lineages-and-gotras.md) — (Bhāgavata Purāṇa 9.15–16; Mahābhārata)
- **Vishvamitra Becomes Brahmarshi** — Born a Kshatriya king Kaushika, Vishvamitra earns brahmarshi status, tests Harishchandra, and trains Rama and Lakshmana, bridging Kshatriya and Brahmana lines _(actors: Vishvamitra)_ → [details](../03-lineages-vamsha/suryavamsha-and-chandravamsha.md) — (Ramayana; Mahabharata)
- **Lava-Kusha & the Ashvamedha** — Vālmīki teaches the Rāmāyaṇa to Sita's twins Lava and Kusha, who recite it at Rama's horse-sacrifice and are revealed as his sons _(actors: Lava, Kusha, Valmiki, Rama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/7-uttara-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Uttara Kanda))
- **Hanumān leaps the ocean to Laṅkā and finds Sītā** — Hanumān, son of Vāyu and partial aṃśa of Śiva, leaps the ocean to Laṅkā and finds the captive Sītā, in supreme devotion to Rāma _(actors: Hanumān, Sītā, Rāma)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/sacred-flora-and-fauna.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa)
- **Killing of Shambuka** — Rama beheads the shudra ascetic Shambuka for performing tapas forbidden to his varna in Treta, reviving a dead brahmana boy; a highly disputed passage _(actors: Rama, Shambuka, Narada)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/7-uttara-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Uttara Kanda))
- **Rāma slays Vālin and allies with Sugrīva** — Rāma slays the usurping vānara Vālin (son of Indra) with his arrow and allies with Sugrīva (son of Sūrya), restoring him as king of Kiṣkindhā _(actors: Rāma, Vālin, Sugrīva)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/sacred-flora-and-fauna.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa)
- **Sita's return to the earth** — Asked to prove her purity again, Sita calls on her mother Bhumi (Earth), who opens and receives her forever _(actors: Sita, Rama, Bhumi-Devi)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/7-uttara-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Uttara Kanda))
- **Agastya presses down the Vindhya mountains** — Agastya humbles the growing Vindhya range, pressing it down and opening the southward spread of Vedic culture _(actors: Agastya)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rishi-lineages-and-gotras.md) — (Mahābhārata Vana Parva)
- **Nala and Nīla architect Rāma's bridge to Laṅkā** — The engineer-vānaras Nala and Nīla architect the floating bridge (Setu) by which Rāma's monkey host crosses to storm Laṅkā _(actors: Nala, Nīla, Rāma, the vānaras)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/sacred-flora-and-fauna.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa)
- **Rama returns to Ayodhya (Diwali)** — Rama, Sita, and Lakshmana return to Ayodhya after fourteen years' exile; the city lights lamps to guide them, the charter of Diwali night _(actors: Rama, Sita, Lakshmana)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/festivals-and-vratas.md) — (Ramayana, Uttara Kanda)
- **Rama's departure (Mahaprasthana)** — Rama enters the Sarayu river in jala-samadhi, resumes his form as Vishnu, and ascends to Vaikuntha; Hanuman is granted deathlessness _(actors: Rama, Lakshmana, Brahma, Hanuman, Kala)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/7-uttara-kanda.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana (Uttara Kanda))
- **Ahalya's Redemption by Rama's Touch** — In Kamban's devotional reframing, Rama's touch delivers Ahalya as an act of divine grace _(actors: Rama, Ahalya)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/recensions/kamban-iramavataram.md) — (Kamban, Iramavataram, Bala Kandam)
- **Jain variant: Lakṣmaṇa slays Rāvaṇa, Rāma attains mokṣa** — In Vimalasūri's Jain Paümacariya, the non-violent Rāma (Padma) does not kill Rāvaṇa — Lakṣmaṇa does — and Rāma attains mokṣa as a Jain layman _(actors: Rāma, Lakṣmaṇa, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/smriti-itihasa-epics.md) — (Paümacariya (Vimalasūri))
- **Viśvāmitra attains Brahmaṛṣi rank** — Born a kṣatriya king, Viśvāmitra forces his way to Brahmaṛṣi rank by colossal tapas, the only Saptarṣi who was not a born brahmin _(actors: Viśvāmitra)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rishi-lineages-and-gotras.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa 51–65; Mahābhārata)
- **Hanumān carries the Sañjīvanī mountain to revive Lakṣmaṇa** — Unable to identify the resurrection-herb Sañjīvanī on Droṇagiri, Hanumān carries the whole mountain to revive the wounded Lakṣmaṇa _(actors: Hanumān, Lakṣmaṇa)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divine-objects-and-treasures.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha Kāṇḍa)
- **Kapila incinerates the sons of Sagara** — The wrathful sage Kapila's glance incinerates the 60,000 sons of Sagara in the Ganga-descent story, referenced from the Sankhya profile _(actors: Kapila, sons of Sagara)_ → [details](../90-literature-corpus/darshanas/samkhya-karika.md) — (Ramayana; Bhagavata Purana 9.8)
- **Viśvāmitra creates Triśaṅku's heaven** — Viśvāmitra builds a separate heaven for King Triśaṅku in his contest of tapas _(actors: Viśvāmitra, Triśaṅku)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rishi-lineages-and-gotras.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa)
- **Puṣpaka Vimāna flies Rāma home to Ayodhyā** — The self-moving flying palace Puṣpaka — made by Viśvakarmā, passing Brahmā to Kubera to the seizing Rāvaṇa — finally flies Rāma, Sītā and the army home to Ayodhyā, then is returned to Kubera _(actors: Rāma, Sītā, Viśvakarmā, Kubera, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/divine-objects-and-treasures.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa, Yuddha/Uttara Kāṇḍa; Pushpaka Vimana (Wikipedia))
- **Matali drives Indra's chariot for Rāma against Rāvaṇa** — Indra's charioteer Matali drives the celestial ratha lent to Rāma for the duel with Rāvaṇa (and later to Arjuna for his heaven-journey) _(actors: Matali, Indra, Rāma, Rāvaṇa)_ → [details](../09-artifacts-symbols-and-arts/vahanas-and-celestial-vehicles.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa; Mahabharata)
- **Vasiṣṭha-Viśvāmitra cow-war and death of Vasiṣṭha's sons** — The arch-rivalry of Vasiṣṭha and Viśvāmitra over the wish-cow erupts in war, and Vasiṣṭha's hundred sons (including Śakti) are killed by Viśvāmitra's agency _(actors: Vasiṣṭha, Viśvāmitra, Śakti)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rishi-lineages-and-gotras.md) — (Ṛgveda; Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa; Mahābhārata)
- **Gautama curses Ahalyā to stone** — The sage Gautama curses his wife Ahalyā (the Indra-deception myth) to become stone, later restored by Rāma's touch _(actors: Gautama, Ahalyā, Indra, Rāma)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/rishi-lineages-and-gotras.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa)
- **Akal-bodhan — Rama's Autumn Worship of Durga** — With Chandika shielding Ravana, Brahma counsels Rama to propitiate Durga out of season in autumn; Rama performs the untimely worship from Sashthi to Dashami, the mythic charter of Sharadiya Durga Puja _(actors: Rama, Durga/Chandika, Indra, Brahma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/recensions/krittivasi-ramayan.md) — (Krittivasi Ramayan; Devi-Bhagavata; Kalika/Brihaddharma Purana)
- **The 108 Blue Lotuses and Rama's Eye** — Hanuman fetches 108 celestial blue lotuses for the worship; Durga hides one to test Rama, who prepares to pluck out his own lotus-eye to complete the count, whereupon Durga stays his hand and grants victory _(actors: Rama, Hanuman, Durga, Vibhishana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/recensions/krittivasi-ramayan.md) — (Krittivasi Ramayan)
- **Mahiravana Abducts Rama and Lakshmana; Hanuman's Patala Rescue** — The sorcerer-king Mahiravana of Patala abducts the sleeping Rama and Lakshmana; Hanuman descends to the netherworld, learns the demon's life resides in bees, and slays him to rescue them _(actors: Mahiravana, Rama, Lakshmana, Hanuman)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/recensions/krittivasi-ramayan.md) — (Krittivasi Ramayan (folk interpolation))
- **Śeṣa incarnates as Lakṣmaṇa** — When Viṣṇu becomes Rāma, Śeṣa accompanies him as the ever-devoted younger brother Lakṣmaṇa _(actors: Śeṣa, Lakṣmaṇa, Rāma)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/shesha-ananta.md) — (Vaiṣṇava tradition; Rāmāyaṇa commentary)
- **Martyrdom of Taranisen** — Taranisen, the Rama-devotee son of Vibhishana, fights for Ravana's side out of duty while chanting Rama's name and is slain by Rama — a poignant martyrdom motif _(actors: Taranisen, Vibhishana, Rama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/recensions/krittivasi-ramayan.md) — (Krittivasi Ramayan (folk interpolation))
- **Birth of Bhagiratha from Two Co-widows** — In a Krittivasi accretion, Bhagiratha (later bringer of the Ganga to earth) is born of King Dilipa's two co-widows by divine blessing — a rare premodern parthenogenic/same-sex generation motif _(actors: Bhagiratha, two co-widows of Dilipa)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/recensions/krittivasi-ramayan.md) — (Krittivasi Ramayan (late interpolation))
- **Sita Slays Sahasra-Ravana as Mahakali (Adbhuta Ramayana)** — When the thousand-headed Sahasra-Ravana of Pushkara fells Rama on the battlefield, Sita assumes the form of Mahakali and slays him, inverting hero and heroine roles _(actors: Sita/Mahakali, Sahasra-Ravana, Rama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/adhyatma-and-variant-ramayanas.md) — (Adbhuta Ramayana)
- **Vamana pushes Bali to the netherworld (Bali Pratipada/Onam)** — Vamana, after the three-strides boon, pushes the asura-king Bali/Mahabali to Patala, who is granted one yearly return celebrated as Onam and Bali Pratipada _(actors: Vamana, Bali (Mahabali))_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/festivals-and-vratas.md) — (Bhagavata Purana; Kerala tradition)
- **Sita Born to Mandodari (Adbhuta Ramayana)** — In the Shakta Adbhuta Ramayana, Sita — the supreme Shakti — is born to Ravana's queen Mandodari rather than discovered by Janaka _(actors: Sita, Mandodari, Ravana)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/adhyatma-and-variant-ramayanas.md) — (Adbhuta Ramayana)
- **Ravana Abducts Kausalya (Ananda Ramayana)** — In the Ananda Ramayana, foretold that Kausalya's son by Dasharatha will kill him, Ravana abducts Kausalya but fails to keep her — a peripheral back-story _(actors: Ravana, Kausalya, Dasharatha)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/adhyatma-and-variant-ramayanas.md) — (Ananda Ramayana)
- **Vasishtha Instructs Young Rama (Yoga-Vasishtha)** — A young, world-weary Rama is instructed by the sage Vasishtha in radical Advaita (ajatavada, non-origination) across hundreds of nested parables in the Yoga-Vasishtha _(actors: Vasishtha, Rama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/adhyatma-and-variant-ramayanas.md) — (Yoga-Vasishtha / Vasishtha Ramayana)
- **Vamana's three strides subdue Bali** — Vishnu as the dwarf Vamana begs three paces of land from the asura king Bali, then swells to cosmic size (Trivikrama), covering earth and heaven in two strides and pushing Bali to the netherworld with the third _(actors: Vamana/Trivikrama (Vishnu), Bali)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/dashavatara.md) — (Rig Veda 1.154; Bhagavata Purana 8.15-23; Vishnu Purana)
- **Parashurama exterminates the kshatriyas** — After King Kartavirya Arjuna steals his father Jamadagni's wish-cow and kills the sage, the axe-bearing Parashurama annihilates the tyrant and the entire kshatriya class twenty-one times over _(actors: Parashurama, Kartavirya Arjuna, Jamadagni)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/dashavatara.md) — (Mahabharata Vana Parva; Bhagavata Purana 9.15-16; Vishnu Purana)
- **Rama slays Ravana and recovers Sita (Ramayana)** — Rama, exiled prince of Ayodhya, allies with Hanuman and the vanaras, builds a bridge to Lanka, slays the rakshasa king Ravana, and recovers his abducted wife Sita _(actors: Rama, Sita, Ravana, Hanuman)_ → [details](../02-deities-and-cosmic-hierarchy/dashavatara.md) — (Valmiki Ramayana; Bhagavata Purana 9.10-11)
- **Lakshmana Kills Ravana (Jain Ramayanas)** — In the Jain tradition the Vasudeva Lakshmana, not the non-violent Baladeva Rama, slays the Prativasudeva Ravana — and consequently falls to hell, while Rama attains moksha _(actors: Lakshmana, Ravana, Rama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/recensions/jain-and-buddhist-ramayanas.md) — (Paumacariya; Padma-purana)
- **Vali Renounces and Attains Nirvana (Paumacariya)** — In Vimalasuri's Paumacariya, after the conflict with Sugriva, Vali renounces to become a muni and attains nirvana instead of being shot dead by Rama _(actors: Vali, Sugriva, Rama)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/recensions/jain-and-buddhist-ramayanas.md) — (Paumacariya)
- **Sita Born of Ravana and Mandodari, Then Abandoned (Gunabhadra/Sanghadasa)** — In the Gunabhadra and Sanghadasa Jain strands, Sita is born to Ravana and Mandodari and exposed because of a prophecy of family destruction, making Ravana's later abduction unwitting near-incest _(actors: Sita, Ravana, Mandodari)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/recensions/jain-and-buddhist-ramayanas.md) — (Uttara-purana (Gunabhadra); Sanghadasa)
- **Rama Attains Kevala-jnana and Moksha (Jain ending)** — The Jain epic closes with Rama attaining omniscience (kevala-jnana) and liberation at Tungi Giri, Lakshmana and Ravana going to hell, and Sita becoming a nun and ascending to heaven _(actors: Rama, Lakshmana, Ravana, Sita)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/ramayana/recensions/jain-and-buddhist-ramayanas.md) — (Paumacariya; Jain tradition)
- **Rāvaṇa drives Kubera from Laṅkā** — Rāvaṇa drives his half-brother Kubera from Laṅkā and seizes his flying chariot, after which Kubera removes to Alakā in the Himālayas _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Kubera)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/yakshas-and-guhyakas.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Uttara Kāṇḍa)
- **Rāvaṇa steals the Puṣpaka Vimāna** — Kubera's self-moving aerial chariot, a gift of Brahmā, is stolen by Rāvaṇa and later used by Rāma to return to Ayodhyā _(actors: Rāvaṇa, Kubera, Rāma)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/yakshas-and-guhyakas.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa)
- **Nalakūbara curses Rāvaṇa** — Kubera's son Nalakūbara, consort of Rambhā, curses Rāvaṇa that his head will burst if he forces a woman, a curse that later protects Sītā _(actors: Nalakūbara, Rāvaṇa, Sītā)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/yakshas-and-guhyakas.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Uttara Kāṇḍa)
- **Suketu's daughter Tāḍakā born by boon and cursed by Agastya** — The yakṣa king Suketu obtains a daughter Tāḍakā by boon, who, cursed by Agastya, becomes a man-eating ogress later slain by Rāma _(actors: Suketu, Tāḍakā, Agastya, Rāma)_ → [details](../08-beings-and-bestiary/yakshas-and-guhyakas.md) — (Rāmāyaṇa Bāla Kāṇḍa)
- **Birth of Rama (Rama Navami)** — Rama, the 7th dashavatara, is born to Dasharatha of Ayodhya, opening the Bala Kanda; commemorated on Chaitra Shukla Navami _(actors: Rama, Dasharatha)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/festivals-and-vratas.md) — (Ramayana, Bala Kanda)
- **Birth of Hanuman (Hanuman Jayanti)** — Hanuman, the vanara devotee of Rama and a partial incarnation of Shiva/Vayu, is born, commemorated on Hanuman Jayanti _(actors: Hanuman)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/festivals-and-vratas.md) — (Ramayana tradition)
- **Lakshmi ties a thread on Bali (Raksha Bandhan)** — Lakshmi, disguised as a poor woman, ties a protective thread on Bali to win back her husband Vishnu, a Raksha Bandhan charter linked to the Vamana cycle _(actors: Lakshmi, Bali, Vishnu)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/festivals-and-vratas.md) — (Puranic)
- **Birth of Parashurama / descent of the Ganga (Akshaya Tritiya)** — Akshaya Tritiya stacks charters including the birth of Parashurama, the descent of the Ganga, the start of Treta Yuga, and Vyasa beginning the Mahabharata _(actors: Parashurama, Ganga, Vyasa)_ → [details](../05-concepts-and-dharma/festivals-and-vratas.md) — (Puranic)
- **Yudhājit's embassy to Ayodhyā** — Kaikeyī's brother Yudhājit, king of the Kekayas, sends the brahmin Gārgya to urge Rāma to conquer the rich, unclaimed country of the Gandharvas on both banks of the Sindhu _(actors: Yudhājit, Gārgya, Rāma)_ → [details](../04-deep-dives/bharata-gandharva-conquest-takshashila.md) — (Vālmīki Rāmāyaṇa, Uttara-kāṇḍa 100)
